===== C:\anime-site\proxy.ts =====
import { createServerClient } from '@supabase/ssr'
import { NextResponse, type NextRequest } from 'next/server'

export async function proxy(request: NextRequest) {
  let supabaseResponse = NextResponse.next({
    request,
  })

  const supabase = createServerClient(
    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL!,
    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY!,
    {
      cookies: {
        getAll() {
          return request.cookies.getAll()
        },
        setAll(cookiesToSet) {
          cookiesToSet.forEach(({ name, value, options }) => request.cookies.set(name, value))
          supabaseResponse = NextResponse.next({
            request,
          })
          cookiesToSet.forEach(({ name, value, options }) =>
            supabaseResponse.cookies.set(name, value, options)
          )
        },
      },
    }
  )

  // Pega o usuário logado
  const {
    data: { user },
  } = await supabase.auth.getUser()

  // PROTEÇÃO DE ROTAS:
  // Se o usuário NÃO estiver logado e tentar acessar /perfil ou /top10, manda para /login
  if (
    !user &&
    !request.nextUrl.pathname.startsWith('/login') &&
    !request.nextUrl.pathname.startsWith('/auth')
  ) {
    // Se a rota for protegida (você pode adicionar mais rotas aqui depois)
    if (request.nextUrl.pathname.startsWith('/perfil') || request.nextUrl.pathname.startsWith('/top10')) {
        const url = request.nextUrl.clone()
        url.pathname = '/login'
        return NextResponse.redirect(url)
    }
  }

  return supabaseResponse
}

export const config = {
  matcher: [
    '/((?!_next/static|_next/image|favicon.ico|.*\\.(?:svg|png|jpg|jpeg|gif|webp)$).*)',
  ],
}

===== C:\anime-site\README.md =====
This is a [Next.js](https://nextjs.org) project bootstrapped with [`create-next-app`](https://nextjs.org/docs/app/api-reference/cli/create-next-app).

## Getting Started

First, run the development server:

```bash
npm run dev
# or
yarn dev
# or
pnpm dev
# or
bun dev
```

Open [http://localhost:3000](http://localhost:3000) with your browser to see the result.

You can start editing the page by modifying `app/page.tsx`. The page auto-updates as you edit the file.

This project uses [`next/font`](https://nextjs.org/docs/app/building-your-application/optimizing/fonts) to automatically optimize and load [Geist](https://vercel.com/font), a new font family for Vercel.

## Learn More

To learn more about Next.js, take a look at the following resources:

- [Next.js Documentation](https://nextjs.org/docs) - learn about Next.js features and API.
- [Learn Next.js](https://nextjs.org/learn) - an interactive Next.js tutorial.

You can check out [the Next.js GitHub repository](https://github.com/vercel/next.js) - your feedback and contributions are welcome!

## Deploy on Vercel

The easiest way to deploy your Next.js app is to use the [Vercel Platform](https://vercel.com/new?utm_medium=default-template&filter=next.js&utm_source=create-next-app&utm_campaign=create-next-app-readme) from the creators of Next.js.

Check out our [Next.js deployment documentation](https://nextjs.org/docs/app/building-your-application/deploying) for more details.

===== C:\anime-site\app\globals.css =====
@import "tailwindcss";

/* Ativa a classe 'dark' para o Tailwind v4 */
@custom-variant dark (&:where(.dark, .dark *));

@theme {
  /* CORES DO MODO CLARO (Ajustadas para Bege/Creme) */
  --color-bg: #f6f5f2;         /* Fundo geral: Bege bem claro (tipo papel) */
  --color-surface: #ffffff;    /* Cards: Branco puro para dar contraste com o fundo */
  --color-border: #e5e2db;     /* Bordas num tom areia para combinar */
  --color-text: #2d2a26;       /* Texto: Marrom quase preto (mais suave que o preto puro) */
  --color-muted: #78716c;      /* Texto secundário: Cinza quente */
  
  --color-accent: #a855f7;
}

/* CORES DO MODO ESCURO (Quando a classe .dark estiver ativa no html) */
.dark {
  --color-bg: #0a0a0f;
  --color-surface: #14141c;
  --color-border: #26262f;
  --color-text: #e5e5ea;
  --color-muted: #8a8a99;
}

body {
  background-color: var(--color-bg);
  color: var(--color-text);
  font-family: system-ui, sans-serif;
  min-height: 100vh;
  margin: 0;
  transition: background-color 0.3s ease, color 0.3s ease; /* Transição suave ao mudar de tema */
}

html, body {
  background-color: var(--color-bg);
  overscroll-behavior-y: none;
}

.pb-safe {
  padding-bottom: env(safe-area-inset-bottom);
}

.scrollbar-hide::-webkit-scrollbar {
  display: none;
}
.scrollbar-hide {
  -ms-overflow-style: none;
  scrollbar-width: none;
}
/* Esconde o botão de revelar senha nativo do navegador (Edge/Chrome) */
input[type="password"]::-ms-reveal,
input[type="password"]::-ms-clear {
  display: none;
}

    description: "Acompanhe os lançamentos, monte seu Top 10 e converse com outros fãs de anime.",
  },
  other: {
    'mylead-verification': '32db5d4576599a2b45af8d6facef1d9e',
  },
};

export default function RootLayout({
  children,
  modal,
}: Readonly<{
  children: React.ReactNode;
  modal: React.ReactNode;
}>) {
  return (
    <html lang="pt-BR">
      <body className={`${geistSans.variable} ${geistMono.variable} antialiased min-h-screen flex flex-col bg-bg`}>
        <Navbar />
        <main className="flex-1 w-full max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 md:px-6 pt-6 pb-24 md:pb-12">
          {children}
        </main>
        <Footer />
        {modal}
      </body>
    </html>
  );
}

===== C:\anime-site\app\page.tsx =====
'use client';

import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { SeletorDias } from '@/components/lancamentos/SeletorDias';
import { GridAnimes } from '@/components/lancamentos/GridAnimes';
import { AnimeData } from '@/components/lancamentos/CardAnime';
import { AlertCircle } from 'lucide-react';
import { EspacoAnuncio } from '@/components/layout/EspacoAnuncio';

// Função para descobrir a temporada atual
function getTemporadaAtual() {
  const data = new Date();
  const mes = data.getMonth(); 
  const ano = data.getFullYear();

  let temporada = '';
  if (mes >= 0 && mes <= 2) temporada = 'Inverno';
  else if (mes >= 3 && mes <= 5) temporada = 'Primavera';
  else if (mes >= 6 && mes <= 8) temporada = 'Verão';
  else temporada = 'Outono';

  return `${temporada} ${ano}`;
}

export default function Home() {
  const [diaSelecionado, setDiaSelecionado] = useState<string>('');
  const [animes, setAnimes] = useState<AnimeData[]>([]);
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState<boolean>(true);
  const [temporada, setTemporada] = useState<string>('');
  const [erroApi, setErroApi] = useState<string | null>(null);

  // Define o dia de hoje e a temporada ao abrir
  useEffect(() => {
    setTemporada(getTemporadaAtual());

    const dataHoje = new Date();
    const diaSemanaString = dataHoje.toLocaleDateString('pt-BR', { timeZone: 'America/Sao_Paulo', weekday: 'short' });
    
    const mapaDias: Record<string, string> = { 
      'dom.': 'dom', 'seg.': 'seg', 'ter.': 'ter', 
      'qua.': 'qua', 'qui.': 'qui', 'sex.': 'sex', 'sáb.': 'sab' 
    };
    
    setDiaSelecionado(mapaDias[diaSemanaString] || 'qua');
  }, []);

  // Busca os animes
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!diaSelecionado) return;

    async function fetchAnimes() {
      setIsLoading(true);
      setErroApi(null); 
      
      try {
        const res = await fetch(`/api/test-anilist?dia=${diaSelecionado}`);
        
        if (!res.ok) {
          throw new Error("Falha na resposta do servidor");
        }

        const data = await res.json();
        
        if (data.error) {
          setErroApi("Não conseguimos carregar os lançamentos no AniVerse agora. Nossos servidores podem estar em manutenção.");
          setAnimes([]);
        } else {
          setAnimes(data.animesDeHoje || []);
        }
      } catch (error) {
        console.error("Erro ao buscar animes:", error);
        setErroApi("Falha de conexão com os servidores do AniVerse. Tente novamente em instantes.");
      } finally {
        setIsLoading(false);
      }
    }
    fetchAnimes();
  }, [diaSelecionado]);

  return (
    <div className="w-full">
      <header className="mb-8 flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-end justify-between gap-2">
        <div>
          {/* Título Principal (H1) */}
          <h1 className="text-2xl md:text-3xl font-bold text-text flex items-center gap-3">
            Lançamentos da Semana
            {temporada && (
              <span className="text-sm font-semibold bg-accent text-white px-3 py-1 rounded-full border border-accent">
                {temporada}
              </span>
            )}
          </h1>
          {/* Texto de Apoio (Contraste Melhorado) */}
          <p className="text-text/80 text-sm mt-2">
            Acompanhe os episódios que saem hoje e nos próximos dias.
          </p>
        </div>
      </header>

      <SeletorDias 
        diaSelecionado={diaSelecionado} 
        onChangeDia={setDiaSelecionado} 
      />

      <div className="mt-8">
        {/* Título da Seção (H2) para organizar a hierarquia */}
        <h2 className="sr-only">Animes de Hoje</h2> 

        {erroApi ? (
          <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center py-20 text-center bg-surface rounded-xl border border-red-500/20">
            <AlertCircle size={48} className="text-red-400 mb-4" />
            <h3 className="text-lg font-bold text-text mb-2">Ops, tivemos um problema!</h3>
            <p className="text-text/80 text-sm max-w-md">{erroApi}</p>
            <button 
              onClick={() => setDiaSelecionado(diaSelecionado)} 
              className="mt-6 px-4 py-2 bg-accent text-white font-medium rounded-lg hover:bg-accent/80 transition-colors"
            >
              Tentar novamente
            </button>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <GridAnimes animes={animes} isLoading={isLoading} />
        )}
      </div>

      {/* ESPAÇO PARA ANÚNCIO NO FINAL */}
      <div className="mt-10">
        <EspacoAnuncio formato="banner" />
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

===== C:\anime-site\app\robots.ts =====
import { MetadataRoute } from 'next';

export default function robots(): MetadataRoute.Robots {
  const baseUrl = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SITE_URL || 'https://anime-site-2dhx.vercel.app';

  return {
    rules: {
      userAgent: '*',
      allow: '/',
      disallow: [
        '/admin/',   // Bloqueia o painel de admin
        '/api/',     // Bloqueia as rotas de API
        '/perfil/',  // Bloqueia a página de perfil privada
      ],
    },
    sitemap: `${baseUrl}/sitemap.xml`,
  };
}

===== C:\anime-site\app\sitemap.ts =====
import type { MetadataRoute } from 'next';

export default function sitemap(): MetadataRoute.Sitemap {
  // Substitua pelo seu domínio quando comprar (ex: 'https://aniverse.com.br')
  // Enquanto isso, usamos a URL da Vercel como base.
  const baseUrl = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SITE_URL || 'https://anime-site-2dhx.vercel.app';

  // Lista de páginas estáticas principais
  const rotas = [
    '',
    '/destaques',
    '/top10',
    '/chat',
    '/login',
    '/privacidade',
    '/termos',
  ];

  return rotas.map((rota) => ({
    url: `${baseUrl}${rota}`,
    lastModified: new Date(),
    changeFrequency: 'daily' as const,
    priority: rota === '' ? 1.0 : 0.8,
  }));
}

===== C:\anime-site\app\@modal\(.)login\page.tsx =====
'use client';

import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { createClient } from '@/lib/supabase/client';
import { Mail, Lock, Loader2, Eye, EyeOff, User, X, CheckCircle2, AlertCircle } from 'lucide-react';
import { SeletorAvatares } from '@/components/perfil/SelecionarAvatar';
import Link from 'next/link';

export default function LoginModal() {
  const [isLogin, setIsLogin] = useState(true);
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
  const [username, setUsername] = useState('');
  const [avatarUrl, setAvatarUrl] = useState('');
  
  const [showPassword, setShowPassword] = useState(false);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [socialLoading, setSocialLoading] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [usernameStatus, setUsernameStatus] = useState<'idle' | 'checking' | 'available' | 'taken'>('idle');
  
  const router = useRouter();
  const supabase = createClient();

  const dominiosPermitidos = ['gmail.com', 'hotmail.com', 'outlook.com', 'yahoo.com', 'icloud.com', 'bol.com.br', 'uol.com.br'];

  useEffect(() => {
    if (isLogin) {
      setUsernameStatus('idle');
      return;
    }

    if (username.trim().length < 3) {
      setUsernameStatus('idle');
      return;
    }

    setUsernameStatus('checking');

    const timer = setTimeout(async () => {
      const { data, error } = await supabase
        .from('profiles')
        .select('username')
        .ilike('username', username.trim())
        .maybeSingle();

      if (error) {
        setUsernameStatus('idle');
        return;
      }

      if (data) {
        setUsernameStatus('taken');
      } else {
        setUsernameStatus('available');
      }
    }, 500);

    return () => clearTimeout(timer);
  }, [username, isLogin, supabase]);

  const handleAuth = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setLoading(true);
    setError(null);

    const emailRegex = /^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/;
    if (!emailRegex.test(email)) {
      setError('Por favor, insira um e-mail válido.');
      setLoading(false);
      return;
    }

    const dominio = email.split('@')[1].toLowerCase();
    if (!dominiosPermitidos.includes(dominio)) {
      setError('Use um e-mail de um provedor conhecido (Gmail, Hotmail, etc).');
      setLoading(false);
      return;
    }

    if (!isLogin) {
      if (password.length < 6) {
        setError('A senha deve ter no mínimo 6 caracteres.');
        setLoading(false);
        return;
      }
      const senhasFaceis = ['123456', 'abcdef', 'qwerty', '12345678', 'senha123'];
      if (senhasFaceis.includes(password.toLowerCase())) {
        setError('Sua senha é muito fácil. Escolha outra.');
        setLoading(false);
        return;
      }
      if (!/(?=.*[a-zA-Z])(?=.*\d)/.test(password)) {
        setError('A senha deve conter pelo menos uma letra e um número.');
        setLoading(false);
        return;
      }
      if (username.trim().length < 3) {
        setError('O nome de usuário deve ter pelo menos 3 letras.');
        setLoading(false);
        return;
      }
      if (usernameStatus === 'taken') {
        setError('Este nome de usuário já está em uso. Escolha outro.');
        setLoading(false);
        return;
      }
      if (!avatarUrl) {
        setError('Escolha um avatar para continuar.');
        setLoading(false);
        return;
      }
    }

    try {
      if (isLogin) {
        const { error } = await supabase.auth.signInWithPassword({ email, password });
        if (error) throw error;
      } else {
        const { data: authData, error: authError } = await supabase.auth.signUp({ 
          email, 
          password, 
          options: { data: { username: username.trim().toLowerCase(), avatar_url: avatarUrl } } 
        });
        if (authError) throw authError;

        if (authData.user) {
          await supabase.from('profiles').update({ avatar_url: avatarUrl }).eq('id', authData.user.id);
        }
      }
      
      router.back();
      router.refresh();
 
    } catch (err: any) {
      if (err.message.includes('Invalid login credentials')) setError('E-mail ou senha incorretos.');
      else if (err.message.includes('User already registered')) setError('Este e-mail já está cadastrado.');
      else setError(err.message || 'Ocorreu um erro.');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const handleSocialLogin = async (provider: 'google') => {
    setSocialLoading(provider);
    setError(null);

    const { error } = await supabase.auth.signInWithOAuth({
      provider: provider,
      options: {
        redirectTo: `${window.location.origin}/auth/callback`,
      },
    });

    if (error) {
      setError(`Erro ao entrar com ${provider}. Tente novamente.`);
      setSocialLoading(null);
    }
  };

  const isSubmitDisabled = loading || (!isLogin && (username.trim().length < 3 || usernameStatus === 'taken' || usernameStatus === 'checking' || !avatarUrl));

  return (
    // MUDANÇA: Fundo apenas com o gradiente sutil, sem o grid xadrez
    <div key={isLogin ? 'login' : 'cadastro'} className="fixed inset-0 z-[100] flex items-center justify-center bg-bg/80 backdrop-blur-md p-4">
      
      {/* FUNDO ESTILIZADO (Apenas Gradiente, sem grid) */}
      <div className="absolute inset-0 z-0 opacity-20 pointer-events-none">
        <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-br from-accent/20 via-transparent to-accent/10" />
      </div>

      <div className="w-full max-w-md bg-surface/80 backdrop-blur-xl border border-border rounded-2xl p-8 shadow-2xl relative z-10 max-h-[90vh] overflow-y-auto">
        
        <button 
          onClick={() => router.back()} 
          className="absolute top-4 right-4 text-muted hover:text-red-400 transition-colors cursor-pointer z-10"
        >
          <X size={20} />
        </button>

        <div className="text-center mb-8 mt-2">
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-text mb-2">
            {isLogin ? 'Bem-vindo de volta' : 'Crie sua conta'}
          </h1>
          <p className="text-muted text-sm">
            {isLogin ? 'Entre para salvar seus animes e notas.' : 'Junte-se ao AniVerse e monte seu Top 10.'}
          </p>
        </div>

        {error && (
          <div className="bg-red-500/10 border border-red-500/50 text-red-400 text-sm p-3 rounded-lg mb-6">
            {error}
          </div>
        )}

        <form onSubmit={handleAuth} className="flex flex-col gap-4">
          {!isLogin && (
            <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
              <div className="relative">
                <User className={`absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 ${
                  usernameStatus === 'taken' ? 'text-red-500' : 
                  usernameStatus === 'available' ? 'text-green-500' : 'text-muted'
                }`} size={18} />
                <input
                  type="text" 
                  placeholder="Seu nome de usuário (único)" 
                  value={username} 
                  onChange={(e) => setUsername(e.target.value)} 
                  required={!isLogin}
                  className={`w-full bg-transparent border rounded-lg py-3 pl-10 pr-10 focus:outline-none transition-all ${
                    usernameStatus === 'taken' ? 'border-red-500 focus:ring-red-500' : 
                    usernameStatus === 'available' ? 'border-green-500 focus:ring-green-500' : 
                    'border-border focus:border-accent focus:ring-accent'
                  }`}
                />
                <div className="absolute right-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2">
                  {usernameStatus === 'checking' && <Loader2 className="animate-spin text-muted" size={18} />}
                  {usernameStatus === 'available' && <CheckCircle2 className="text-green-500" size={18} />}
                  {usernameStatus === 'taken' && <AlertCircle className="text-red-500" size={18} />}
                </div>
              </div>
              
              {usernameStatus === 'taken' && (
                <span className="text-xs text-red-500 font-medium ml-1">Este nome já está em uso. Escolha outro.</span>
              )}
              {usernameStatus === 'available' && (
                <span className="text-xs text-green-500 font-medium ml-1">Nome de usuário disponível!</span>
              )}
            </div>
          )}

          <div className="relative">
            <Mail className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-muted" size={18} />
            <input
              type="email" placeholder="Seu e-mail" value={email} onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)} required
              className="w-full bg-transparent border border-border text-text rounded-lg py-3 pl-10 pr-4 focus:outline-none focus:border-accent focus:ring-1 focus:ring-accent transition-all"
            />
          </div>

          <div className="relative">
            <Lock className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-muted" size={18} />
            <input
              type={showPassword ? 'text' : 'password'} placeholder="Sua senha" value={password} onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)} required
              className="w-full bg-transparent border border-border text-text rounded-lg py-3 pl-10 pr-12 focus:outline-none focus:border-accent focus:ring-1 focus:ring-accent transition-all"
            />
            <button type="button" onClick={() => setShowPassword(!showPassword)} className="absolute right-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-muted hover:text-accent transition-colors cursor-pointer">
              {showPassword ? <EyeOff size={18} /> : <Eye size={18} />}
            </button>
          </div>

          {isLogin && (
            <div className="flex justify-end mt-[-8px]">
              <Link 
                href="/esqueceu-senha" 
                onClick={(e) => {
                  e.preventDefault();
                  router.back();
                  setTimeout(() => router.push('/esqueceu-senha'), 100);
                }}
                className="text-sm text-accent hover:underline"
              >
                Esqueceu a senha?
              </Link>
            </div>
          )}

          {!isLogin && (
            <div className="mt-2">
              <label className="text-sm font-semibold text-text mb-2 block">Escolha seu avatar</label>
              <SeletorAvatares selectedAvatar={avatarUrl} onSelect={setAvatarUrl} />
            </div>
          )}

          <button 
            type="submit" 
            disabled={isSubmitDisabled} 
            className="w-full bg-accent text-white font-bold py-3 rounded-lg hover:bg-accent/90 transition-all mt-2 flex items-center justify-center gap-2 disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed cursor-pointer"
          >
            {loading ? <Loader2 className="animate-spin" size={20} /> : (isLogin ? 'Entrar' : 'Criar Conta')}
          </button>
        </form>

        <div className="relative my-6">
          <div className="absolute inset-0 flex items-center"><div className="w-full border-t border-border"></div></div>
          <div className="relative flex justify-center text-xs uppercase"><span className="bg-surface px-2 text-muted">Ou continue com</span></div>
        </div>

        <div className="flex">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => handleSocialLogin('google')}
            disabled={!!socialLoading}
            className="flex-1 flex items-center justify-center gap-3 bg-transparent border border-border text-text py-3 rounded-lg hover:border-accent transition-colors cursor-pointer font-medium text-sm disabled:opacity-50"
          >
            {socialLoading === 'google' ? <Loader2 className="animate-spin" size={18} /> : (
              <svg className="w-5 h-5" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                <path fill="#4285F4" d="M22.56 12.25c0-.78-.07-1.53-.2-2.25H12v4.26h5.92c-.26 1.37-1.04 2.53-2.21 3.31v2.77h3.57c2.08-1.92 3.28-4.74 3.28-8.09z"/>
                <path fill="#34A853" d="M12 23c2.97 0 5.46-.98 7.28-2.66l-3.57-2.77c-.98.66-2.23 1.06-3.71 1.06-2.86 0-5.29-1.93-6.16-4.53H2.18v2.84C3.99 20.53 7.7 23 12 23z"/>
                <path fill="#FBBC05" d="M5.84 14.09c-.22-.66-.35-1.36-.35-2.09s.13-1.43.35-2.09V7.07H2.18C1.43 8.55 1 10.22 1 12s.43 3.45 1.18 4.93l2.85-2.22.81-.62z"/>
                <path fill="#EA4335" d="M12 5.38c1.62 0 3.06.56 4.21 1.64l3.15-3.15C17.45 2.09 14.97 1 12 1 7.7 1 3.99 3.47 2.18 7.07l3.66 2.84c.87-2.6 3.3-4.53 6.16-4.53z"/>
              </svg>
            )}
            Entrar com Google
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="mt-6 text-center text-sm text-muted">
          {isLogin ? 'Ainda não tem conta?' : 'Já tem uma conta?'}
          <button onClick={() => { setIsLogin(!isLogin); setError(null); setUsernameStatus('idle'); }} className="ml-2 text-accent font-bold hover:underline cursor-pointer">
            {isLogin ? 'Cadastre-se' : 'Faça Login'}
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

===== C:\anime-site\app\actions\feed.ts =====
'use server'

import { createClient } from '@/lib/supabase/server'
import { revalidatePath } from 'next/cache'

export async function toggleLike(postId: string, isLiked: boolean) {
  const supabase = await createClient()
  const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser()

  if (!user) throw new Error('Não autorizado')

  if (isLiked) {
    await supabase.from('post_likes').delete().match({ post_id: postId, user_id: user.id })
  } else {
    await supabase.from('post_likes').insert({ post_id: postId, user_id: user.id })
  }

  revalidatePath('/feed')
}

===== C:\anime-site\app\actions\feedbacks.ts =====
'use server';

import { createClient } from '@/lib/supabase/server';
import { revalidatePath } from 'next/cache';

// Deletar feedback
export async function deletarFeedback(id: string) {
  const supabase = await createClient();
  
  // Verifica se é admin (segurança extra)
  const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
  if (!user) throw new Error('Não autorizado');
  
  const { data: profile } = await supabase.from('profiles').select('is_admin').eq('id', user.id).single();
  if (!profile?.is_admin) throw new Error('Acesso negado');

  const { error } = await supabase.from('feedbacks').delete().eq('id', id);
  if (error) throw new Error(error.message);

  revalidatePath('/admin');
}

// Marcar como resolvido (alternar status)
export async function toggleResolvido(id: string, isRead: boolean) {
  const supabase = await createClient();
  
  const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
  if (!user) throw new Error('Não autorizado');
  
  const { data: profile } = await supabase.from('profiles').select('is_admin').eq('id', user.id).single();
  if (!profile?.is_admin) throw new Error('Acesso negado');

  const { error } = await supabase.from('feedbacks').update({ is_read: !isRead }).eq('id', id);
  if (error) throw new Error(error.message);

  revalidatePath('/admin');
}

===== C:\anime-site\app\actions\ModalComentarios.tsx =====
'use client';

import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { createClient } from '@/lib/supabase/client';
import { X, Send, Loader2 } from 'lucide-react';
import Image from 'next/image';

interface Post { id: string; }

export function ModalComentarios({ post, onClose }: { post: Post, onClose: () => void }) {
  const supabase = createClient();
  const [comentarios, setComentarios] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [novoComentario, setNovoComentario] = useState('');
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [carregandoInicial, setCarregandoInicial] = useState(true);

  useEffect(() => {
    const fetchComentarios = async () => {
      const { data } = await supabase
        .from('post_comments')
        .select(`id, content, created_at, profiles (username, avatar_url)`)
        .eq('post_id', post.id)
        .order('created_at', { ascending: true });
      
      if (data) setComentarios(data);
      setCarregandoInicial(false);
    };
    fetchComentarios();
  }, [post.id]);

  const handleEnviarComentario = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!novoComentario.trim()) return;
    setLoading(true);

    const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
    if (!user) { alert('Faça login para comentar.'); return; }

    const { data, error } = await supabase.from('post_comments').insert({
      post_id: post.id,
      user_id: user.id,
      content: novoComentario
    }).select(`id, content, created_at, profiles (username, avatar_url)`).single();

    if (!error && data) {
      setComentarios([...comentarios, data]);
      setNovoComentario('');
    } else {
      alert('Erro ao comentar.');
    }
    setLoading(false);
  };

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-[200] bg-black/80 flex items-center justify-center p-4 backdrop-blur-sm" onClick={onClose}>
      <div className="w-full max-w-lg bg-surface rounded-xl flex flex-col max-h-[85vh]" onClick={e => e.stopPropagation()}>
        
        {/* Header */}
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between p-4 border-b border-border">
          <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-text">Comentários</h2>
          <button onClick={onClose} className="text-muted hover:text-text"><X size={24} /></button>
        </div>

        {/* Lista de Comentários */}
        <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-4 space-y-4">
          {carregandoInicial ? (
            <div className="flex justify-center"><Loader2 className="animate-spin text-accent" /></div>
          ) : comentarios.length === 0 ? (
            <p className="text-center text-muted text-sm py-8">Nenhum comentário ainda. Seja o primeiro!</p>
          ) : (
            comentarios.map((c) => (
              <div key={c.id} className="flex gap-3">
                <div className="relative w-8 h-8 rounded-full overflow-hidden bg-border shrink-0">
                  {c.profiles?.avatar_url ? (
                    <Image src={c.profiles.avatar_url} alt="Avatar" fill className="object-cover" />
                  ) : (
                    <div className="w-full h-full flex items-center justify-center text-xs font-bold">{c.profiles?.username?.charAt(0).toUpperCase()}</div>
                  )}
                </div>
                <div>
                  <p className="text-sm font-bold text-text">{c.profiles?.username}</p>
                  <p className="text-sm text-muted">{c.content}</p>
                </div>
              </div>
            ))
          )}
        </div>

        {/* Input */}
        <form onSubmit={handleEnviarComentario} className="p-3 border-t border-border flex gap-2">
          <input 
            type="text" 
            value={novoComentario}
            onChange={e => setNovoComentario(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="Adicione um comentário..."
            className="flex-1 bg-background border border-border rounded-lg px-4 py-2 text-sm text-text focus:outline-none focus:border-accent"
          />
          <button 
            type="submit" 
            disabled={loading || !novoComentario.trim()}
            className="bg-accent text-white p-2 rounded-lg disabled:opacity-50 hover:bg-accent/90 transition-colors"
          >
            {loading ? <Loader2 className="animate-spin" size={20} /> : <Send size={20} />}
          </button>
        </form>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

===== C:\anime-site\app\actions\posts.ts =====
'use server';

import { createClient } from '@/lib/supabase/server';
import { revalidatePath } from 'next/cache';

export async function deletarPost(postId: string, mediaUrls: string[]) {
  const supabase = await createClient();
  const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser();

  if (!user) throw new Error('Não autorizado');

  // 1. Verifica se o post pertence ao usuário (ou se ele é admin)
  const { data: post } = await supabase
    .from('posts')
    .select('user_id')
    .eq('id', postId)
    .single();

  const { data: profile } = await supabase
    .from('profiles')
    .select('is_admin')
    .eq('id', user.id)
    .single();

  if (post?.user_id !== user.id && !profile?.is_admin) {
    throw new Error('Você não tem permissão para deletar este post.');
  }

  // 2. Deleta as imagens do Storage
  if (mediaUrls && mediaUrls.length > 0) {
    // Pega o nome do arquivo a partir da URL pública
    const fileNames = mediaUrls.map(url => url.split('/').pop()).filter(Boolean) as string[];
    await supabase.storage.from('feed_media').remove(fileNames);
  }

  // 3. Deleta o post do banco (as curtidas e comentários vão junto por causa do ON DELETE CASCADE)
  const { error } = await supabase.from('posts').delete().eq('id', postId);
  
  if (error) throw new Error('Erro ao deletar o post.');

  revalidatePath('/feed');
  return { success: true };
}

export const dynamic = 'force-dynamic';

export default async function AdminPage() {
  const supabase = await createServerClient();
  const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser();

  if (!user) redirect('/login');

  const { data: profile } = await supabase.from('profiles').select('is_admin').eq('id', user.id).single();
  if (!profile?.is_admin) return <div className="text-red-500 p-8">Acesso Negado</div>;

  const supabaseAdmin = createSupabaseClient(
    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL!,
    process.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY!,
    { auth: { autoRefreshToken: false, persistSession: false } }
  );

  // 1. Busca a configuração do site
  const { data: config } = await supabaseAdmin.from('site_config').select('chat_enabled, feed_enabled').single();
  const chatAtivo = config?.chat_enabled ?? true;
  const feedAtivo = config?.feed_enabled ?? true;

  // 2. Busca usuários e estatísticas
  const { data: users } = await supabaseAdmin.from('profiles').select('id, username, avatar_url, created_at').order('created_at', { ascending: false });
  const { data: allStatus } = await supabaseAdmin.from('user_anime').select('user_id, status, rating');

  // 3. Busca os feedbacks (só para admin)
  const { data: feedbacks } = await supabaseAdmin
    .from('feedbacks')
    .select('*, profiles(username)')
    .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
    .limit(20);

  const usersData = (users || []).map(u => {
    const userStatus = (allStatus || []).filter(s => s.user_id === u.id);
    const notas = userStatus.filter(s => s.rating && s.rating > 0);
    return {
      ...u,
      assistindo: userStatus.filter(s => s.status === 'assistindo').length,
      concluido: userStatus.filter(s => s.status === 'concluido').length,
      quero: userStatus.filter(s => s.status === 'quero').length,
      totalNotas: notas.length,
      media: notas.length > 0 ? notas.reduce((acc, curr) => acc + (curr.rating || 0), 0) / notas.length : 0
    };
  });

  return (
    <div className="w-full max-w-5xl mx-auto py-8">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-8">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <ShieldAlert className="text-accent" size={32} />
          <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold text-text">Painel de Administração</h1>
        </div>
        <Link href="/" className="text-sm font-semibold text-accent hover:underline flex items-center gap-1">
          <ArrowLeft size={16} /> Voltar para a Home
        </Link>
      </div>

      {/* BLOCO DE CONTROLE */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-6 mb-8">
        <div className="bg-surface border border-border p-6 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-between">
          <div>
            <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-text flex items-center gap-2">
              {chatAtivo ? '🟢 Chat Liberado' : '🔴 Chat Bloqueado'}
            </h2>
            <p className="text-muted text-sm mt-1">
              {chatAtivo ? 'Os usuários podem enviar mensagens.' : 'Os usuários veem uma tela de manutenção.'}
            </p>
          </div>
          <ToggleChat inicial={chatAtivo} />
        </div>

        <div className="bg-surface border border-border p-6 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-between">
          <div>
            <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-text flex items-center gap-2">
              {feedAtivo ? '🟢 Feed Liberado' : '🔴 Feed Bloqueado'}
            </h2>
            <p className="text-muted text-sm mt-1">
              {feedAtivo ? 'Os usuários podem postar no Feed.' : 'Os usuários veem uma tela de manutenção.'}
            </p>
          </div>
          <ToggleFeed inicial={feedAtivo} />

        </div>
        <GerenciarStreaming />
      </div>

      {/* ESTATÍSTICAS */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 gap-4 mb-8">
        <div className="bg-surface border border-border p-6 rounded-xl flex items-center gap-4">
          <div className="p-3 bg-accent/10 rounded-lg text-accent"><Users size={24} /></div>
          <div><p className="text-sm text-muted">Total de Usuários</p><p className="text-2xl font-bold text-text">{users?.length || 0}</p></div>
