===== C:\anime-site\app\amigos\page.tsx =====
'use client';

import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { createClient } from '@/lib/supabase/client';
import { Search, Loader2, UserPlus, User, MessageCircle } from 'lucide-react';
import Image from 'next/image';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { SeloAdmin } from '@/components/layout/SeloAdmin';

interface Profile {
  id: string;
  username: string;
  avatar_url: string;
  is_admin: boolean;
}

export default function BuscarAmigosPage() {
  const [query, setQuery] = useState('');
  const [resultados, setResultados] = useState<Profile[]>([]);
  const [buscando, setBuscando] = useState(false);
  const [buscou, setBuscou] = useState(false);
  const supabase = createClient();

  useEffect(() => {
    const delayDebounceFn = setTimeout(async () => {
      if (query.trim().length < 2) {
        setResultados([]);
        setBuscou(false);
        return;
      }

      setBuscando(true);
      setBuscou(true);

      // Busca usuários que contenham o texto digitado (ignorando maiúsculas/minúsculas)
      const { data } = await supabase
        .from('profiles')
        .select('id, username, avatar_url, is_admin')
        .ilike('username', `%${query}%`)
        .limit(10);

      if (data) setResultados(data);
      setBuscando(false);
    }, 500); // Debounce de 500ms para não fazer uma requisição a cada letra

    return () => clearTimeout(delayDebounceFn);
  }, [query, supabase]);

  return (
    <div className="w-full max-w-3xl mx-auto py-8">
      <header className="mb-8 text-center">
        <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold text-text mb-2">Encontre seus Amigos</h1>
        <p className="text-muted">Busque por usuários do AniVerse e comece a interagir.</p>
      </header>

      {/* Barra de Busca */}
      <div className="relative mb-8">
        <Search className="absolute left-4 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-muted" size={20} />
        <input
          type="text"
          value={query}
          onChange={(e) => setQuery(e.target.value)}
          placeholder="Digite o nome de usuário..."
          className="w-full bg-surface border border-border text-text rounded-2xl py-4 pl-12 pr-4 focus:outline-none focus:border-accent transition-colors text-lg shadow-sm"
        />
        {buscando && (
          <Loader2 className="absolute right-4 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 animate-spin text-accent" size={20} />
        )}
      </div>

      {/* Resultados */}
      <div className="bg-surface border border-border rounded-2xl overflow-hidden shadow-lg">
        {buscando && resultados.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="flex justify-center py-10"><Loader2 className="animate-spin text-accent" size={32} /></div>
        ) : !buscou ? (
          <div className="text-center py-20">
            <User className="mx-auto text-muted mb-4" size={48} />
            <p className="text-muted">Digite pelo menos 2 letras para começar a buscar.</p>
          </div>
        ) : resultados.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="text-center py-20">
            <p className="text-muted">Nenhum usuário encontrado com "{query}".</p>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div>
            {resultados.map((profile) => (
              <div key={profile.id} className="flex items-center gap-4 p-4 border-b border-border last:border-0 hover:bg-bg/50 transition-colors">
                <Link href={`/perfil/${profile.username}`} className="relative w-12 h-12 rounded-full overflow-hidden bg-border shrink-0 hover:ring-2 hover:ring-accent transition-all">
                  {profile.avatar_url ? (
                    <Image src={profile.avatar_url} alt="Avatar" fill sizes="48px" className="object-cover" />
                  ) : (
                    <div className="w-full h-full flex items-center justify-center text-sm font-bold">
                      {profile.username?.charAt(0).toUpperCase()}
                    </div>
                  )}
                </Link>
                
                <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                    <Link href={`/perfil/${profile.username}`} className="font-bold text-text truncate hover:text-accent transition-colors">
                      {profile.username}
                    </Link>
                    {profile.is_admin && <SeloAdmin />}
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <Link
                    href={`/perfil/${profile.username}`}
                    className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2 px-3 py-2 bg-bg border border-border text-text rounded-lg hover:border-accent hover:text-accent transition-colors font-medium text-sm cursor-pointer"
                  >
                    Ver Perfil
                  </Link>
                  <Link
                    href={`/mensagens/${profile.id}`}
                    className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2 px-3 py-2 bg-accent text-white rounded-lg hover:bg-accent/90 transition-colors font-medium text-sm cursor-pointer"
                  >
                    <MessageCircle size={16} /> Mensagem
                  </Link>
                </div>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

===== C:\anime-site\app\anime\[id]\page.tsx =====
import Image from 'next/image';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { ArrowLeft, Star } from 'lucide-react';
import { AcoesAnime } from '@/components/anime/AcoesAnime';
import { BotaoTrailer } from '@/components/anime/BotaoTrailer';
import { SecaoComentarios } from '@/components/anime/SecaoComentarios'; 
import { EspacoAnuncio } from '@/components/layout/EspacoAnuncio';
import { OndeAssistir } from '@/components/anime/OndeAssistir';
import { BannerGeek } from '@/components/anime/BannerGeek';

// ==========================================
// FUNÇÕES DE TRADUÇÃO E BUSCA
// ==========================================
async function traduzirComGroq(texto: string) {
  const API_KEY = process.env.GROQ_API_KEY; 
  if (!API_KEY) return null;
  const prompt = `Traduza a seguinte sinopse de anime para o português do Brasil. Retorne APENAS o texto traduzido: "${texto}"`;
  try {
    const res = await fetch('https://api.groq.com/openai/v1/chat/completions', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'Authorization': `Bearer ${API_KEY}` },
      body: JSON.stringify({ model: "openai/gpt-oss-20b", messages: [{ role: "system", content: "Você é um tradutor profissional de animes." }, { role: "user", content: prompt }], temperature: 0.3 })
    });
    if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`Groq HTTP: ${res.status}`);
    const data = await res.json();
    return data.choices?.[0]?.message?.content || null; 
  } catch (error) { return null; }
}

async function traduzirComGemini(texto: string) {
  const API_KEY = process.env.GEMINI_API_KEY; 
  if (!API_KEY) return null; 
  const prompt = `Traduza a seguinte sinopse de anime para o português do Brasil. Retorne APENAS o texto traduzido: "${texto}"`;
  try {
    const res = await fetch(`https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/models/gemini-3.5-flash-lite:generateContent?key=${API_KEY}`, {
      method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ contents: [{ parts: [{ text: prompt }] }] })
    });
    if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`Gemini HTTP: ${res.status}`);
    const data = await res.json();
    return data.candidates?.[0]?.content?.parts?.[0]?.text || null; 
  } catch (error) { return null; }
}

async function traduzirTexto(texto: string) {
  let traducao = await traduzirComGroq(texto);
  if (traducao) return traducao;
  traducao = await traduzirComGemini(texto);
  if (traducao) return traducao;
  return texto;
}

async function getAnimeFromAniList(id: string) {
  const query = `
    query ($id: Int) {
      Media(id: $id, type: ANIME) {
        id title { romaji english } coverImage { extraLarge large } bannerImage
        description(asHtml: false) genres status episodes seasonYear
        studios(isMain: true) { nodes { name } } averageScore trailer { id site }
      }
    }
  `;
  const res = await fetch('https://graphql.anilist.co', {
    method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ query, variables: { id: parseInt(id) } }), next: { revalidate: 86400 } 
  });
  if (!res.ok) throw new Error('AniList HTTP Error');
  const json = await res.json();
  if (json.errors || !json.data.Media) return null;
  const anime = json.data.Media;
  if (anime.description) {
    const textoLimpo = anime.description.replace(/<[^>]*>?/gm, '');
    anime.description = await traduzirTexto(textoLimpo); 
  }
  return anime;
}

async function getAnimeFromJikan(id: string) {
  const res = await fetch(`https://api.jikan.moe/v4/anime/${id}/full`, { next: { revalidate: 86400 } });
  if (!res.ok) return null;
  const json = await res.json();
  const data = json.data;
  if (!data) return null;
  let sinopse = data.synopsis || 'Sem sinopse disponível.';
  if (sinopse !== 'Sem sinopse disponível.') { sinopse = await traduzirTexto(sinopse); }
  return {
    id: data.mal_id, title: { english: data.title_english, romaji: data.title },
    coverImage: { extraLarge: data.images.webp.large_image_url, large: data.images.webp.large_image_url },
    bannerImage: data.trailer?.images?.maximum_image_url || null, description: sinopse,
    genres: data.genres?.map((g: any) => g.name) || [],
    status: data.status === 'Currently Airing' ? 'RELEASING' : 'FINISHED',
    episodes: data.episodes, seasonYear: data.year,
    studios: { nodes: data.studios?.map((s: any) => ({ name: s.name })) || [] },
    averageScore: data.score ? data.score * 10 : 0, 
    trailer: data.trailer?.youtube_id ? { id: data.trailer.youtube_id, site: 'youtube' } : null
  };
}

async function getAnimeDetails(id: string) {
  try {
    const anime = await getAnimeFromAniList(id);
    if (anime) return anime;
    throw new Error('Anime não encontrado no AniList');
  } catch (error) {
    try { return await getAnimeFromJikan(id); } catch (jikanError) { return null; }
  }
}

// ==========================================
// COMPONENTE DA PÁGINA
// ==========================================
export default async function AnimePage({ params }: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> }) {
  const { id } = await params;
  const anime = await getAnimeDetails(id);

  if (!anime) {
    return (
      <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center min-h-[50vh]">
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-text mb-4">Anime não encontrado</h1>
        <Link href="/" className="text-accent hover:underline flex items-center gap-2">
          <ArrowLeft size={16} /> Voltar para a Home
        </Link>
      </div>
    );
  }

  const title = anime.title.english || anime.title.romaji;
  const studio = anime.studios?.nodes?.[0]?.name || 'Estúdio Desconhecido';
  const sinopse = anime.description || 'Sem sinopse disponível.';

  return (
    <div className="relative w-full -mt-6">
      
      {/* Fundo Imersivo */}
      <div className="absolute top-0 left-0 w-full h-[40vh] md:h-[50vh] -z-10 overflow-hidden">
        <Image
          src={anime.bannerImage || anime.coverImage.large}
          alt={`Banner de ${title}`}
          fill sizes="100vw" className="object-cover blur-sm opacity-40 scale-105" priority
        />
        <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-b from-transparent via-bg/80 to-bg" />
      </div>

      <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 md:px-6 pt-20 md:pt-32 pb-12 flex flex-col lg:flex-row gap-8">
        
        {/* COLUNA ESQUERDA (CONTEÚDO PRINCIPAL) */}
        <div className="flex-1 w-full">
          
          {/* ANÚNCIO NO TOPO */}
          <div className="mb-8">
            <EspacoAnuncio formato="banner" />
          </div>

          <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row gap-8 md:gap-12">
            {/* PÔSTER + TRAILER + ANÚNCIO LATERAL ESQUERDO */}
            <div className="w-full md:w-1/3 flex-shrink-0 flex flex-col gap-6">
              <div className="relative aspect-[2/3] w-full max-w-[300px] mx-auto md:mx-0 rounded-xl overflow-hidden border-2 border-border shadow-2xl shadow-black/50">
                <Image 
                  src={anime.coverImage.extraLarge || anime.coverImage.large} 
                  alt={title} fill sizes="(max-width: 768px) 300px, 25vw" className="object-cover" priority 
                />
              </div>
              <div className="w-full max-w-[300px] mx-auto md:mx-0">
                <BotaoTrailer 
                  trailerId={anime.trailer?.site === 'youtube' ? anime.trailer.id : undefined} 
                  tituloAnime={title}
                />
              </div>
              <div className="w-full max-w-[300px] mx-auto md:mx-0 hidden md:block">
                <EspacoAnuncio formato="vertical" />
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* INFORMAÇÕES + SINOPSE + COMENTÁRIOS */}
            <div className="w-full md:w-2/3 flex flex-col">
              
              {/* Informações Básicas (Contraste Melhorado) */}
              <div className="flex items-center gap-3 mb-2 text-text/80 text-sm font-semibold">
                <span>{anime.seasonYear || 'Ano Desconhecido'}</span>
                <span>•</span>
                <span>{anime.status === 'RELEASING' ? 'Em exibição' : 'Finalizado'}</span>
                <span>•</span>
                <span>{anime.episodes || '?'} Episódios</span>
              </div>

              <h1 className="text-3xl md:text-5xl font-bold text-text mb-4 leading-tight">
                {title}
              </h1>

              <div className="flex items-center gap-4 mb-6">
                {/* Nota com Cores Melhoradas */}
                <div className="flex items-center gap-1 text-yellow-600 font-bold text-lg bg-yellow-400/20 px-3 py-1 rounded-lg border border-yellow-400/30">
                  <Star size={20} fill="currentColor" />
                  {anime.averageScore ? (anime.averageScore / 10).toFixed(1) : 'N/A'}
                </div>
                {/* Estúdio com Contraste Melhorado */}
                <div className="text-text/80 text-sm">
                  Estúdio: <span className="text-text font-medium">{studio}</span>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2 mb-4">
                {anime.genres?.map((genre: string) => (
                  <span key={genre} className="px-3 py-1 text-xs font-semibold bg-surface border border-border rounded-full text-text/80">
                    {genre}
                  </span>
                ))}
              </div>

              <div className="flex flex-col gap-5">
                <AcoesAnime animeId={anime.id} />
                <div className="-mt-4">
                  <OndeAssistir tituloAnime={title} animeId={anime.id} />
                </div>
                
                {/* BANNER DE PRODUTOS GEEK */}
                <BannerGeek tituloAnime={title} />
              </div>

              {/* SINOPSE com Contraste Melhorado */}
              <div className="mb-8 mt-6">
                <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-text mb-3">Sinopse</h2>
                <p className="text-text/80 leading-relaxed text-sm md:text-base">
                  {sinopse}
                </p>
              </div>

              <SecaoComentarios animeId={anime.id} />
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* ANÚNCIO NO FINAL */}
          <div className="mt-10">
            <EspacoAnuncio formato="banner" />
          </div>

          <div className="mt-6 pt-6 border-t border-border">
            <Link href="/" className="text-sm font-semibold text-accent hover:underline flex items-center gap-1">
              <ArrowLeft size={16} /> Voltar para os Lançamentos
            </Link>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* COLUNA DIREITA (ANÚNCIO LATERAL FINO) */}
        <div className="hidden lg:block w-40 flex-shrink-0">
          <div className="sticky top-24">
            <EspacoAnuncio formato="vertical" />
          </div>
        </div>

      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

===== C:\anime-site\app\api\admin\notificar\route.ts =====
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { createClient as createServerClient } from '@/lib/supabase/server';
import { createClient as createSupabaseClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js';

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  try {
    const { userId, title, message } = await req.json();

    if (!userId || !title || !message) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Dados incompletos' }, { status: 400 });
    }

    // 1. Verifica quem está chamando a API (precisa ser Admin)
    const supabase = await createServerClient();
    const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser();

    if (!user) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Não autorizado' }, { status: 401 });

    const { data: profile } = await supabase.from('profiles').select('is_admin').eq('id', user.id).single();
    if (!profile?.is_admin) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Acesso negado' }, { status: 403 });

    // 2. Cliente Admin para inserir a notificação (ignora RLS)
    const supabaseAdmin = createSupabaseClient(
      process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL!,
      process.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY!,
      { auth: { autoRefreshToken: false, persistSession: false } }
    );

    // 3. Insere a notificação
    const { error: insertError } = await supabaseAdmin
      .from('notifications')
      .insert({
        user_id: userId,
        title: title,
        message: message,
        is_read: false
      });

    if (insertError) throw insertError;

    return NextResponse.json({ success: true });

  } catch (error: any) {
    console.error("Erro ao enviar notificação:", error);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: error.message || 'Erro interno' }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

===== C:\anime-site\app\api\admin\toggle-chat\route.ts =====
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { createClient as createServerClient } from '@/lib/supabase/server';
import { createClient as createSupabaseClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js';

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  try {
    const { enabled } = await req.json();
    
    const supabase = await createServerClient();
    const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
    
    if (!user) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Não autorizado' }, { status: 401 });

    const supabaseAdmin = createSupabaseClient(
      process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL!,
      process.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY!,
      { auth: { autoRefreshToken: false, persistSession: false } }
    );

    const { error } = await supabaseAdmin
      .from('site_config')
      .update({ chat_enabled: enabled })
      .eq('id', 1);

    if (error) return NextResponse.json({ error: error.message }, { status: 500 });
    return NextResponse.json({ success: true });
  } catch (error) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erro interno' }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

===== C:\anime-site\app\api\admin\toggle-feed\route.ts =====
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { createClient } from '@/lib/supabase/server';

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  const supabase = await createClient();
  
  // Verifica se o usuário está logado
  const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
  if (!user) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Não autorizado' }, { status: 401 });

  // Pega o valor que o botão enviou (true ou false)
  const { enabled } = await req.json();

  // Salva no banco
  const { error } = await supabase
    .from('site_config')
    .update({ feed_enabled: enabled })
    .eq('id', 1); 

  if (error) {
    console.error("Erro no banco:", error);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: error.message }, { status: 500 });
  }

  return NextResponse.json({ success: true });
}

===== C:\anime-site\app\api\anime\[id]\route.ts =====
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';

export async function GET(
  req: Request,
  { params }: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> }
) {
  try {
    const { id } = await params;
    const animeId = parseInt(id);

    if (isNaN(animeId)) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'ID inválido' }, { status: 400 });
    }

    // Voltando para o AniList (mais estável)
    const res = await fetch('https://graphql.anilist.co', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        query: `query ($id: Int) { Media(id: $id, type: ANIME) { id title { romaji english } coverImage { large } averageScore } }`,
        variables: { id: animeId }
      }),
      next: { revalidate: 86400 }
    });

    if (!res.ok) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Não encontrado' }, { status: 404 });
    }

    const json = await res.json();
    if (!json.data || !json.data.Media) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Anime não encontrado' }, { status: 404 });
    }

    const media = json.data.Media;

    return NextResponse.json({
      id: media.id,
      title: media.title.english || media.title.romaji,
      coverUrl: media.coverImage.large,
      score: media.averageScore ? (media.averageScore / 10).toFixed(1) : 'N/A'
    });

  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Erro na rota do AniList:", error);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erro ao buscar anime' }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

===== C:\anime-site\app\api\comments\route.ts =====
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { createClient } from '@/lib/supabase/server';

// 1. Buscar comentários de um anime
export async function GET(req: Request) {
  const { searchParams } = new URL(req.url);
  const animeId = searchParams.get('animeId');

  if (!animeId) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'ID do anime é obrigatório' }, { status: 400 });

  const supabase = await createClient();

  const { data, error } = await supabase
    .from('comments')
    .select(`
      id,
      content,
      has_spoiler,
      created_at,
      user_id,
      profiles (username)
    `)
    .eq('anime_id', animeId)
    .order('created_at', { ascending: false }); // Mais recentes primeiro

  if (error) return NextResponse.json({ error: error.message }, { status: 500 });

  return NextResponse.json(data);
}

===== C:\anime-site\app\api\comments\[id]\route.ts =====
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { createClient } from '@/lib/supabase/server';

export async function DELETE(req: Request, { params }: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> }) {
  const { id } = await params;
  const supabase = await createClient();
  const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser();

  if (!user) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Não autorizado' }, { status: 401 });

  // Deleta o comentário (o RLS garante que ele só apaga se for o dono)
  const { error } = await supabase
    .from('comments')
    .delete()
    .eq('id', id)
    .eq('user_id', user.id);

  if (error) return NextResponse.json({ error: error.message }, { status: 500 });

  return NextResponse.json({ success: true });
}

===== C:\anime-site\app\api\cron\route.ts =====
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { createClient as createAdminClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js';

export async function GET(req: Request) {
  // 1. Verifica a segurança (Vercel ou Supabase chamando)
  const authHeader = req.headers.get('authorization');
  if (authHeader !== `Bearer ${process.env.CRON_SECRET}`) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Não autorizado' }, { status: 401 });
  }

  const supabaseAdmin = createAdminClient(
    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL!,
    process.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY!,
    { auth: { autoRefreshToken: false, persistSession: false } }
  );

  try {
    // 2. Busca no AniList os animes que lançam nos próximos 20 minutos
    const agora = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);
    const daqui20min = agora + 1200;

    const query = `
      query ($inicio: Int, $fim: Int) {
        Page(page: 1, perPage: 50) {
          airingSchedules(airingAt_greater: $inicio, airingAt_lesser: $fim) {
            mediaId
            episode
            media {
              title { romaji english }
            }
          }
        }
      }
    `;

    const res = await fetch('https://graphql.anilist.co', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ query, variables: { inicio: agora - 300, fim: daqui20min } })
    });

    const json = await res.json();
    const episodios = json.data?.Page?.airingSchedules || [];

    if (episodios.length === 0) {
      return NextResponse.json({ message: 'Nenhum episódio lançando agora.' });
    }

    let notificacoesEnviadas = 0;

    // 3. Para cada anime lançando, busca os usuários que marcaram "Assistindo"
    for (const ep of episodios) {
      const animeId = ep.mediaId;
      const titulo = ep.media.title.english || ep.media.title.romaji;

      const { data: usuarios } = await supabaseAdmin
        .from('user_anime')
        .select('user_id')
        .eq('anime_id', animeId)
        .eq('status', 'assistindo');

      if (usuarios && usuarios.length > 0) {
        const novasNotificacoes = usuarios.map((u) => ({
          user_id: u.user_id,
          title: `Novo episódio de ${titulo}! 🍿`,
          message: `O episódio ${ep.episode} acabou de ser lançado. Vá assistir!`
        }));

        const { error } = await supabaseAdmin.from('notifications').insert(novasNotificacoes);
        
        if (!error) notificacoesEnviadas += novasNotificacoes.length;
      }
    }

    return NextResponse.json({ 
      success: true, 
      animesAnalisados: episodios.length, 
      notificacoesEnviadas 
    });

  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Erro no Cron Job:", error);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erro interno' }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

===== C:\anime-site\app\api\delete-account\route.ts =====
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { createClient } from '@/lib/supabase/server';
import { createClient as createAdminClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js';

export async function DELETE() {
  console.log("🚀 [API Delete] Iniciando processo de exclusão...");

  try {
    // 1. Verifica o usuário logado
    const supabase = await createClient();
    const { data: { user }, error: authError } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
    
    if (authError || !user) {
      console.error("🚨 [API Delete] Erro de autenticação: Usuário não encontrado ou sessão inválida.");
      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Não autorizado' }, { status: 401 });
    }

    console.log(`✅ [API Delete] Usuário autenticado: ${user.id}. Preparando para deletar...`);

    // 2. Verifica se a chave secreta existe
    const serviceRoleKey = process.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY;
    if (!serviceRoleKey) {
      console.error("🚨 [API Delete] ERRO GRAVE: A chave SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY não foi encontrada no .env.local");
      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erro de configuração no servidor' }, { status: 500 });
    }

    // 3. Cria o cliente Admin
    const supabaseAdmin = createAdminClient(
      process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL!,
      serviceRoleKey,
      {
        auth: { autoRefreshToken: false, persistSession: false },
      }
    );

    // 4. Deleta o usuário
    console.log("⏳ [API Delete] Solicitando exclusão ao Supabase...");
    const { error: deleteError } = await supabaseAdmin.auth.admin.deleteUser(user.id);

    if (deleteError) {
      console.error("🚨 [API Delete] Erro retornado pelo Supabase ao deletar:", deleteError);
      throw deleteError;
    }

    console.log("✅ [API Delete] Usuário deletado com sucesso do banco!");
    
    // 5. Desloga o usuário
    await supabase.auth.signOut();

    return NextResponse.json({ success: true });

  } catch (error: any) {
    console.error("🚨 [API Delete] Erro fatal no bloco Catch:", error);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: error.message || 'Erro interno do servidor' }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

===== C:\anime-site\app\api\destaques\route.ts =====
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';

export async function GET() {
  try {
    const dataHoje = new Date();
    const mes = parseInt(dataHoje.toLocaleString('pt-BR', { timeZone: 'America/Sao_Paulo', month: 'numeric' }));
    const ano = parseInt(dataHoje.toLocaleString('pt-BR', { timeZone: 'America/Sao_Paulo', year: 'numeric' }));

    let temporada = 'WINTER';
    if (mes >= 4 && mes <= 6) temporada = 'SPRING';
    else if (mes >= 7 && mes <= 9) temporada = 'SUMMER';
    else if (mes >= 10 && mes <= 12) temporada = 'FALL';

    const query = `
      query ($temporada: MediaSeason, $ano: Int) {
        doDia: Page(page: 1, perPage: 10) {
          media(type: ANIME, sort: TRENDING_DESC) {
            id title { romaji english } coverImage { large } bannerImage averageScore episodes
          }
        }
        daSemana: Page(page: 1, perPage: 10) {
          media(status: RELEASING, type: ANIME, sort: POPULARITY_DESC) {
            id title { romaji english } coverImage { large } averageScore episodes nextAiringEpisode { episode }
          }
        }
        daTemporada: Page(page: 1, perPage: 10) {
          media(season: $temporada, seasonYear: $ano, type: ANIME, sort: POPULARITY_DESC) {
            id title { romaji english } coverImage { large } averageScore episodes
          }
        }
      }
    `;

    const res = await fetch('https://graphql.anilist.co', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ query, variables: { temporada, ano } }),
      next: { revalidate: 21600 }
    });

    if (!res.ok) throw new Error('AniList falhou');
    const json = await res.json();
    if (json.errors) throw new Error('Erro na query do AniList');

    const formatar = (media: any[]) => {
      if (!media) return [];
      return media.map((m) => ({
        id: m.id,
        title: m.title.english || m.title.romaji,
        coverUrl: m.coverImage.large,
        bannerUrl: m.bannerImage || m.coverImage.large,
        score: m.averageScore ? (m.averageScore / 10).toFixed(1) : 'N/A',
        episodes: m.nextAiringEpisode?.episode || m.episodes || '?'
      }));
    };

    return NextResponse.json({
      doDia: formatar(json.data?.doDia?.media || []),
      daSemana: formatar(json.data?.daSemana?.media || []),
      daTemporada: formatar(json.data?.daTemporada?.media || []),
    });

  } catch (error: any) {
    return NextResponse.json({ doDia: [], daSemana: [], daTemporada: [] }, { status: 200 }); 
  }
}

===== C:\anime-site\app\api\lancamentos-semana\route.ts =====
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';

// A MESMA query que a sua Home usa (que já está funcionando)
const ANILIST_QUERY = `
  query {
    Page(page: 1, perPage: 50) {
      media(status: RELEASING, sort: POPULARITY_DESC, type: ANIME) {
        id
        title { romaji english native }
        coverImage { extraLarge large }
        averageScore
        nextAiringEpisode { airingAt timeUntilAiring episode }
      }
    }
  }
`;

export async function GET() {
  try {
    const response = await fetch('https://graphql.anilist.co', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'Accept': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ query: ANILIST_QUERY }),
      next: { revalidate: 21600 } // Cache de 6 horas
    });

    if (!response.ok) throw new Error('Falha na API do AniList');
    const json = await response.json();
    
    if (json.errors) throw new Error('AniList retornou erro');

    // Filtra apenas os que têm próximo episódio e formata os dados
    const animes = json.data.Page.media
      .filter((anime: any) => anime.nextAiringEpisode !== null)
      .map((anime: any) => ({
        id: anime.id,
        title: anime.title.english || anime.title.romaji,
        coverUrl: anime.coverImage.large,
        score: anime.averageScore || 0,
      }));

    return NextResponse.json(animes);

  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Erro na rota /api/lancamentos-semana:", error);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erro ao buscar animes' }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

===== C:\anime-site\app\api\proxy-image\route.ts =====
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';

export async function GET(req: Request) {
  const { searchParams } = new URL(req.url);
  const imageUrl = searchParams.get('url');

  if (!imageUrl) return new NextResponse('URL missing', { status: 400 });

  try {
    const res = await fetch(imageUrl);
    const buffer = await res.arrayBuffer();

    return new NextResponse(buffer, {
      headers: {
        'Content-Type': res.headers.get('Content-Type') || 'image/jpeg',
        'Cache-Control': 'public, max-age=86400', // Cache de 1 dia
        // Libera o acesso para o seu site
        'Access-Control-Allow-Origin': '*', 
      },
    });
  } catch (error) {
    return new NextResponse('Failed to fetch image', { status: 500 });
  }
}

===== C:\anime-site\app\api\search-anime\route.tsx =====
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  try {
    const { search } = await req.json();

    const res = await fetch('https://graphql.anilist.co', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        query: `
          query ($search: String) {
            Page(page: 1, perPage: 5) {
              media(search: $search, type: ANIME) {
                id
                title { romaji english }
                coverImage { large }
                averageScore
              }
            }
          }
        `,
        variables: { search }
      }),
      next: { revalidate: 86400 }
    });

    if (!res.ok) throw new Error('AniList falhou');

    const json = await res.json();
    
    const results = json.data.Page.media.map((media: any) => ({
      id: media.id,
      title: media.title.english || media.title.romaji,
      coverUrl: media.coverImage.large,
      score: media.averageScore ? (media.averageScore / 10).toFixed(1) : 'N/A'
    }));

    return NextResponse.json(results);
  } catch (error) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erro na busca' }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

===== C:\anime-site\app\api\test-anilist\route.ts =====
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';

// ==========================================
// 1. QUERY DO ANILIST (PLANO A)
// ==========================================
const ANILIST_QUERY = `
  query {
    Page(page: 1, perPage: 50) {
      media(status: RELEASING, sort: POPULARITY_DESC, type: ANIME) {
        id
        title { romaji english native }
        coverImage { extraLarge large }
        averageScore
        nextAiringEpisode { airingAt timeUntilAiring episode }
      }
    }
  }
`;

// ==========================================
// 2. FUNÇÃO DO JIKAN (PLANO B)
// ==========================================
async function fetchJikanFallback(diaSelecionado: number) {
  const res = await fetch('https://api.jikan.moe/v4/seasons/now?limit=25&sfw=true', {
    next: { revalidate: 21600 } // Cache de 6 horas
  });

  if (!res.ok) throw new Error('Jikan também falhou');
  
  const json = await res.json();
  const animesDoJikan = json.data;

  // Traduzindo o formato do Jikan para o formato do AniList (que o seu site já usa)
  const animesFormatados = animesDoJikan
    .map((anime: any) => {
      // O Jikan traz os horários no padrão "Wednesdays" (Quartas)
      const diasDaSemanaIngles = ['Sundays', 'Mondays', 'Tuesdays', 'Wednesdays', 'Thursdays', 'Fridays', 'Saturdays'];
      const diasDaSemanaPt = ['Dom', 'Seg', 'Ter', 'Qua', 'Qui', 'Sex', 'Sáb'];
      const diaDoAnimeIngles = anime.broadcast?.day;

      // Verifica se o anime passa no dia selecionado
      if (!diaDoAnimeIngles || diasDaSemanaIngles.indexOf(diaDoAnimeIngles) !== diaSelecionado) {
        return null; // Descarta se não for do dia
      }

      return {
        id: anime.mal_id,
        title: anime.title_english || anime.title,
        coverUrl: anime.images.webp.large_image_url,
        score: anime.score ? anime.score * 10 : 0, // Jikan usa 0-10, AniList usa 0-100
        formattedTime: anime.broadcast.time ? anime.broadcast.time : '00:00',
        episode: anime.episodes || 1, // Se não tiver episódio definido, assume 1
        source: 'jikan' // Apenas para debug, caso queira saber de onde veio
      };
    })
    .filter((anime: any) => anime !== null); // Remove os nulos

  return animesFormatados;
}

// ==========================================
// 3. A ROTA GET (O Cérebro)
// ==========================================
export async function GET(req: Request) {
  // Pega o dia da URL (ex: ?dia=2)
  const { searchParams } = new URL(req.url);
  const diaUrl = searchParams.get('dia');
  // Converte 'qua' para o número 3 (Domingo=0, Segunda=1...)
  const mapaDias: Record<string, number> = { 'dom': 0, 'seg': 1, 'ter': 2, 'qua': 3, 'qui': 4, 'sex': 5, 'sab': 6 };
  const diaSelecionado = mapaDias[diaUrl || 'qua']; // Fallback para quarta se não vier nada

  try {
    // ---- TENTATIVA 1: ANILIST ----
    const response = await fetch('https://graphql.anilist.co', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'Accept': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ query: ANILIST_QUERY }),
      next: { revalidate: 21600 } // Cache de 6 horas
    });

    if (!response.ok) throw new Error('AniList caiu');
    const json = await response.json();
    
    if (json.errors) throw new Error('AniList retornou erro');

    // Filtra os animes que são do dia selecionado
    const animesDoAniList = json.data.Page.media
      .filter((anime: any) => anime.nextAiringEpisode !== null)
      .filter((anime: any) => {
        const dataLancamento = new Date(anime.nextAiringEpisode.airingAt * 1000);
        return dataLancamento.getDay() === diaSelecionado;
      })
      .map((anime: any) => {
        const dataLancamento = new Date(anime.nextAiringEpisode.airingAt * 1000);
        const formattedTime = dataLancamento.toLocaleTimeString('pt-BR', {
          hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit', timeZone: 'America/Sao_Paulo'
        });

        return {
          id: anime.id,
          title: anime.title.english || anime.title.romaji,
          coverUrl: anime.coverImage.large,
          score: anime.averageScore || 0,
          formattedTime,
          episode: anime.nextAiringEpisode.episode,
          source: 'anilist'
        };
      });

    return NextResponse.json({ success: true, animesDeHoje: animesDoAniList, source: 'AniList' });

  } catch (error) {
    // ---- TENTATIVA 2: JIKAN (FALLBACK) ----
    console.warn("⚠️ AniList falhou ou está fora do ar. Acionando plano B (Jikan)...");
    
    try {
      const animesDoJikan = await fetchJikanFallback(diaSelecionado);
      return NextResponse.json({ success: true, animesDeHoje: animesDoJikan, source: 'Jikan' });
      
    } catch (jikanError) {
      // ---- TENTATIVA 3: TUDO FALHOU ----
      console.error("🚨 Ambas as APIs caíram:", jikanError);
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: "As APIs de anime estão em manutenção. Tente novamente mais tarde.", animesDeHoje: [] }, 
        { status: 503 }
      );
    }
  }
}

===== C:\anime-site\app\api\top10-image\route.tsx =====
import { ImageResponse } from '@vercel/og';
import { NextRequest } from 'next/server';

// Mudamos de 'edge' para 'nodejs' para evitar bloqueios de rede
export const runtime = 'nodejs'; 

export async function GET(req: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const { searchParams } = new URL(req.url);
    const username = searchParams.get('username') || 'usuario';
    const animesParam = searchParams.get('animes');

    if (!animesParam) return new Response('Faltando animes', { status: 400 });

    const animes = JSON.parse(animesParam);

    // Precisamos baixar as imagens e converter para Base64 aqui no servidor
    // para o gerador de imagem não ter bloqueio de CORS
    const animesComImagens = await Promise.all(
      animes.map(async (anime: any) => {
        try {
          const response = await fetch(anime.coverUrl);
          const arrayBuffer = await response.arrayBuffer();
          const base64 = Buffer.from(arrayBuffer).toString('base64');
          const mimeType = response.headers.get('content-type') || 'image/jpeg';
          return { ...anime, coverBase64: `data:${mimeType};base64,${base64}` };
        } catch (e) {
          return { ...anime, coverBase64: anime.coverUrl }; // Se falhar, tenta o link direto
        }
      })
    );

    return new ImageResponse(
      (
        <div
          style={{
            width: '100%', height: '100%', display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column',
            alignItems: 'center', backgroundColor: '#0a0a0f', padding: '48px',
            fontFamily: 'sans-serif',
          }}
        >
          {/* Cabeçalho */}
          <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', alignItems: 'center', marginBottom: '40px' }}>
            <h2 style={{ color: '#a855f7', fontSize: '30px', margin: 0, textTransform: 'uppercase', letterSpacing: '2px' }}>
              Meu Top 10 Animes
            </h2>
            <h1 style={{ color: 'white', fontSize: '60px', margin: '10px 0 0 0', fontWeight: '900' }}>
              @{username}
            </h1>
          </div>

          {/* Grid de Animes */}
          <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexWrap: 'wrap', gap: '16px', justifyContent: 'center', width: '100%' }}>
            {animesComImagens.map((anime: any, index: number) => (
              <div key={index} style={{ 
                display: 'flex', position: 'relative', width: '310px', height: '465px', 
                borderRadius: '16px', overflow: 'hidden', backgroundColor: '#14141c' 
              }}>
                {/* eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element */}
                <img src={anime.coverBase64} alt={anime.title} style={{ width: '100%', height: '100%', objectFit: 'cover' }} />
                
                {/* Número */}
                <div style={{ 
                  position: 'absolute', top: '8px', left: '8px', width: '40px', height: '40px', 
                  backgroundColor: '#a855f7', color: 'white', display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', 
                  justifyContent: 'center', fontSize: '24px', fontWeight: 'bold', borderRadius: '8px' 
                }}>
                  {index + 1}
                </div>

                {/* Título */}
                <div style={{ 
                  position: 'absolute', bottom: 0, width: '100%', padding: '12px',
                  background: 'linear-gradient(to top, rgba(0,0,0,0.9), transparent)',
                }}>
                  <h3 style={{ color: 'white', fontSize: '16px', margin: 0, fontWeight: 'bold' }}>
                    {anime.title}
                  </h3>
                </div>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>

          {/* Logo */}
          <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '12px', marginTop: '40px' }}>
            <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', width: '40px', height: '40px', borderRadius: '50%', border: '4px solid #a855f7' }}>
              <span style={{ color: '#a855f7', fontWeight: '900', fontSize: '20px', marginTop: '4px' }}>A</span>
            </div>
            <span style={{ color: 'white', fontSize: '24px', fontWeight: 'bold', letterSpacing: '2px' }}>
              <span style={{ color: '#a855f7' }}>ANI</span>VERSE
            </span>
          </div>
        </div>
      ),
      { width: 1080, height: 1350 }
    );
  } catch (e: any) {
    console.error("Erro na geração da imagem:", e);
    return new Response(`Falha ao gerar imagem: ${e.message}`, { status: 500 });
  }
}

===== C:\anime-site\app\auth\callback\route.ts =====
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { createClient } from '@/lib/supabase/server';

export async function GET(request: Request) {
  const { searchParams, origin } = new URL(request.url);
  const code = searchParams.get('code');
  const next = searchParams.get('next') ?? '/';

  if (code) {
    const supabase = await createClient();
    const { error } = await supabase.auth.exchangeCodeForSession(code);
    
    if (!error) {
      // Verifica se o usuário já tem um username
      const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
      const { data: profile } = await supabase
        .from('profiles')
        .select('username')
        .eq('id', user?.id)
        .single();

      // Se não tiver username, manda para completar o perfil
      if (!profile?.username) {
        return NextResponse.redirect(`${origin}/completar-perfil`);
      }

      // Se já tiver, segue o fluxo normal
      return NextResponse.redirect(`${origin}${next}`);
    }
  }

  return NextResponse.redirect(`${origin}/login?error=Could not authenticate`);
}

===== C:\anime-site\app\chat\page.tsx =====
'use client';

import { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { createClient } from '@/lib/supabase/client';
import { Loader2, Send, Lock } from 'lucide-react';
import { formatDistanceToNow } from 'date-fns';
import { ptBR } from 'date-fns/locale';
import Image from 'next/image';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { SeloAdmin } from '@/components/layout/SeloAdmin'; // <-- IMPORT DO SELO

interface Mensagem {
  id: string;
  content: string;
  created_at: string;
  user_id: string;
  profiles: {
    username: string;
    avatar_url: string;
    is_admin: boolean; // <-- is_admin
  };
}

export default function ChatPage() {
  const [mensagens, setMensagens] = useState<Mensagem[]>([]);
  const [novaMensagem, setNovaMensagem] = useState('');
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [bloqueado, setBloqueado] = useState(false);
  const [userId, setUserId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  
  const bottomRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const supabase = createClient();

  useEffect(() => {
    const init = async () => {
      // Verifica bloqueio
      const { data: config } = await supabase.from('site_config').select('chat_enabled').single();
      if (config?.chat_enabled === false) {
        setBloqueado(true);
        setLoading(false);
        return;
      }

      const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
      if (user) setUserId(user.id);

      // Busca mensagens iniciais
      const { data } = await supabase
        .from('chat_messages')
        .select(`id, content, created_at, user_id, profiles (username, avatar_url, is_admin)`)
        .order('created_at', { ascending: true })
        .limit(50);

      if (data) setMensagens(data as unknown as Mensagem[]);
      setLoading(false);
    };

    init();

    // Realtime
    const channel = supabase
      .channel('chat-realtime')
      .on(
        'postgres_changes',
        { event: 'INSERT', schema: 'public', table: 'chat_messages' },
        async (payload) => {
          // Quando chega uma nova, busca os dados do perfil dela
          const { data } = await supabase
            .from('chat_messages')
            .select(`id, content, created_at, user_id, profiles (username, avatar_url, is_admin)`)
            .eq('id', payload.new.id)
            .single();
            
          if (data) {
            setMensagens((prev) => [...prev, data as unknown as Mensagem]);
          }
        }
      )
      .subscribe();

    return () => { supabase.removeChannel(channel); };
  }, [supabase]);

  useEffect(() => {
    // Auto-scroll para o final
    bottomRef.current?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth' });
  }, [mensagens]);

  const handleEnviar = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!novaMensagem.trim() || !userId) return;

    const msg = novaMensagem;
    setNovaMensagem('');

    await supabase.from('chat_messages').insert({
      user_id: userId,
      content: msg
    });
  };

  if (loading) {
    return <div className="flex justify-center items-center h-[50vh]"><Loader2 className="animate-spin text-accent" size={40} /></div>;
  }

  if (bloqueado) {
    return (
      <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center min-h-[70vh] text-center px-4">
        <div className="bg-surface border border-border p-6 rounded-full mb-6">
          <Lock size={48} className="text-muted" />
        </div>
        <h2 className="text-3xl font-bold text-text mb-4">Chat em Manutenção</h2>
        <p className="text-muted max-w-md mx-auto mb-8">Estamos preparando a comunidade do AniVerse para você. O chat será liberado em breve!</p>
        <Link href="/lancamentos" className="bg-accent text-white font-bold px-8 py-3 rounded-lg hover:bg-accent/90 transition-colors shadow-lg shadow-accent/20">
          Voltar para os Lançamentos
        </Link>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="w-full max-w-3xl mx-auto py-8 flex flex-col h-[85vh]">
      <div className="bg-surface border border-border rounded-2xl flex flex-col flex-1 overflow-hidden shadow-lg">
        
        <div className="p-4 border-b border-border bg-bg/50 flex items-center justify-between">
          <h1 className="text-xl font-bold text-text">Chat da Comunidade</h1>
        </div>

        <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-4 space-y-4">
          {mensagens.map((msg) => {
            const isMe = msg.user_id === userId;
            return (
              <div key={msg.id} className={`flex gap-3 ${isMe ? 'flex-row-reverse' : ''}`}>
                <div className="relative w-8 h-8 rounded-full overflow-hidden bg-border shrink-0">
                  {msg.profiles?.avatar_url ? (
                    <Image src={msg.profiles.avatar_url} alt="Avatar" fill className="object-cover" />
                  ) : (
                    <div className="w-full h-full flex items-center justify-center text-xs font-bold">{msg.profiles?.username?.charAt(0).toUpperCase()}</div>
                  )}
                </div>
                <div className={`flex flex-col ${isMe ? 'items-end' : 'items-start'} max-w-[80%]`}>
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 mb-1">
                    <span className="text-xs font-bold text-muted">{msg.profiles?.username}</span>
                    {msg.profiles?.is_admin && <SeloAdmin />} {/* <-- SELO AQUI */}
                  </div>
                  <div className={`p-3 rounded-2xl text-sm ${isMe ? 'bg-accent text-white rounded-tr-sm' : 'bg-bg text-text rounded-tl-sm border border-border'}`}>
                    {msg.content}
                  </div>
                  <span className="text-[10px] text-muted mt-1 px-1">
                    {formatDistanceToNow(new Date(msg.created_at), { addSuffix: true, locale: ptBR })}
                  </span>
                </div>
              </div>
            );
          })}
          <div ref={bottomRef} />
        </div>

        <form onSubmit={handleEnviar} className="p-3 border-t border-border bg-surface flex gap-2">
          <input
            type="text"
            value={novaMensagem}
            onChange={(e) => setNovaMensagem(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="Digite sua mensagem..."
            className="flex-1 bg-bg border border-border rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm text-text focus:outline-none focus:border-accent transition-colors"
          />
          <button type="submit" disabled={!novaMensagem.trim()} className="bg-accent text-white p-3 rounded-xl disabled:opacity-50 hover:bg-accent/90 transition-colors cursor-pointer">
            <Send size={20} />
          </button>
        </form>

      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

===== C:\anime-site\app\completar-perfil\page.tsx =====
'use client';

import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { createClient } from '@/lib/supabase/client';
import { Loader2, User, Check } from 'lucide-react';
import Image from 'next/image';

// Lista de avatares (os mesmos que você já tem)
const AVATARES = [
  { id: 'aria', name: 'Aria', image: '/avatars/Aria.png' },
  { id: 'astra', name: 'Astra', image: '/avatars/Astra.png' },
  { id: 'ignis', name: 'Ignis', image: '/avatars/Ignis.png' },
  { id: 'kira', name: 'Kira', image: '/avatars/Kira.png' },
  { id: 'koby', name: 'Koby', image: '/avatars/Koby.png' },
  { id: 'maya', name: 'Maya', image: '/avatars/Maya.png' },
  { id: 'nyx', name: 'Nyx', image: '/avatars/Nyx.png' },
  { id: 'orion', name: 'Orion', image: '/avatars/Orion.png' },
];

export default function CompletarPerfilPage() {
  const [username, setUsername] = useState('');
  const [selectedAvatar, setSelectedAvatar] = useState('');
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  
  const router = useRouter();
  const supabase = createClient();

  useEffect(() => {
    // Se o usuário não estiver logado, manda para o login
    const checkUser = async () => {
      const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
      if (!user) {
        router.push('/login');
        return;
      }

      // Se ele já tiver um username, manda para a home (já completou)
      const { data: profile } = await supabase
        .from('profiles')
        .select('username')
        .eq('id', user.id)
        .single();

      if (profile?.username) {
        router.push('/');
      }
    };
    checkUser();
  }, [supabase, router]);

  const handleSalvar = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setLoading(true);
    setError(null);

    if (username.trim().length < 3) {
      setError('O nome deve ter pelo menos 3 letras.');
      setLoading(false);
      return;
    }

    if (!selectedAvatar) {
      setError('Escolha um avatar para continuar.');
      setLoading(false);
      return;
    }

    const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
    if (!user) return;

    // Atualiza o perfil no banco
    const { error } = await supabase
      .from('profiles')
      .update({ 
        username: username.trim(),
        avatar_url: selectedAvatar 
      })
      .eq('id', user.id);

    if (error) {
      if (error.code === '23505') { // Erro de username duplicado
        setError('Este nome de usuário já está em uso. Escolha outro.');
      } else {
        setError('Erro ao salvar. Tente novamente.');
      }
      setLoading(false);
    } else {
      // Sucesso! Manda para a home
      router.push('/');
      router.refresh();
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="min-h-[80vh] flex items-center justify-center px-4 py-8">
      <div className="w-full max-w-lg bg-surface border border-border rounded-2xl p-8 shadow-2xl">
        <div className="text-center mb-8">
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-text mb-2">Quase lá!</h1>
          <p className="text-muted text-sm">Escolha como você quer ser chamado no AniVerse.</p>
        </div>

        {error && (
          <div className="bg-red-500/10 border border-red-500/50 text-red-400 text-sm p-3 rounded-lg mb-6">
            {error}
          </div>
        )}

        <form onSubmit={handleSalvar} className="flex flex-col gap-6">
          <div>
            <label className="text-sm font-semibold text-text mb-2 block">Seu nome de usuário</label>
            <div className="relative">
              <User className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-muted" size={18} />
              <input
                type="text"
                value={username}
                onChange={(e) => setUsername(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="Ex: Kal"
                required
                className="w-full bg-bg border border-border text-text rounded-lg py-3 pl-10 pr-4 focus:outline-none focus:border-accent"
              />
            </div>
          </div>

          <div>
            <label className="text-sm font-semibold text-text mb-2 block">Escolha seu avatar</label>
            <div className="grid grid-cols-4 gap-3">
              {AVATARES.map((avatar) => (
                <button
                  key={avatar.id}
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setSelectedAvatar(avatar.image)}
                  className={`relative aspect-square rounded-full overflow-hidden border-2 transition-all ${
                    selectedAvatar === avatar.image ? 'border-accent ring-4 ring-accent/20' : 'border-border hover:border-accent/50'
                  }`}
                >
                  <Image src={avatar.image} alt={avatar.name} fill className="object-cover" />
                  {selectedAvatar === avatar.image && (
                    <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-accent/20 flex items-center justify-center">
                      <Check className="text-white drop-shadow-md" size={24} />
                    </div>
                  )}
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>

          <button
            type="submit"
            disabled={loading}
            className="w-full bg-accent text-white font-bold py-3 rounded-lg hover:bg-accent/90 transition-all flex items-center justify-center gap-2 disabled:opacity-50 cursor-pointer"
          >
            {loading ? <Loader2 className="animate-spin" size={20} /> : 'Começar a usar o AniVerse'}
          </button>
        </form>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

===== C:\anime-site\app\destaques\page.tsx =====
'use client';

import { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import Link from 'next/link';
import Image from 'next/image';
import { Flame, Star, Loader2, Sparkles, TrendingUp, PlayCircle, ChevronRight, ChevronLeft } from 'lucide-react';
import { EspacoAnuncio } from '@/components/layout/EspacoAnuncio'; // <-- IMPORT DO ANÚNCIO

export default function DestaquesPage() {
  const [data, setData] = useState<{ doDia: any[], daSemana: any[], daTemporada: any[] } | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

  const carrosselDiaRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const carrosselTemporadaRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    const fetchDestaques = async () => {
      try {
        const res = await fetch('/api/destaques');
        if (!res.ok) throw new Error('Erro na API');
        const json = await res.json();
        setData(json);
      } catch (error) {
        console.error(error);
      } finally {
        setLoading(false);
      }
    };
    fetchDestaques();
  }, []);

  const scrollCarrossel = (ref: React.RefObject<HTMLDivElement | null>, direcao: 'esquerda' | 'direita') => {
    if (ref.current) {
      const scrollAmount = direcao === 'direita' ? 350 : -350;
      ref.current.scrollBy({ left: scrollAmount, behavior: 'smooth' });
    }
  };

  if (loading) {
    return <div className="flex justify-center items-center h-[50vh]"><Loader2 className="animate-spin text-accent" size={40} /></div>;
  }

  return (
    <div className="w-full pb-12">
      
      {/* ESPAÇO PARA ANÚNCIO NO TOPO */}
      <div className="mb-8">
        <EspacoAnuncio formato="banner" />
      </div>

      {/* 1. CARROSSEL DE DESTAQUES (EM ALTA) */}
      <section className="mb-12">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-4">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <Flame className="text-red-500" size={24} />
            <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold text-text">Em Alta (Destaques do Dia)</h2>
          </div>
          
          <div className="hidden md:flex items-center gap-2">
            <button onClick={() => scrollCarrossel(carrosselDiaRef, 'esquerda')} className="p-2 rounded-full bg-surface border border-border hover:bg-bg hover:text-accent transition-colors cursor-pointer">
              <ChevronLeft size={20} />
            </button>
            <button onClick={() => scrollCarrossel(carrosselDiaRef, 'direita')} className="p-2 rounded-full bg-surface border border-border hover:bg-bg hover:text-accent transition-colors cursor-pointer">
              <ChevronRight size={20} />
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
        
        <div 
          ref={carrosselDiaRef}
          className="flex overflow-x-auto gap-4 pb-4 snap-x snap-mandatory scrollbar-thin scrollbar-thumb-accent scrollbar-track-transparent"
        >
          {data?.doDia.map((anime, index) => (
            <Link 
              key={anime.id} 
              href={`/anime/${anime.id}`} 
              className="group flex-shrink-0 w-[160px] md:w-[200px] flex flex-col bg-surface rounded-xl overflow-hidden border border-border hover:border-accent transition-all snap-start"
            >
              <div className="relative aspect-[2/3] w-full">
                <Image src={anime.coverUrl} alt={anime.title} fill sizes="200px" className="object-cover group-hover:scale-110 transition-transform duration-300" />
                
                <div className="absolute top-2 left-2 bg-black/70 text-white font-bold px-2 py-1 rounded-md text-xs backdrop-blur-sm">
                  #{index + 1} em Alta
                </div>

                <div className="absolute top-2 right-2 bg-black/70 text-yellow-400 text-xs font-bold px-2 py-1 rounded-md flex items-center gap-1">
                  <Star size={12} fill="currentColor" /> {anime.score}
                </div>

                <div className="absolute bottom-2 left-2 bg-accent text-white text-xs font-bold px-2 py-1 rounded-md flex items-center gap-1">
                  <PlayCircle size={12} /> EP {anime.episodes}
                </div>
              </div>
              <div className="p-3">
                <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-text line-clamp-2 group-hover:text-accent transition-colors">{anime.title}</h3>
              </div>
            </Link>
          ))}
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* 2. TOP 10 RANKING (LISTA VERTICAL EM 2 COLUNAS) */}
      <section className="mb-12">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-4">
          <TrendingUp className="text-accent" size={24} />
          <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold text-text">Top 10 Lançamentos da Semana</h2>
        </div>
        
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 md:grid-flow-col md:grid-rows-5 gap-3">
          {data?.daSemana.slice(0, 10).map((anime, index) => (
            <Link 
              key={anime.id} 
              href={`/anime/${anime.id}`} 
              className="flex items-center gap-3 bg-surface border border-border p-2 md:p-3 rounded-xl hover:border-accent transition-colors group"
            >
              <div className="text-4xl md:text-5xl font-black text-border group-hover:text-accent/50 transition-colors w-10 text-center flex-shrink-0">
                {index + 1}
              </div>

              <div className="relative w-12 h-16 md:w-14 md:h-20 flex-shrink-0 rounded-md overflow-hidden">
                <Image src={anime.coverUrl} alt={anime.title} fill className="object-cover" />
              </div>

              <div className="flex flex-col flex-1 min-w-0">
                <h3 className="text-text font-bold text-sm line-clamp-2 group-hover:text-accent transition-colors mb-1">{anime.title}</h3>
                <p className="text-muted text-xs flex items-center gap-2">
                  <span className="text-yellow-500 font-bold"><Star size={12} className="inline mr-1" fill="currentColor"/>{anime.score}</span>
                  • EP {anime.episodes}
                </p>
              </div>
            </Link>
          ))}
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* 3. DESTAQUES DA TEMPORADA */}
      <section>
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-4">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <Sparkles className="text-accent" size={24} />
            <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold text-text">Destaques da Temporada</h2>
          </div>

          <div className="hidden md:flex items-center gap-2">
            <button onClick={() => scrollCarrossel(carrosselTemporadaRef, 'esquerda')} className="p-2 rounded-full bg-surface border border-border hover:bg-bg hover:text-accent transition-colors cursor-pointer">
              <ChevronLeft size={20} />
            </button>
            <button onClick={() => scrollCarrossel(carrosselTemporadaRef, 'direita')} className="p-2 rounded-full bg-surface border border-border hover:bg-bg hover:text-accent transition-colors cursor-pointer">
              <ChevronRight size={20} />
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
        
        <div 
          ref={carrosselTemporadaRef}
          className="flex overflow-x-auto gap-4 pb-4 snap-x snap-mandatory scrollbar-thin scrollbar-thumb-accent scrollbar-track-transparent"
        >
          {data?.daTemporada.map((anime) => (
            <Link key={anime.id} href={`/anime/${anime.id}`} className="group flex-shrink-0 w-[160px] md:w-[200px] flex flex-col bg-surface rounded-xl overflow-hidden border border-border hover:border-accent transition-all snap-start">
              <div className="relative aspect-[2/3] w-full">
                <Image src={anime.coverUrl} alt={anime.title} fill sizes="200px" className="object-cover group-hover:scale-110 transition-transform duration-300" />
                <div className="absolute top-2 right-2 bg-black/70 text-yellow-400 text-xs font-bold px-2 py-1 rounded-md flex items-center gap-1">
                  <Star size={12} fill="currentColor" /> {anime.score}
                </div>
              </div>
              <div className="p-3">
                <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-text line-clamp-2 group-hover:text-accent transition-colors">{anime.title}</h3>
              </div>
            </Link>
          ))}
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* ESPAÇO PARA ANÚNCIO NO FINAL */}
      <div className="mt-10">
        <EspacoAnuncio formato="banner" />
      </div>

    </div>
  );
}

===== C:\anime-site\app\esqueceu-senha\page.tsx =====
'use client';

import { useState } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation'; // <-- IMPORT DO ROUTER
import { createClient } from '@/lib/supabase/client';
import { Loader2, Mail, ArrowLeft } from 'lucide-react';
import Link from 'next/link';

export default function EsqueceuSenhaPage() {
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [mensagem, setMensagem] = useState('');
  const [erro, setErro] = useState('');

  const router = useRouter(); // <-- INICIALIZA O ROUTER
  const supabase = createClient();

  const handleRecuperar = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setLoading(true);
    setMensagem('');
    setErro('');

    // Garante que a URL será a correta (localhost ou produção)
    const origin = window.location.origin;
    const isLocalhost = origin.includes('localhost');
    const baseUrl = isLocalhost ? origin : 'https://www.aniverse.com.br';
    const redirectUrl = `${baseUrl}/redefinir-senha`;

    const { error } = await supabase.auth.resetPasswordForEmail(email, {
      redirectTo: redirectUrl,
    });

    if (error) {
      setErro('Erro ao enviar o e-mail. Verifique o endereço digitado.');
      setLoading(false);
    } else {
      setMensagem('Enviamos um código de recuperação para o seu e-mail!');
      
      // REDIRECIONA PARA A PÁGINA DE VALIDAÇÃO APÓS 2 SEGUNDOS
      setTimeout(() => {
        router.push('/redefinir-senha');
      }, 2000);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center min-h-[80vh] px-4">
      <div className="w-full max-w-md bg-surface border border-border p-8 rounded-2xl shadow-lg">
        <Link href="/login" className="text-muted hover:text-text flex items-center gap-1 text-sm mb-6 transition-colors">
          <ArrowLeft size={16} /> Voltar para o login
        </Link>

        <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-text mb-2">Recuperar Senha</h1>
        <p className="text-muted text-sm mb-6">Digite o e-mail cadastrado na sua conta para receber o código de redefinição.</p>

        {erro && <div className="bg-red-500/10 text-red-500 p-3 rounded-lg text-sm mb-4">{erro}</div>}
        {mensagem && <div className="bg-green-500/10 text-green-500 p-3 rounded-lg text-sm mb-4">{mensagem}</div>}

        <form onSubmit={handleRecuperar} className="flex flex-col gap-4">
          <div className="relative">
            <Mail className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-muted" size={20} />
            <input
              type="email"
              required
              placeholder="Seu e-mail"
              value={email}
              onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
              className="w-full bg-bg border border-border text-text rounded-lg py-3 pl-10 pr-4 focus:outline-none focus:border-accent transition-colors"
            />
          </div>
          
          <button
            type="submit"
            disabled={loading || mensagem !== ''}
            className="bg-accent text-white font-bold py-3 rounded-lg hover:bg-accent/90 transition-colors disabled:opacity-50 flex justify-center items-center gap-2"
          >
            {loading ? <Loader2 className="animate-spin" size={20} /> : 'Enviar Código de Recuperação'}
          </button>
        </form>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

===== C:\anime-site\app\exclusao-de-dados\page.tsx =====
import Link from 'next/link';
import { ArrowLeft, Trash2, ShieldAlert } from 'lucide-react';

export default function ExclusaoDadosPage() {
  return (
    <div className="w-full max-w-3xl mx-auto py-12 px-4">
      <Link href="/" className="text-muted hover:text-accent flex items-center gap-2 mb-8 transition-colors">
        <ArrowLeft size={16} /> Voltar para a Home
      </Link>

      <div className="flex items-center gap-3 mb-6">
        <ShieldAlert className="text-accent" size={32} />
        <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold text-text">Exclusão de Dados do Usuário</h1>
      </div>

      <div className="prose prose-invert max-w-none text-text space-y-6">
        <p className="text-muted">
          No <strong>AniVerse</strong>, levamos a sua privacidade a sério. Se você deseja excluir permanentemente a sua conta e todos os dados associados a ela, você pode fazer isso de duas formas:
        </p>

        <div className="bg-surface border border-border p-6 rounded-xl">
          <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-text mb-3 flex items-center gap-2">

Ao confirmar, todos os seus dados (animes salvos, Top 10, comentários, curtidas e notificações) serão apagados imediatamente dos nossos servidores.
          </p>

        <div className="bg-surface border border-border p-6 rounded-xl">

          <ul className="list-disc pl-5 text-muted space-y-2">
            <li>Seu perfil público (nome de usuário, avatar e bio).</li>
            <li>Sua lista de animes (Assistindo, Quero Ver, Concluído).</li>

          </ul>
