===== C:\anime-site\app\feed\page.tsx =====
'use client';

import { useState, useEffect, useOptimistic, startTransition } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { createClient } from '@/lib/supabase/client';
import { Loader2, Sparkles, Heart, MessageCircle, AlertTriangle, X, Trash2, PlusCircle, Share2 } from 'lucide-react';
import Image from 'next/image';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { CarrosselImagens } from '@/components/feed/CarrosselImagens';
import { ModalComentarios } from '@/components/feed/ModalComentarios';
import { toggleLike } from '@/app/actions/feed';
import { deletarPost } from '@/app/actions/posts';
import { SeloAdmin } from '@/components/layout/SeloAdmin';
import { TopCriadores } from '@/components/feed/TopCriadores';
import { CriadorPost } from '@/components/feed/CriadorPost';
import { EspacoAnuncio } from '@/components/layout/EspacoAnuncio';
import { TelaBloqueio } from '@/components/layout/TelaBloqueio'; // <-- IMPORT

const supabase = createClient();

interface Post {
  id: string;
  user_id: string;
  media_url: string | null;
  media_urls: string[] | null;
  media_type: string;
  caption: string | null;
  has_spoiler: boolean;
  created_at: string;
  is_ranking: boolean;
  profiles: { username: string; avatar_url: string; is_admin: boolean } | null;
  likes_count: number;
  comments_count: number;
  user_has_liked: boolean;
}

export default function FeedPage() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [posts, setPosts] = useState<Post[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [feedBloqueado, setFeedBloqueado] = useState(false);
  const [imagemAberta, setImagemAberta] = useState<Post | null>(null);
  const [postComentariosAberto, setPostComentariosAberto] = useState<Post | null>(null);
  const [currentUserId, setCurrentUserId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [isAdmin, setIsAdmin] = useState(false);
  const [criadorAberto, setCriadorAberto] = useState(false);

  const getImagensOrdenadas = (post: Post) => {
    if (!post.media_urls || post.media_urls.length === 0) {
      return post.media_url ? [post.media_url] : [];
    }
    return post.media_urls; 
  };

  const [optimisticLikes, addOptimisticLike] = useOptimistic<
    Post[],
    { postId: string; isLiked: boolean }
  >(
    posts,
    (state, { postId, isLiked }) => {
      return state.map((p) => {
        if (p.id === postId) {
          return {
            ...p,
            user_has_liked: !isLiked,
            likes_count: isLiked ? p.likes_count - 1 : p.likes_count + 1,
          };
        }
        return p;
      });
    }
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    const checkAndFetch = async () => {
      const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
      if (!user) {
        router.push('/login');
        return;
      }

      const { data: config } = await supabase.from('site_config').select('feed_enabled').single();
      if (config?.feed_enabled === false) {
        setFeedBloqueado(true);
        setLoading(false);
        return;
      }

      setCurrentUserId(user.id);
      const { data: profile } = await supabase.from('profiles').select('is_admin').eq('id', user.id).single();
      if (profile?.is_admin) setIsAdmin(true);

      const { data: postsData } = await supabase
        .from('posts')
        .select(`
          id, user_id, media_url, media_urls, media_type, caption, has_spoiler, created_at, is_ranking,
          profiles (username, avatar_url, is_admin)
        `)
        .order('created_at', { ascending: false });

      if (postsData) {
        const postsComContagem = await Promise.all(
          postsData.map(async (post) => {
            const { count: likes } = await supabase.from('post_likes').select('*', { count: 'exact', head: true }).eq('post_id', post.id);
            const { count: comments } = await supabase.from('post_comments').select('*', { count: 'exact', head: true }).eq('post_id', post.id);
            
            let user_has_liked = false;
            if (user) {
              const { data: like } = await supabase.from('post_likes').select('id').eq('post_id', post.id).eq('user_id', user.id).maybeSingle();
              user_has_liked = !!like;
            }

            return {
              ...post,
              likes_count: likes || 0,
              comments_count: comments || 0,
              user_has_liked
            };
          })
        );
        setPosts(postsComContagem as unknown as Post[]);
      }
      setLoading(false);
    };

    checkAndFetch();
  }, [supabase, router]);

  const handleLike = (post: Post) => {
    startTransition(async () => {
      addOptimisticLike({ postId: post.id, isLiked: post.user_has_liked });
      await toggleLike(post.id, post.user_has_liked);
      setPosts((prev) => 
        prev.map((p) => 
          p.id === post.id 
            ? { ...p, user_has_liked: !post.user_has_liked, likes_count: post.user_has_liked ? p.likes_count - 1 : p.likes_count + 1 } 
            : p
        )
      );
    });
  };

  const handleDeletar = async (post: Post) => {
    if (!confirm('Tem certeza que deseja deletar este post?')) return;

    try {
      await deletarPost(post.id, post.media_urls || []);
      setPosts((prev) => prev.filter((p) => p.id !== post.id));
    } catch (error: any) {
      alert(error.message || 'Erro ao deletar o post.');
    }
  };

  const handleCompartilhar = (postId: string) => {
    const url = `${window.location.origin}/feed/${postId}`;
    navigator.clipboard.writeText(url);
    alert('Link copiado para compartilhar no WhatsApp!');
  };

  if (loading) {
    return <div className="flex justify-center items-center h-[50vh]"><Loader2 className="animate-spin text-accent" size={40} /></div>;
  }

  if (feedBloqueado) {
    return (
      <TelaBloqueio 
        titulo="Feed em Manutenção"
        descricao="Estamos organizando a comunidade do AniVerse para você. O Feed será liberado em breve!"
        spoiler={
          <div className="text-sm text-text">
            <p>Em breve você poderá:</p>
            <ul className="list-disc list-inside text-left mt-2 space-y-1 text-muted">
              <li>Postar seu Top 10 com temas personalizados.</li>
              <li>Interagir com outros fãs no Feed.</li>
              <li>Concorrer ao título de "Top Criador".</li>
            </ul>
          </div>
        }
      />
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="w-full max-w-6xl mx-auto py-8 px-4">
      
      {imagemAberta && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-[200] bg-black/90 flex items-center justify-center p-4 backdrop-blur-sm overflow-y-auto" onClick={() => setImagemAberta(null)}>
          <button className="absolute top-4 right-4 text-white hover:text-red-400 transition-colors cursor-pointer z-10" onClick={() => setImagemAberta(null)}><X size={32} /></button>
          <div className="w-full max-w-md bg-surface rounded-lg overflow-hidden" onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}>
            <CarrosselImagens imagens={getImagensOrdenadas(imagemAberta)} isRanking={imagemAberta.is_ranking} />
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {postComentariosAberto && (
        <ModalComentarios post={postComentariosAberto} onClose={() => setPostComentariosAberto(null)} />
      )}

      {criadorAberto && (
        <CriadorPost onClose={() => setCriadorAberto(false)} />
      )}

      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-12 gap-8 items-start">
        
        <div className="hidden lg:block lg:col-span-2"></div>

        <div className="lg:col-span-6 w-full max-w-md mx-auto lg:mx-0">
          
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-6 w-full">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
              <Sparkles className="text-accent" size={32} />
              <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold text-text">Feed</h1>
            </div>
            <button 
              onClick={() => setCriadorAberto(true)}
              className="bg-accent text-white p-2 rounded-lg hover:bg-accent/90 transition-colors cursor-pointer shadow-lg shadow-accent/20"
              title="Criar novo post"
            >
              <PlusCircle size={24} />
            </button>
          </div>

          <div className="mb-6">
            <EspacoAnuncio formato="banner" />
          </div>

          {optimisticLikes.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="text-center py-20 border-2 border-dashed border-border rounded-2xl">
              <p className="text-muted mb-4">O Feed está vazio. Seja o primeiro a postar!</p>
              <Link href="/top10" className="bg-accent text-white font-bold px-6 py-3 rounded-lg hover:bg-accent/90 transition-all">
                Criar meu Top 10
              </Link>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="flex flex-col gap-6">
              {optimisticLikes.map((post) => (
                <div key={post.id} className="bg-surface border border-border rounded-xl overflow-hidden shadow-sm hover:border-accent transition-colors w-full">

                  <div className="flex items-center gap-3 p-3">
                    <Link href={`/perfil/${post.profiles?.username}`} className="relative w-8 h-8 rounded-full overflow-hidden bg-border shrink-0 hover:ring-2 hover:ring-accent transition-all">
                      {post.profiles?.avatar_url ? (
                        <Image src={post.profiles.avatar_url} alt="Avatar" fill className="object-cover" />
                      ) : (
                        <div className="w-full h-full flex items-center justify-center text-xs font-bold">{post.profiles?.username?.charAt(0).toUpperCase() || '?'}</div>
                      )}
                    </Link>
                    <div className="flex-1">
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                        <Link 
                          href={`/perfil/${post.profiles?.username}`} 
                          className="text-sm font-bold text-text hover:text-accent hover:underline transition-colors"
                        >
                          {post.profiles?.username || 'Usuário'}
                        </Link>
                        {post.profiles?.is_admin && <SeloAdmin />}
                      </div>
                    </div>

                    <div className="flex items-center gap-1">
                      <button 
                        onClick={() => handleCompartilhar(post.id)}
                        className="text-muted hover:text-accent transition-colors p-1 cursor-pointer"
                        title="Compartilhar post"
                      >
                        <Share2 size={18} />
                      </button>

                      {(post.user_id === currentUserId || isAdmin) && (
                        <button 
                          onClick={() => handleDeletar(post)}
                          className="text-muted hover:text-red-500 transition-colors p-1 cursor-pointer"
                          title="Deletar post"
                        >
                          <Trash2 size={18} />
                        </button>
                      )}
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  {getImagensOrdenadas(post).length > 0 ? (
                    <div className="cursor-pointer relative" onClick={() => setImagemAberta(post)}>
                      <CarrosselImagens imagens={getImagensOrdenadas(post)} isRanking={post.is_ranking} />
                      {post.has_spoiler && (
                        <div className="absolute top-2 right-2 bg-red-500 text-white text-xs font-bold px-2 py-1 rounded-md flex items-center gap-1 z-10">
                          <AlertTriangle size={12} /> Spoiler
                        </div>
                      )}
                    </div>
                  ) : (
                    <div className="px-4 py-2">
                      <p className="text-text text-lg whitespace-pre-wrap">{post.caption}</p>
                    </div>
                  )}

                  <div className="p-3">
                    {post.caption && getImagensOrdenadas(post).length > 0 && <p className="text-sm text-text mb-3 line-clamp-2">{post.caption}</p>}
                    
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-4 text-muted text-sm">
                      <button onClick={() => handleLike(post)} className={`flex items-center gap-1 transition-colors cursor-pointer ${post.user_has_liked ? 'text-red-500' : 'hover:text-red-500'}`}>
                        <Heart size={18} fill={post.user_has_liked ? "currentColor" : "none"} /> {post.likes_count}
                      </button>
                      <button onClick={() => setPostComentariosAberto(post)} className="flex items-center gap-1 hover:text-blue-500 transition-colors cursor-pointer">
                        <MessageCircle size={18} /> {post.comments_count}
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        <div className="lg:col-span-4 w-full max-w-md mx-auto lg:mx-0 lg:sticky lg:top-24">
          <TopCriadores />
          <div className="mt-6">
            <EspacoAnuncio formato="vertical" />
          </div>
        </div>
        
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

===== C:\anime-site\app\feed\[id]\opengraph-image.tsx =====
import { ImageResponse } from 'next/og';
import { createClient } from '@/lib/supabase/server';

export const runtime = 'edge';
export const alt = 'Post do AniVerse';
export const size = { width: 1200, height: 630 };
export const contentType = 'image/png';

export default async function Image({ params }: { params: { id: string } }) {
  const supabase = await createClient();
  
  // Busca o post no banco para pegar as imagens e a legenda
  const { data: post } = await supabase
    .from('posts')
    .select('media_urls, caption, profiles(username)')
    .eq('id', params.id)
    .single();

  const capa = post?.media_urls?.[0] || 'https://aniverse.com.br/fallback.jpg';
  const legenda = post?.caption || 'Confira este post no AniVerse!';
  const autor = post?.profiles?.[0]?.username || 'AniVerse';

  return new ImageResponse(
    (
      <div
        style={{
          width: '100%',
          height: '100%',
          display: 'flex',
          flexDirection: 'column',
          alignItems: 'center',
          justifyContent: 'center',
          backgroundColor: '#0a0a0f',
          position: 'relative',
        }}
      >
        {/* Imagem de Fundo */}
        <img
          src={capa}
          alt="Capa"
          style={{
            position: 'absolute',
            top: 0,
            left: 0,
            width: '100%',
            height: '100%',
            objectFit: 'cover',
            opacity: 0.4,
            filter: 'blur(10px)',
          }}
        />
        
        {/* Overlay */}
        <div
          style={{
            position: 'absolute',
            top: 0,
            left: 0,
            width: '100%',
            height: '100%',
            background: 'linear-gradient(to bottom, transparent, #0a0a0f)',
          }}
        />

        {/* Logo do AniVerse */}
        <div style={{ position: 'absolute', top: 40, left: 40, display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 10 }}>
          <div style={{ width: 40, height: 40, borderRadius: '50%', border: '4px solid #9333ea', display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' }}>
            <span style={{ color: '#9333ea', fontSize: 24, fontWeight: 'bold' }}>A</span>
          </div>
          <span style={{ color: 'white', fontSize: 28, fontWeight: 'bold' }}>ANIVERSE</span>
        </div>

        {/* Conteúdo do Post */}
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', zIndex: 10, padding: 40, textAlign: 'center' }}>
          <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 20, marginBottom: 30 }}>
             {/* Mostra as 3 primeiras capas como destaque */}
            {post?.media_urls?.slice(0, 3).map((img: string, i: number) => (
              <img
                key={i}
                src={img}
                alt={`Capa ${i + 1}`}
                style={{
                  width: 200,
                  height: 300,
                  objectFit: 'cover',
                  borderRadius: 12,
                  border: '2px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.1)',
                  boxShadow: '0 10px 30px rgba(0,0,0,0.5)',
                }}
              />
            ))}
          </div>
          <h1 style={{ color: 'white', fontSize: 40, fontWeight: 'bold', marginBottom: 10, lineHeight: 1.2 }}>
            {legenda.substring(0, 80)}{legenda.length > 80 ? '...' : ''}
          </h1>
          <p style={{ color: '#a1a1aa', fontSize: 24 }}>@{autor}</p>
        </div>
      </div>
    ),
    {
      ...size,
    }
  );
}

===== C:\anime-site\app\feed\[id]\page.tsx =====
import { createClient } from '@/lib/supabase/server';
import { notFound } from 'next/navigation';
import { CarrosselImagens } from '@/components/feed/CarrosselImagens';
import Image from 'next/image';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { ArrowLeft } from 'lucide-react';

export default async function PostPage({ params }: { params: { id: string } }) {
  const supabase = await createClient();
  
  const { data: post } = await supabase
    .from('posts')
    .select('*, profiles(username, avatar_url)')
    .eq('id', params.id)
    .single();

  if (!post) return notFound();

  return (
    <div className="w-full max-w-2xl mx-auto py-8 px-4">
      <Link href="/feed" className="flex items-center gap-2 text-muted hover:text-accent mb-6">
        <ArrowLeft size={20} /> Voltar para o Feed
      </Link>
      
      <div className="bg-surface border border-border rounded-2xl overflow-hidden shadow-lg">
        {/* Header */}
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3 p-4 border-b border-border">
          <div className="relative w-10 h-10 rounded-full overflow-hidden bg-border">
            {post.profiles?.avatar_url ? (
              <Image src={post.profiles.avatar_url} alt="Avatar" fill className="object-cover" />
            ) : (
              <div className="w-full h-full flex items-center justify-center text-sm font-bold">{post.profiles?.username?.charAt(0).toUpperCase()}</div>
            )}
          </div>
          <div>
            <Link href={`/perfil/${post.profiles?.username}`} className="font-bold text-text hover:text-accent">
              {post.profiles?.username}
            </Link>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Carrossel */}
        <CarrosselImagens imagens={post.media_urls || (post.media_url ? [post.media_url] : [])} isRanking={post.is_ranking} />

        {/* Legenda */}
        {post.caption && (
          <div className="p-4">
            <p className="text-text">{post.caption}</p>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

===== C:\anime-site\app\login\page.tsx =====
'use client';

import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { createClient } from '@/lib/supabase/client';
import { Mail, Lock, Loader2, ArrowLeft, Eye, EyeOff, User, CheckCircle2, AlertCircle } from 'lucide-react';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { SeletorAvatares } from '@/components/perfil/SelecionarAvatar';

export default function LoginPage() {
  const [isLogin, setIsLogin] = useState(true);
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
  const [username, setUsername] = useState('');
  const [avatarUrl, setAvatarUrl] = useState(''); 
  
  const [showPassword, setShowPassword] = useState(false);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [socialLoading, setSocialLoading] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  
  const [usernameStatus, setUsernameStatus] = useState<'idle' | 'checking' | 'available' | 'taken'>('idle');
  
  const router = useRouter();
  const supabase = createClient();

  const dominiosPermitidos = ['gmail.com', 'hotmail.com', 'outlook.com', 'yahoo.com', 'icloud.com', 'bol.com.br', 'uol.com.br'];

  useEffect(() => {
    if (isLogin) {
      setUsernameStatus('idle');
      return;
    }

    if (username.trim().length < 3) {
      setUsernameStatus('idle');
      return;
    }

    setUsernameStatus('checking');

    const timer = setTimeout(async () => {
      const { data, error } = await supabase
        .from('profiles')
        .select('username')
        .ilike('username', username.trim())
        .maybeSingle();

      if (error) {
        setUsernameStatus('idle');
        return;
      }

      if (data) {
        setUsernameStatus('taken');
      } else {
        setUsernameStatus('available');
      }
    }, 500);

    return () => clearTimeout(timer);
  }, [username, isLogin, supabase]);

  const handleAuth = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setLoading(true);
    setError(null);

    const emailRegex = /^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/;
    if (!emailRegex.test(email)) {
      setError('Por favor, insira um e-mail válido.');
      setLoading(false);
      return;
    }

    const dominio = email.split('@')[1].toLowerCase();
    if (!dominiosPermitidos.includes(dominio)) {
      setError('Use um e-mail de um provedor conhecido (Gmail, Hotmail, etc).');
      setLoading(false);
      return;
    }

    if (!isLogin) {
      if (password.length < 6) {
        setError('A senha deve ter no mínimo 6 caracteres.');
        setLoading(false);
        return;
      }
      const senhasFaceis = ['123456', 'abcdef', 'qwerty', '12345678', 'senha123'];
      if (senhasFaceis.includes(password.toLowerCase())) {
        setError('Sua senha é muito fácil. Escolha outra.');
        setLoading(false);
        return;
      }
      if (!/(?=.*[a-zA-Z])(?=.*\d)/.test(password)) {
        setError('A senha deve conter pelo menos uma letra e um número.');
        setLoading(false);
        return;
      }
      if (username.trim().length < 3) {
        setError('O nome de usuário deve ter pelo menos 3 letras.');
        setLoading(false);
        return;
      }
      if (usernameStatus === 'taken') {
        setError('Este nome de usuário já está em uso. Escolha outro.');
        setLoading(false);
        return;
      }
      if (!avatarUrl) {
        setError('Escolha um avatar para continuar.');
        setLoading(false);
        return;
      }
    }

    try {
      if (isLogin) {
        const { error } = await supabase.auth.signInWithPassword({ email, password });
        if (error) throw error;
      } else {
        const { data: authData, error: authError } = await supabase.auth.signUp({ 
          email, 
          password, 
          options: { data: { username: username.trim().toLowerCase(), avatar_url: avatarUrl } } 
        });
        if (authError) throw authError;

        if (authData.user) {
          await supabase.from('profiles').update({ avatar_url: avatarUrl }).eq('id', authData.user.id);
        }
      }
      
      window.location.href = '/';
 
    } catch (err: any) {
      if (err.message.includes('Invalid login credentials')) setError('E-mail ou senha incorretos.');
      else if (err.message.includes('User already registered')) setError('Este e-mail já está cadastrado.');
      else setError(err.message || 'Ocorreu um erro.');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const handleSocialLogin = async (provider: 'google') => {
    setSocialLoading(provider);
    setError(null);

    const { error } = await supabase.auth.signInWithOAuth({
      provider: provider,
      options: {
        redirectTo: `${window.location.origin}/auth/callback`,
      },
    });

    if (error) {
      setError(`Erro ao entrar com ${provider}. Tente novamente.`);
      setSocialLoading(null);
    }
  };

  const isSubmitDisabled = loading || (!isLogin && (username.trim().length < 3 || usernameStatus === 'taken' || usernameStatus === 'checking' || !avatarUrl));

  return (
    <div className="min-h-[80vh] flex items-center justify-center px-4 relative overflow-hidden bg-bg">
      
      {/* FUNDO ESTILIZADO SUTIL (Apenas Gradiente, sem grid) */}
      <div className="absolute inset-0 z-0 opacity-10 pointer-events-none">
        <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-br from-accent/20 via-transparent to-accent/10" />
      </div>

      <div key={isLogin ? 'login' : 'cadastro'} className="w-full max-w-md bg-surface/80 backdrop-blur-xl border border-border rounded-2xl p-8 shadow-2xl relative z-10">
        
        <div className="absolute top-0 left-0 w-full h-1 bg-gradient-to-r from-transparent via-accent to-transparent opacity-50" />

        <Link href="/" className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 text-muted hover:text-accent transition-colors mb-6 text-sm font-medium cursor-pointer">
          <ArrowLeft size={16} /> Voltar para a Home
        </Link>

        <div className="text-center mb-8">
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-text mb-2">
            {isLogin ? 'Bem-vindo de volta' : 'Crie sua conta'}
          </h1>
          <p className="text-muted text-sm">
            {isLogin ? 'Entre para salvar seus animes e notas.' : 'Junte-se ao AniVerse e monte seu Top 10.'}
          </p>
        </div>

        {error && (
          <div className="bg-red-500/10 border border-red-500/50 text-red-400 text-sm p-3 rounded-lg mb-6">
            {error}
          </div>
        )}

        <form onSubmit={handleAuth} className="flex flex-col gap-4">
          {!isLogin && (
            <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
              <div className="relative">
                <User className={`absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 ${
                  usernameStatus === 'taken' ? 'text-red-500' : 
                  usernameStatus === 'available' ? 'text-green-500' : 'text-muted'
                }`} size={18} />
                <input
                  type="text" 
                  placeholder="Seu nome de usuário (único)" 
                  value={username} 
                  onChange={(e) => setUsername(e.target.value)} 
                  required={!isLogin}
                  className={`w-full bg-transparent border rounded-lg py-3 pl-10 pr-10 focus:outline-none transition-all ${
                    usernameStatus === 'taken' ? 'border-red-500 focus:ring-red-500' : 
                    usernameStatus === 'available' ? 'border-green-500 focus:ring-green-500' : 
                    'border-border focus:border-accent focus:ring-accent'
                  }`}
                />
                <div className="absolute right-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2">
                  {usernameStatus === 'checking' && <Loader2 className="animate-spin text-muted" size={18} />}
                  {usernameStatus === 'available' && <CheckCircle2 className="text-green-500" size={18} />}
                  {usernameStatus === 'taken' && <AlertCircle className="text-red-500" size={18} />}
                </div>
              </div>
              
              {usernameStatus === 'taken' && (
                <span className="text-xs text-red-500 font-medium ml-1">Este nome já está em uso. Escolha outro.</span>
              )}
              {usernameStatus === 'available' && (
                <span className="text-xs text-green-500 font-medium ml-1">Nome de usuário disponível!</span>
              )}
            </div>
          )}

          <div className="relative">
            <Mail className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-muted" size={18} />
            <input
              type="email" placeholder="Seu e-mail" value={email} onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)} required
              className="w-full bg-transparent border border-border text-text rounded-lg py-3 pl-10 pr-4 focus:outline-none focus:border-accent focus:ring-1 focus:ring-accent transition-all"
            />
          </div>

          <div className="relative">
            <Lock className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-muted" size={18} />
            <input
              type={showPassword ? 'text' : 'password'} placeholder="Sua senha" value={password} onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)} required
              className="w-full bg-transparent border border-border text-text rounded-lg py-3 pl-10 pr-12 focus:outline-none focus:border-accent focus:ring-1 focus:ring-accent transition-all"
            />
            <button type="button" onClick={() => setShowPassword(!showPassword)} className="absolute right-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-muted hover:text-accent transition-colors cursor-pointer">
              {showPassword ? <EyeOff size={18} /> : <Eye size={18} />}
            </button>
          </div>

          {isLogin && (
            <div className="flex justify-end mt-[-8px]">
              <Link href="/esqueceu-senha" className="text-sm text-accent hover:underline">
                Esqueceu a senha?
              </Link>
            </div>
          )}

          {!isLogin && (
            <div className="mt-2">
              <label className="text-sm font-semibold text-text mb-2 block">Escolha seu avatar</label>
              <SeletorAvatares selectedAvatar={avatarUrl} onSelect={setAvatarUrl} />
            </div>
          )}

          <button 
            type="submit" 
            disabled={isSubmitDisabled} 
            className="w-full bg-accent text-white font-bold py-3 rounded-lg hover:bg-accent/90 transition-all mt-2 flex items-center justify-center gap-2 disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed cursor-pointer"
          >
            {loading ? <Loader2 className="animate-spin" size={20} /> : (isLogin ? 'Entrar' : 'Criar Conta')}
          </button>
        </form>

        <div className="relative my-6">
          <div className="absolute inset-0 flex items-center"><div className="w-full border-t border-border"></div></div>
          <div className="relative flex justify-center text-xs uppercase"><span className="bg-surface px-2 text-muted">Ou continue com</span></div>
        </div>

        <div className="flex">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => handleSocialLogin('google')}
            disabled={!!socialLoading}
            className="flex-1 flex items-center justify-center gap-3 bg-transparent border border-border text-text py-3 rounded-lg hover:border-accent transition-colors cursor-pointer font-medium text-sm disabled:opacity-50"
          >
            {socialLoading === 'google' ? <Loader2 className="animate-spin" size={18} /> : (
              <svg className="w-5 h-5" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                <path fill="#4285F4" d="M22.56 12.25c0-.78-.07-1.53-.2-2.25H12v4.26h5.92c-.26 1.37-1.04 2.53-2.21 3.31v2.77h3.57c2.08-1.92 3.28-4.74 3.28-8.09z"/>
                <path fill="#34A853" d="M12 23c2.97 0 5.46-.98 7.28-2.66l-3.57-2.77c-.98.66-2.23 1.06-3.71 1.06-2.86 0-5.29-1.93-6.16-4.53H2.18v2.84C3.99 20.53 7.7 23 12 23z"/>
                <path fill="#FBBC05" d="M5.84 14.09c-.22-.66-.35-1.36-.35-2.09s.13-1.43.35-2.09V7.07H2.18C1.43 8.55 1 10.22 1 12s.43 3.45 1.18 4.93l2.85-2.22.81-.62z"/>
                <path fill="#EA4335" d="M12 5.38c1.62 0 3.06.56 4.21 1.64l3.15-3.15C17.45 2.09 14.97 1 12 1 7.7 1 3.99 3.47 2.18 7.07l3.66 2.84c.87-2.6 3.3-4.53 6.16-4.53z"/>
              </svg>
            )}
            Entrar com Google
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="mt-6 text-center text-sm text-muted">
          {isLogin ? 'Ainda não tem conta?' : 'Já tem uma conta?'}
          <button onClick={() => { setIsLogin(!isLogin); setError(null); setUsernameStatus('idle'); }} className="ml-2 text-accent font-bold hover:underline cursor-pointer">
            {isLogin ? 'Cadastre-se' : 'Faça Login'}
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

===== C:\anime-site\app\mensagens\ChatComunidade.tsx =====
'use client';

import { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { createClient } from '@/lib/supabase/client';
import { Loader2, Send, Lock, Trash2, Clock } from 'lucide-react';
import Image from 'next/image';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { formatDistanceToNow } from 'date-fns';
import { ptBR } from 'date-fns/locale';
import { SeloAdmin } from '@/components/layout/SeloAdmin';

interface Mensagem {
  id: string;
  content: string;
  created_at: string;
  user_id: string;
  profiles: {
    username: string;
    avatar_url: string;
    is_admin: boolean;
  };
}

export function ChatComunidade() {
  const [mensagens, setMensagens] = useState<Mensagem[]>([]);
  const [novaMensagem, setNovaMensagem] = useState('');
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [userId, setUserId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [isAdmin, setIsAdmin] = useState(false);
  const [bloqueado, setBloqueado] = useState(false);
  
  const bottomRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const supabase = createClient();

  useEffect(() => {
    const init = async () => {
      // 1. Verifica bloqueio
      const { data: config } = await supabase.from('site_config').select('chat_enabled').single();
      if (config?.chat_enabled === false) {
        setBloqueado(true);
        setLoading(false);
        return;
      }

      // 2. Pega usuário e verifica se é admin
      const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
      if (user) {
        setUserId(user.id);
        const { data: profile } = await supabase.from('profiles').select('is_admin').eq('id', user.id).single();
        if (profile?.is_admin) setIsAdmin(true);
      }

      // 3. Busca mensagens
      const { data } = await supabase
        .from('chat_messages')
        .select(`id, content, created_at, user_id, profiles (username, avatar_url, is_admin)`)
        .order('created_at', { ascending: true })
        .limit(50);

      if (data) setMensagens(data as unknown as Mensagem[]);
      setLoading(false);
    };

    init();

    // Realtime
    const channel = supabase
      .channel('chat-geral')
      .on(
        'postgres_changes',
        { event: 'INSERT', schema: 'public', table: 'chat_messages' },
        async (payload) => {
          if (bloqueado) return;

          const { data } = await supabase
            .from('chat_messages')
            .select(`id, content, created_at, user_id, profiles (username, avatar_url, is_admin)`)
            .eq('id', payload.new.id)
            .single();
            
          if (data) setMensagens((prev) => [...prev, data as unknown as Mensagem]);
        }
      )
      .subscribe();

    return () => { supabase.removeChannel(channel); };
  }, [supabase, bloqueado]);

  useEffect(() => {
    bottomRef.current?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth' });
  }, [mensagens]);

  const handleEnviar = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!novaMensagem.trim() || !userId || bloqueado) return;

    const msg = novaMensagem;
    setNovaMensagem('');

    await supabase.from('chat_messages').insert({ user_id: userId, content: msg });
  };

  // FUNÇÃO PARA DELETAR UMA MENSAGEM ESPECÍFICA
  const handleDeletarMensagem = async (id: string) => {
    if (!confirm('Tem certeza que deseja apagar esta mensagem?')) return;

    const { error } = await supabase.from('chat_messages').delete().eq('id', id);
    
    if (error) {
      alert('Erro ao apagar a mensagem.');
    } else {
      // Remove da tela na hora
      setMensagens((prev) => prev.filter((msg) => msg.id !== id));
    }
  };

  if (loading) {
    return <div className="flex justify-center py-10"><Loader2 className="animate-spin text-accent" size={32} /></div>;
  }

  if (bloqueado) {
    return (
      <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center min-h-[60vh] text-center px-4 bg-bg/20">
        <div className="bg-surface border border-border p-6 rounded-full mb-6">
          <Lock className="text-muted" size={48} />
        </div>
        <h3 className="text-2xl font-bold text-text mb-4">Chat em Manutenção</h3>
        <p className="text-muted max-w-md mx-auto mb-8">
          Estamos preparando a comunidade do AniVerse para você. O chat será liberado em breve!
        </p>
        <Link href="/" className="bg-accent text-white font-bold px-8 py-3 rounded-lg hover:bg-accent/90 transition-colors shadow-lg shadow-accent/20">
          Voltar para os Lançamentos
        </Link>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col h-[60vh] bg-bg/20 rounded-b-2xl">
      
      {/* AVISO DE LIMPEZA AUTOMÁTICA */}
      <div className="p-2 border-b border-border bg-surface/50 flex items-center justify-center gap-2 text-muted text-[10px]">
        <Clock size={12} />
        <span>As mensagens são apagadas automaticamente a cada 24h.</span>
      </div>

      <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-4 space-y-4">
        {mensagens.map((msg) => {
          const isMe = msg.user_id === userId;
          // Pode deletar se for a própria mensagem OU se for admin
          const podeDeletar = isMe || isAdmin;

          return (
            <div key={msg.id} className={`flex gap-3 group ${isMe ? 'flex-row-reverse' : ''}`}>
              <div className="relative w-8 h-8 rounded-full overflow-hidden bg-border shrink-0">
                {msg.profiles?.avatar_url ? (
                  <Image src={msg.profiles.avatar_url} alt="Avatar" fill sizes="32px" className="object-cover" />
                ) : (
                  <div className="w-full h-full flex items-center justify-center text-xs font-bold">{msg.profiles?.username?.charAt(0).toUpperCase()}</div>
                )}
              </div>
              <div className={`flex flex-col ${isMe ? 'items-end' : 'items-start'} max-w-[80%]`}>
                <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 mb-1">
                  <span className="text-xs font-bold text-muted">{msg.profiles?.username}</span>
                  {msg.profiles?.is_admin && <SeloAdmin />}
                </div>
                
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  {/* BOTÃO DE LIXEIRA (Aparece no hover) */}
                  {podeDeletar && (
                    <button 
                      onClick={() => handleDeletarMensagem(msg.id)}
                      className={`opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 transition-opacity text-muted hover:text-red-500 ${isMe ? 'order-first' : 'order-last'}`}
                      title="Apagar mensagem"
                    >
                      <Trash2 size={14} />
                    </button>
                  )}
                  
                  <div className={`p-3 rounded-2xl text-sm ${isMe ? 'bg-accent text-white rounded-tr-sm' : 'bg-surface text-text rounded-tl-sm border border-border'}`}>
                    {msg.content}
                  </div>
                </div>

                <span className="text-[10px] text-muted mt-1 px-1">
                  {formatDistanceToNow(new Date(msg.created_at), { addSuffix: true, locale: ptBR })}
                </span>
              </div>
            </div>
          );
        })}
        <div ref={bottomRef} />
      </div>

      <form onSubmit={handleEnviar} className="p-3 border-t border-border bg-surface flex gap-2 rounded-b-2xl">
        <input
          type="text"
          value={novaMensagem}
          onChange={(e) => setNovaMensagem(e.target.value)}
          placeholder="Digite no chat da comunidade..."
          disabled={bloqueado}
          className="flex-1 bg-bg border border-border rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm text-text focus:outline-none focus:border-accent transition-colors disabled:opacity-50"
        />
        <button 
          type="submit" 
          disabled={!novaMensagem.trim() || bloqueado} 
          className="bg-accent text-white p-3 rounded-xl disabled:opacity-50 hover:bg-accent/90 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
        >
          <Send size={20} />
        </button>
      </form>
    </div>
  );
}

===== C:\anime-site\app\mensagens\page.tsx =====
'use client';

import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation'; // <-- IMPORT DO ROUTER
import { createClient } from '@/lib/supabase/client';
import { MessageCircle, Users, Loader2, Lock } from 'lucide-react';
import Link from 'next/link';
import Image from 'next/image';
import { SeloAdmin } from '@/components/layout/SeloAdmin';
import { ChatComunidade } from './ChatComunidade';

export default function MensagensPage() {
  const router = useRouter(); // <-- INICIALIZA O ROUTER
  const [abaAtiva, setAbaAtiva] = useState<'comunidade' | 'privadas'>('comunidade');
  const [conversas, setConversas] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [chatBloqueado, setChatBloqueado] = useState(false);
  const supabase = createClient();

  useEffect(() => {
    const checkAndFetch = async () => {
      // 1. VERIFICA SE ESTÁ LOGADO
      const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
      if (!user) {
        router.push('/login');
        return;
      }

      // 2. Verifica se o chat está bloqueado
      const { data: config } = await supabase.from('site_config').select('chat_enabled').single();
      if (config?.chat_enabled === false) {
        setChatBloqueado(true);
        setLoading(false);
        return;
      }

      // 3. Busca as conversas privadas
      const { data: mensagens } = await supabase
        .from('direct_messages')
        .select(`
          id, sender_id, receiver_id, content, created_at, is_read,
          sender:profiles!direct_messages_sender_id_fkey (id, username, avatar_url, is_admin),
          receiver:profiles!direct_messages_receiver_id_fkey (id, username, avatar_url, is_admin)
        `)
        .or(`sender_id.eq.${user.id},receiver_id.eq.${user.id}`)
        .order('created_at', { ascending: false });

      if (mensagens) {
        const conversasMap = new Map();
        mensagens.forEach((msg: any) => {
          const isSender = msg.sender_id === user.id;
          const outroUsuario = isSender ? msg.receiver : msg.sender;
          
          if (!conversasMap.has(outroUsuario.id)) {
            conversasMap.set(outroUsuario.id, {
              usuario: outroUsuario,
              ultimaMensagem: msg.content,
              data: msg.created_at,
              naoLidas: (!isSender && !msg.is_read) ? 1 : 0
            });
          } else {
            if (!isSender && !msg.is_read) {
              const conversa = conversasMap.get(outroUsuario.id);
              conversa.naoLidas += 1;
            }
          }
        });
        setConversas(Array.from(conversasMap.values()));
      }
      setLoading(false);
    };
    checkAndFetch();
  }, [supabase, router]); // <-- ADICIONA ROUTER NAS DEPENDÊNCIAS

  if (loading) {
    return <div className="flex justify-center items-center h-[50vh]"><Loader2 className="animate-spin text-accent" size={40} /></div>;
  }

  if (chatBloqueado) {
    return (
      <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center min-h-[70vh] text-center px-4">
        <div className="bg-surface border border-border p-6 rounded-full mb-6">
          <Lock size={48} className="text-muted" />
        </div>
        <h2 className="text-3xl font-bold text-text mb-4">Chat em Manutenção</h2>
        <p className="text-muted max-w-md mx-auto mb-8">
          Estamos preparando a comunidade do AniVerse para você. O chat será liberado em breve!
        </p>
        <Link href="/" className="bg-accent text-white font-bold px-8 py-3 rounded-lg hover:bg-accent/90 transition-colors shadow-lg shadow-accent/20">
          Voltar para os Lançamentos
        </Link>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="w-full max-w-3xl mx-auto py-8">
      <div className="bg-surface border border-border rounded-2xl overflow-hidden shadow-lg">
        <div className="flex border-b border-border">
          <button
            onClick={() => setAbaAtiva('comunidade')}
            className={`flex-1 py-4 font-bold text-sm flex items-center justify-center gap-2 transition-colors ${
              abaAtiva === 'comunidade' ? 'bg-accent/10 text-accent border-b-2 border-accent' : 'text-muted hover:bg-bg'
            }`}
          >
            <Users size={18} /> Chat da Comunidade
          </button>
          <button
            onClick={() => setAbaAtiva('privadas')}
            className={`flex-1 py-4 font-bold text-sm flex items-center justify-center gap-2 transition-colors ${
              abaAtiva === 'privadas' ? 'bg-accent/10 text-accent border-b-2 border-accent' : 'text-muted hover:bg-bg'
            }`}
          >
            <MessageCircle size={18} /> Mensagens Privadas
          </button>
        </div>

        {abaAtiva === 'comunidade' ? (
          <ChatComunidade />
        ) : (
          <div className="min-h-[60vh] bg-bg/20">
            {conversas.length === 0 ? (
              <div className="text-center py-20">
                <MessageCircle className="mx-auto text-muted mb-4" size={48} />
                <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-text mb-2">Nenhuma conversa privada</h2>
                <p className="text-muted text-sm">Vá no Feed ou em um Perfil e clique em "Mensagem" para começar.</p>
              </div>
            ) : (
              <div>
                {conversas.map((conv) => (
                  <Link
                    key={conv.usuario.id}
                    href={`/mensagens/${conv.usuario.id}`}
                    className="flex items-center gap-4 p-4 border-b border-border last:border-0 hover:bg-bg/50 transition-colors"
                  >
                    <div className="relative w-12 h-12 rounded-full overflow-hidden bg-border shrink-0">
                      {conv.usuario.avatar_url ? (
                        <Image src={conv.usuario.avatar_url} alt="Avatar" fill sizes="48px" className="object-cover" />
                      ) : (
                        <div className="w-full h-full flex items-center justify-center text-sm font-bold">
                          {conv.usuario.username?.charAt(0).toUpperCase()}
                        </div>
                      )}
                    </div>
                    <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 mb-1">
                        <span className="font-bold text-text truncate">{conv.usuario.username}</span>
                        {conv.usuario.is_admin && <SeloAdmin />}
                      </div>
                      <p className={`text-sm truncate ${conv.naoLidas > 0 ? 'text-text font-medium' : 'text-muted'}`}>
                        {conv.ultimaMensagem}
                      </p>
                    </div>
                    {conv.naoLidas > 0 && (
                      <span className="bg-accent text-white text-xs font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full">
                        {conv.naoLidas}
                      </span>
                    )}
                  </Link>
                ))}
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

===== C:\anime-site\app\mensagens\[id]\page.tsx =====
'use client';

import { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { createClient } from '@/lib/supabase/client';
import { Loader2, Send, ArrowLeft, Trash2 } from 'lucide-react';
import Image from 'next/image';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { useRouter, useParams } from 'next/navigation';
import { formatDistanceToNow } from 'date-fns';
import { ptBR } from 'date-fns/locale';
import { SeloAdmin } from '@/components/layout/SeloAdmin';
import { TelaBloqueio } from '@/components/layout/TelaBloqueio'; // <-- IMPORT

interface Profile {
  id: string;
  username: string;
  avatar_url: string;
  is_admin: boolean;
}

interface Message {
  id: string;
  content: string;
  created_at: string;
  sender_id: string;
}

export default function MensagensPage() {
  const params = useParams();
  const targetUserId = params.id as string;
  
  const [messages, setMessages] = useState<Message[]>([]);
  const [newMessage, setNewMessage] = useState('');
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [chatBloqueado, setChatBloqueado] = useState(false);
  const [myUserId, setMyUserId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [isAdmin, setIsAdmin] = useState(false);
  const [targetProfile, setTargetProfile] = useState<Profile | null>(null);
  
  const bottomRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const supabase = createClient();
  const router = useRouter();

  useEffect(() => {
    let channel: any;

    const init = async () => {
      const { data: config } = await supabase.from('site_config').select('chat_enabled').single();
      if (config?.chat_enabled === false) {
        setChatBloqueado(true);
        setLoading(false);
        return;
      }

      const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
      if (!user) {
        router.push('/login');
        return;
      }
      setMyUserId(user.id);

      const { data: myProfile } = await supabase.from('profiles').select('is_admin').eq('id', user.id).single();
      if (myProfile?.is_admin) setIsAdmin(true);

      const { data: profile } = await supabase
        .from('profiles')
        .select('id, username, avatar_url, is_admin')
        .eq('id', targetUserId)
        .single();
      if (profile) setTargetProfile(profile);

      const { data: msgs } = await supabase
        .from('direct_messages')
        .select('*')
        .or(`and(sender_id.eq.${user.id},receiver_id.eq.${targetUserId}),and(sender_id.eq.${targetUserId},receiver_id.eq.${user.id})`)
        .order('created_at', { ascending: true });

      if (msgs) setMessages(msgs);
      setLoading(false);
      
      await supabase.from('direct_messages').update({ is_read: true }).eq('sender_id', targetUserId).eq('receiver_id', user.id).eq('is_read', false);

      const channelName = `chat-privado-${targetUserId}-${Date.now()}`;
      channel = supabase.channel(channelName);

      channel.on(
        'postgres_changes',
        { event: 'INSERT', schema: 'public', table: 'direct_messages' },
        (payload: any) => {
          if (chatBloqueado) return;

          const newMsg = payload.new as Message;
          if (
            (newMsg.sender_id === myUserId && (newMsg as any).receiver_id === targetUserId) ||
            (newMsg.sender_id === targetUserId && (newMsg as any).receiver_id === myUserId)
          ) {
            setMessages((prev) => [...prev, newMsg]);
          }
        }
      );

      channel.subscribe();
    };

    init();

    return () => {
      if (channel) supabase.removeChannel(channel);
    };
  }, [supabase, router, targetUserId, myUserId, chatBloqueado]);

  useEffect(() => {
    bottomRef.current?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth' });
  }, [messages]);

  const handleEnviar = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!newMessage.trim() || !myUserId || chatBloqueado) return;

    const msg = newMessage;
    setNewMessage('');

    await supabase.from('direct_messages').insert({
      sender_id: myUserId,
      receiver_id: targetUserId,
      content: msg
    });
  };

  const handleDeletarMensagem = async (id: string) => {
    if (!confirm('Tem certeza que deseja apagar esta mensagem?')) return;

    const { error } = await supabase.from('direct_messages').delete().eq('id', id);
    
    if (error) {
      alert('Erro ao apagar a mensagem.');
    } else {
      setMessages((prev) => prev.filter((msg) => msg.id !== id));
    }
  };

  if (loading) {
    return <div className="flex justify-center items-center h-[50vh]"><Loader2 className="animate-spin text-accent" size={40} /></div>;
  }

  if (chatBloqueado) {
    return (
      <TelaBloqueio 
        titulo="Chat em Manutenção"
        descricao="Estamos preparando a comunidade do AniVerse para você. As mensagens serão liberadas em breve!"
        spoiler={
          <p className="text-sm text-muted text-left">
            Em breve você poderá enviar mensagens privadas, criar grupos e compartilhar seus animes favoritos!
          </p>
        }
      />
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="w-full max-w-3xl mx-auto py-8 flex flex-col h-[85vh]">
      <div className="bg-surface border border-border rounded-2xl flex flex-col flex-1 overflow-hidden shadow-lg">
        
        <div className="p-4 border-b border-border bg-bg/50 flex items-center gap-4">
          <Link href="/mensagens" className="text-muted hover:text-text transition-colors">
            <ArrowLeft size={20} />
          </Link>
          
          <Link href={`/perfil/${targetProfile?.username}`} className="flex items-center gap-3 hover:opacity-80 transition-opacity">
            <div className="relative w-10 h-10 rounded-full overflow-hidden bg-border">
              {targetProfile?.avatar_url ? (
                <Image src={targetProfile.avatar_url} alt="Avatar" fill sizes="40px" className="object-cover" />
              ) : (
                <div className="w-full h-full flex items-center justify-center text-xs font-bold">{targetProfile?.username?.charAt(0).toUpperCase()}</div>
              )}
            </div>
            <div>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                <h1 className="text-base font-bold text-text">{targetProfile?.username}</h1>
                {targetProfile?.is_admin && <SeloAdmin />}
              </div>
              <p className="text-xs text-muted">Mensagem Direta</p>
            </div>
          </Link>
        </div>

        <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-4 space-y-4 bg-bg/20">
          {messages.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center h-full text-muted">
              <p>Nenhuma mensagem ainda.</p>
              <p className="text-sm">Diga oi para {targetProfile?.username}!</p>
            </div>
          ) : (
            messages.map((msg) => {
              const isMe = msg.sender_id === myUserId;
              const podeDeletar = isMe || isAdmin;

              return (
                <div key={msg.id} className={`flex ${isMe ? 'justify-end' : 'justify-start'} group`}>
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2 max-w-[70%]">
                    {podeDeletar && (
                      <button 
                        onClick={() => handleDeletarMensagem(msg.id)}
                        className={`opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 transition-opacity text-muted hover:text-red-500 ${isMe ? 'order-first' : 'order-last'}`}
                        title="Apagar mensagem"
                      >
                        <Trash2 size={14} />
                      </button>
                    )}
                    
                    <div className={`p-3 rounded-2xl text-sm ${
                      isMe 
                        ? 'bg-accent text-white rounded-tr-sm' 
                        : 'bg-surface text-text rounded-tl-sm border border-border'
                    }`}>
                      {msg.content}
                      <span className={`text-[10px] block mt-1 ${isMe ? 'text-white/70 text-right' : 'text-muted text-left'}`}>
                        {formatDistanceToNow(new Date(msg.created_at), { addSuffix: true, locale: ptBR })}
                      </span>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              );
            })
          )}
          <div ref={bottomRef} />
        </div>

        <form onSubmit={handleEnviar} className="p-3 border-t border-border bg-surface flex gap-2">
          <input
            type="text"
            value={newMessage}
            onChange={(e) => setNewMessage(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="Digite sua mensagem..."
            disabled={chatBloqueado}
            className="flex-1 bg-bg border border-border rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm text-text focus:outline-none focus:border-accent transition-colors disabled:opacity-50"
          />
          <button 
            type="submit" 
            disabled={!newMessage.trim() || chatBloqueado} 
            className="bg-accent text-white p-3 rounded-xl disabled:opacity-50 hover:bg-accent/90 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
          >
            <Send size={20} />
          </button>
        </form>

      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

===== C:\anime-site\app\perfil\page.tsx =====
import { createClient } from '@/lib/supabase/server';
import { redirect } from 'next/navigation';
import Link from 'next/link';
import Image from 'next/image';
import { Settings, PlayCircle, CheckCircle2, Bookmark, Star, User } from 'lucide-react';

export const dynamic = 'force-dynamic';

export default async function PerfilPage() {
  const supabase = await createClient();
  const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser();

  if (!user) redirect('/login');

  // 1. Busca perfil
  const { data: profile } = await supabase
    .from('profiles')
    .select('username, avatar_url')
    .eq('id', user.id)
    .single();

  // 2. Busca todos os animes salvos pelo usuário
  const { data: userAnimes } = await supabase
    .from('user_anime')
    .select('anime_id, status, rating')
    .eq('user_id', user.id)
    .order('updated_at', { ascending: false });

  // Se não tiver nenhum anime, mostra a tela vazia
  if (!userAnimes || userAnimes.length === 0) {
    return (
      <div className="w-full max-w-5xl mx-auto py-8">
        <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-center justify-between gap-6 mb-10 bg-surface border border-border p-6 md:p-8 rounded-2xl">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-5">
            {/* Avatar Estático */}
            <div className="relative w-20 h-20 rounded-full overflow-hidden border-4 border-accent bg-surface shadow-lg">
              {profile?.avatar_url ? (
                <Image src={profile.avatar_url} alt="Avatar" fill className="object-cover" />
              ) : (
                <div className="w-full h-full flex items-center justify-center text-accent">
                  <User size={40} />
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
            <div>
              <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold text-text">{profile?.username || user.email}</h1>
              <p className="text-muted mt-1">Bem-vindo ao seu espaço no AniVerse.</p>
            </div>
          </div>
          <Link href="/perfil/configuracoes" className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2 px-4 py-2.5 bg-bg border border-border text-text rounded-lg hover:border-accent hover:text-accent transition-colors font-medium text-sm cursor-pointer w-fit">
            <Settings size={18} /> Configurações
          </Link>
        </div>
        <div className="text-center py-20 border-2 border-dashed border-border rounded-2xl">
          <p className="text-muted">Você ainda não marcou nenhum anime.</p>
          <Link href="/" className="text-accent font-bold hover:underline mt-2 inline-block">
            Explorar Lançamentos
          </Link>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  // 3. Busca os detalhes de cada anime diretamente no AniList (Server-side)
  const animesDetalhes = await Promise.all(
    userAnimes.map(async (item) => {
      try {
        const res = await fetch('https://graphql.anilist.co', {
          method: 'POST',
          headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
          body: JSON.stringify({
            query: `
              query ($id: Int) {
                Media(id: $id, type: ANIME) {
                  id
                  title { romaji english }
                  coverImage { large }
                }
              }
            `,
            variables: { id: item.anime_id }
          }),
          next: { revalidate: 86400 }
        });

        if (!res.ok) return null;

        const json = await res.json();
        const media = json.data?.Media;
        if (!media) return null;

        return {
          id: media.id,
          title: media.title.english || media.title.romaji,
          coverUrl: media.coverImage.large,
          status: item.status,
          rating: item.rating
        };
      } catch {
        return null;
      }
    })
  );

  // Filtra nulos e separa por status
  const animesValidos = animesDetalhes.filter(Boolean) as any[];
  const assistindo = animesValidos.filter((a: any) => a.status === 'assistindo');
  const concluidos = animesValidos.filter((a: any) => a.status === 'concluido');
  const quero = animesValidos.filter((a: any) => a.status === 'quero');

  const notasValidas = animesValidos.filter((a: any) => a.rating > 0);
  const mediaNotas = notasValidas.length > 0 
    ? (notasValidas.reduce((acc, a) => acc + a.rating, 0) / notasValidas.length).toFixed(1) 
    : '0.0';

  const nomeExibicao = profile?.username || user.email;

  return (
    <div className="w-full max-w-5xl mx-auto py-8">
      {/* CABEÇALHO DO PERFIL */}
      <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-center justify-between gap-6 mb-10 bg-surface border border-border p-6 md:p-8 rounded-2xl">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-5">
          {/* Avatar Estático */}
          <div className="relative w-20 h-20 rounded-full overflow-hidden border-4 border-accent bg-surface shadow-lg">
            {profile?.avatar_url ? (
              <Image src={profile.avatar_url} alt="Avatar" fill className="object-cover" />
            ) : (
              <div className="w-full h-full flex items-center justify-center text-accent">
                <User size={40} />
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
          <div>
            <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold text-text">{nomeExibicao}</h1>
            <p className="text-muted mt-1">Bem-vindo ao seu espaço no AniVerse.</p>
          </div>
        </div>
        <Link href="/perfil/configuracoes" className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2 px-4 py-2.5 bg-bg border border-border text-text rounded-lg hover:border-accent hover:text-accent transition-colors font-medium text-sm cursor-pointer w-fit">
          <Settings size={18} /> Configurações
        </Link>
      </div>

      {/* ESTATÍSTICAS */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-4 gap-4 mb-10">
        <div className="bg-surface border border-border p-4 rounded-xl flex flex-col items-center justify-center">
          <div className="text-blue-500 mb-2"><PlayCircle size={24} /></div>
          <p className="text-2xl font-bold text-text">{assistindo.length}</p>
          <p className="text-xs text-muted font-medium uppercase tracking-wider">Assistindo</p>
        </div>
        <div className="bg-surface border border-border p-4 rounded-xl flex flex-col items-center justify-center">
          <div className="text-green-500 mb-2"><CheckCircle2 size={24} /></div>
          <p className="text-2xl font-bold text-text">{concluidos.length}</p>
          <p className="text-xs text-muted font-medium uppercase tracking-wider">Concluídos</p>
        </div>
        <div className="bg-surface border border-border p-4 rounded-xl flex flex-col items-center justify-center">
          <div className="text-yellow-500 mb-2"><Bookmark size={24} /></div>
          <p className="text-2xl font-bold text-text">{quero.length}</p>
          <p className="text-xs text-muted font-medium uppercase tracking-wider">Quero Ver</p>
        </div>
        <div className="bg-surface border border-border p-4 rounded-xl flex flex-col items-center justify-center">
          <div className="text-accent mb-2"><Star size={24} /></div>
          <p className="text-2xl font-bold text-text">{mediaNotas}</p>
          <p className="text-xs text-muted font-medium uppercase tracking-wider">Média</p>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* LISTAS DE ANIMES */}
      <div className="space-y-10">
        {assistindo.length > 0 && (
          <section>
            <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-text mb-4 flex items-center gap-2">
              <PlayCircle size={20} className="text-blue-500" /> Assistindo
            </h2>
            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-4 lg:grid-cols-5 gap-4">
              {assistindo.map((anime: any) => <AnimeCard key={anime.id} anime={anime} />)}
            </div>
          </section>
        )}

        {quero.length > 0 && (
          <section>
            <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-text mb-4 flex items-center gap-2">
              <Bookmark size={20} className="text-yellow-500" /> Quero Assistir
            </h2>
            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-4 lg:grid-cols-5 gap-4">
              {quero.map((anime: any) => <AnimeCard key={anime.id} anime={anime} />)}
            </div>
          </section>
        )}

        {concluidos.length > 0 && (
          <section>
            <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-text mb-4 flex items-center gap-2">
              <CheckCircle2 size={20} className="text-green-500" /> Concluídos
            </h2>
            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-4 lg:grid-cols-5 gap-4">
              {concluidos.map((anime: any) => <AnimeCard key={anime.id} anime={anime} />)}
            </div>
          </section>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

// --- Componente do Card de Anime ---
function AnimeCard({ anime }: { anime: any }) {
  return (
    <Link 
      href={`/anime/${anime.id}`} 
      className="group flex flex-col bg-surface rounded-xl overflow-hidden border border-border hover:border-accent transition-all shadow-sm"
    >
      <div className="relative aspect-[2/3] w-full overflow-hidden">
        <Image src={anime.coverUrl} alt={anime.title} fill sizes="(max-width: 768px) 50vw, 20vw" className="object-cover group-hover:scale-110 transition-transform duration-300" />
        
        {anime.rating > 0 && (
          <div className="absolute top-2 right-2 bg-black/70 text-yellow-400 text-xs font-bold px-2 py-1 rounded-md flex items-center gap-1">
            <Star size={12} fill="currentColor" /> {anime.rating}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
      <div className="p-3 flex-1 flex flex-col">
        <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-text line-clamp-2 group-hover:text-accent transition-colors">{anime.title}</h3>
      </div>
    </Link>
  );
}

export default async function ConfiguracoesPage() {

  if (!user) {
    redirect('/login');

  return (

===== C:\anime-site\app\perfil\[username]\page.tsx =====
import { createClient } from '@/lib/supabase/server';
import { notFound } from 'next/navigation';
import Image from 'next/image';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { Star, PlayCircle, CheckCircle2, Bookmark, User, MessageCircle } from 'lucide-react';
import { BotaoSeguir } from '@/components/perfil/BotaoSeguir';
import { SeloAdmin } from '@/components/layout/SeloAdmin';

export const dynamic = 'force-dynamic';

interface PageProps {
  params: Promise<{ username: string }>;
}

export default async function PerfilPublicoPage({ params }: PageProps) {
  const resolvedParams = await params;
  const supabase = await createClient();
  
  const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser();

  const { data: profile } = await supabase
    .from('profiles')
    .select('id, username, avatar_url, is_admin')
    .ilike('username', resolvedParams.username)
    .single();

  if (!profile) {
    return notFound();
  }

  const isMe = user?.id === profile.id;

  const { data: userAnimes } = await supabase
    .from('user_anime')
    .select('anime_id, status, rating')
    .eq('user_id', profile.id)
    .order('updated_at', { ascending: false });

  let isFollowing = false;
  if (user && !isMe) {
    const { data: follow } = await supabase
      .from('follows')
      .select('id')
      .eq('follower_id', user.id)
      .eq('following_id', profile.id)
      .maybeSingle();
    isFollowing = !!follow;
  }

  const { count: followersCount } = await supabase.from('follows').select('*', { count: 'exact', head: true }).eq('following_id', profile.id);
  const { count: followingCount } = await supabase.from('follows').select('*', { count: 'exact', head: true }).eq('follower_id', profile.id);

  // Como estamos no servidor, o fetch para AniList pode ser feito diretamente
  const animesDetalhes = await Promise.all(
    (userAnimes || []).map(async (item) => {
      try {
        const res = await fetch('https://graphql.anilist.co', {
          method: 'POST',
          headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
          body: JSON.stringify({
            query: `query ($id: Int) { Media(id: $id, type: ANIME) { id title { romaji english } coverImage { large } } }`,
            variables: { id: item.anime_id }
          }),
          next: { revalidate: 86400 }
        });
        if (!res.ok) return null;
        const json = await res.json();
        const media = json.data?.Media;
        if (!media) return null;
        return { 
          id: media.id, 
          title: media.title.english || media.title.romaji, 
          coverUrl: media.coverImage.large, 
          status: item.status, 
          rating: item.rating 
        };
      } catch { return null; }
    })
  );

  const animesValidos = animesDetalhes.filter((a): a is NonNullable<typeof a> => a !== null);
  const assistindo = animesValidos.filter((a) => a.status === 'assistindo');
  const concluidos = animesValidos.filter((a) => a.status === 'concluido');
  const quero = animesValidos.filter((a) => a.status === 'quero');
  const notasValidas = animesValidos.filter((a) => a.rating > 0);
  const mediaNotas = notasValidas.length > 0 ? (notasValidas.reduce((acc, a) => acc + a.rating, 0) / notasValidas.length).toFixed(1) : '0.0';

  return (
    <div className="w-full max-w-5xl mx-auto py-8">
      {/* CABEÇALHO PÚBLICO */}
      <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-center justify-between gap-6 mb-10 bg-surface border border-border p-6 md:p-8 rounded-2xl">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-5">
          <div className="relative w-20 h-20 rounded-full overflow-hidden border-4 border-accent bg-surface shadow-lg">
            {profile.avatar_url ? (
              <Image src={profile.avatar_url} alt="Avatar" fill sizes="80px" className="object-cover" />
            ) : (
              <div className="w-full h-full flex items-center justify-center text-accent"><User size={40} /></div>
            )}
          </div>
          <div>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold text-text">{profile.username}</h1>
              {profile.is_admin && <SeloAdmin />}
            </div>
            <p className="text-muted mt-1 text-sm">
              <strong className="text-text">{followersCount || 0}</strong> seguidores • <strong className="text-text">{followingCount || 0}</strong> seguindo
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>
        
        {!isMe && user && (
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <BotaoSeguir targetUserId={profile.id} initialIsFollowing={isFollowing} />
            <Link 
              href={`/mensagens/${profile.id}`}
              className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2 px-4 py-2.5 bg-accent text-white rounded-lg hover:bg-accent/90 transition-colors font-medium text-sm cursor-pointer"
            >
              <MessageCircle size={18} /> Mensagem
            </Link>
          </div>
        )}
        {isMe && (
          <Link href="/perfil" className="text-sm font-semibold text-accent hover:underline">
            Editar meu perfil
          </Link>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* ESTATÍSTICAS */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-4 gap-4 mb-10">
        <div className="bg-surface border border-border p-4 rounded-xl flex flex-col items-center justify-center">
          <div className="text-blue-500 mb-2"><PlayCircle size={24} /></div>
          <p className="text-2xl font-bold text-text">{assistindo.length}</p>
          <p className="text-xs text-muted font-medium uppercase tracking-wider">Assistindo</p>
        </div>
        <div className="bg-surface border border-border p-4 rounded-xl flex flex-col items-center justify-center">
          <div className="text-green-500 mb-2"><CheckCircle2 size={24} /></div>
          <p className="text-2xl font-bold text-text">{concluidos.length}</p>
          <p className="text-xs text-muted font-medium uppercase tracking-wider">Concluídos</p>
        </div>
        <div className="bg-surface border border-border p-4 rounded-xl flex flex-col items-center justify-center">
          <div className="text-yellow-500 mb-2"><Bookmark size={24} /></div>
          <p className="text-2xl font-bold text-text">{quero.length}</p>
          <p className="text-xs text-muted font-medium uppercase tracking-wider">Quero Ver</p>
        </div>
        <div className="bg-surface border border-border p-4 rounded-xl flex flex-col items-center justify-center">
          <div className="text-accent mb-2"><Star size={24} /></div>
          <p className="text-2xl font-bold text-text">{mediaNotas}</p>
          <p className="text-xs text-muted font-medium uppercase tracking-wider">Média</p>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* LISTAS DE ANIMES */}
      <div className="space-y-10">
        {assistindo.length > 0 && (
          <section>
            <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-text mb-4 flex items-center gap-2"><PlayCircle size={20} className="text-blue-500" /> Assistindo</h2>
            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-4 lg:grid-cols-5 gap-4">
              {assistindo.map((anime) => <AnimeCard key={anime.id} anime={anime} />)}
            </div>
          </section>
        )}
        {quero.length > 0 && (
          <section>
            <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-text mb-4 flex items-center gap-2"><Bookmark size={20} className="text-yellow-500" /> Quero Assistir</h2>
            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-4 lg:grid-cols-5 gap-4">
              {quero.map((anime) => <AnimeCard key={anime.id} anime={anime} />)}
            </div>
          </section>
        )}
        {concluidos.length > 0 && (
          <section>
            <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-text mb-4 flex items-center gap-2"><CheckCircle2 size={20} className="text-green-500" /> Concluídos</h2>
            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-4 lg:grid-cols-5 gap-4">
              {concluidos.map((anime) => <AnimeCard key={anime.id} anime={anime} />)}
            </div>
          </section>
        )}
        {animesValidos.length === 0 && (
          <div className="text-center py-20 border-2 border-dashed border-border rounded-2xl">
            <p className="text-muted">Este usuário ainda não marcou nenhum anime.</p>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function AnimeCard({ anime }: { anime: { id: number; title: string; coverUrl: string; rating: number } }) {
  return (
    <Link href={`/anime/${anime.id}`} className="group flex flex-col bg-surface rounded-xl overflow-hidden border border-border hover:border-accent transition-all shadow-sm">
      <div className="relative aspect-[2/3] w-full overflow-hidden">
        <Image 
          src={anime.coverUrl} 
          alt={anime.title} 
          fill 
          sizes="(max-width: 768px) 50vw, (max-width: 1200px) 33vw, 20vw" 
          className="object-cover group-hover:scale-110 transition-transform duration-300" 
        />
        {anime.rating > 0 && (
          <div className="absolute top-2 right-2 bg-black/70 text-yellow-400 text-xs font-bold px-2 py-1 rounded-md flex items-center gap-1">
            <Star size={12} fill="currentColor" /> {anime.rating}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
      <div className="p-3 flex-1 flex flex-col">
        <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-text line-clamp-2 group-hover:text-accent transition-colors">{anime.title}</h3>
      </div>
    </Link>
  );
}

        <section>
          <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-text mb-2">5. Seus Direitos (LGPD)</h2>
          <p>De acordo com a LGPD, você tem direito a: acessar, corrigir ou excluir seus dados pessoais a qualquer momento. Para exercer esses direitos, entre em contato pelo e-mail: <strong>carlos.eduardo.c.r@outlook.com</strong>.</p>
        </section>

        <section>
          <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-text mb-2">6. Exclusão de Conta</h2>
          <p>Você pode solicitar a exclusão da sua conta a qualquer momento através da página de configurações do seu perfil. Ao excluir a conta, todos os seus dados (listas, posts, mensagens) serão permanentemente removidos dos nossos servidores.</p>
        </section>

        <section>
          <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-text mb-2">7. Foro e Jurisdição</h2>
          <p>Esta política é regida pelas leis da República Federativa do Brasil. Quaisquer disputas serão resolvidas no foro da comarca de São Paulo/SP.</p>
        </section>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
