===== C:\anime-site\app\redefinir-senha\page.tsx =====
'use client';

import { useState } from 'react';
import { createClient } from '@/lib/supabase/client';
import { Loader2, Lock, Mail, KeyRound, AlertTriangle, ArrowLeft } from 'lucide-react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import Link from 'next/link';

export default function RedefinirSenhaPage() {
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
  const [tokenHash, setTokenHash] = useState('');
  const [senha, setSenha] = useState('');
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [erro, setErro] = useState('');
  const [etapa, setEtapa] = useState<'validar' | 'redefinir'>('validar');
  
  const router = useRouter();
  const supabase = createClient();

  const handleValidarCodigo = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setLoading(true);
    setErro('');

    const { error } = await supabase.auth.verifyOtp({
      token_hash: tokenHash.trim(), // <-- MUDOU AQUI
      type: 'recovery',
    });

    if (error) {
      console.error("Erro no Supabase:", error);
      setErro('Código inválido ou expirado. Solicite um novo e-mail e tente novamente.');
      setLoading(false);
    } else {
      setEtapa('redefinir');
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const handleRedefinirSenha = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setLoading(true);
    setErro('');
    const { error } = await supabase.auth.updateUser({ password: senha });
    if (error) {
      setErro('Erro ao redefinir a senha. Tente novamente.');
      setLoading(false);
    } else {
      alert('Senha alterada com sucesso! Faça login novamente.');
      await supabase.auth.signOut();
      router.push('/login');
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center min-h-[80vh] px-4">
      <div className="w-full max-w-md bg-surface border border-border p-8 rounded-2xl shadow-lg">
        <Link href="/login" className="text-muted hover:text-text flex items-center gap-1 text-sm mb-6 transition-colors">
          <ArrowLeft size={16} /> Voltar para o login
        </Link>
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-text mb-2">Criar Nova Senha</h1>
        {erro && (
          <div className="bg-red-500/10 border border-red-500/50 text-red-500 p-3 rounded-lg text-sm mb-4 flex items-start gap-2">
            <AlertTriangle className="shrink-0" size={18} />
            <span>{erro}</span>
          </div>
        )}
        {etapa === 'validar' && (
          <>
            <p className="text-muted text-sm mb-6">Digite seu e-mail e o código (token hash) que você recebeu.</p>
            <form onSubmit={handleValidarCodigo} className="flex flex-col gap-4">
              <div className="relative">
                <Mail className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-muted" size={20} />
                <input type="email" required placeholder="Seu e-mail" value={email} onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)} className="w-full bg-bg border border-border text-text rounded-lg py-3 pl-10 pr-4 focus:outline-none focus:border-accent" />
              </div>
              <div className="relative">
                <KeyRound className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-muted" size={20} />
                <input type="text" required placeholder="Token Hash do e-mail" value={tokenHash} onChange={(e) => setTokenHash(e.target.value)} className="w-full bg-bg border border-border text-text rounded-lg py-3 pl-10 pr-4 focus:outline-none focus:border-accent text-sm" />
              </div>
              <button type="submit" disabled={loading} className="bg-accent text-white font-bold py-3 rounded-lg hover:bg-accent/90 disabled:opacity-50 flex justify-center items-center gap-2">
                {loading ? <Loader2 className="animate-spin" size={20} /> : 'Validar Código'}
              </button>
            </form>
          </>
        )}
        {etapa === 'redefinir' && (
          <>
            <p className="text-muted text-sm mb-6">Código validado! Agora digite a sua nova senha.</p>
            <form onSubmit={handleRedefinirSenha} className="flex flex-col gap-4">
              <div className="relative">
                <Lock className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-muted" size={20} />
                <input type="password" required placeholder="Nova senha" value={senha} onChange={(e) => setSenha(e.target.value)} minLength={6} className="w-full bg-bg border border-border text-text rounded-lg py-3 pl-10 pr-4 focus:outline-none focus:border-accent" />
              </div>
              <button type="submit" disabled={loading} className="bg-accent text-white font-bold py-3 rounded-lg hover:bg-accent/90 disabled:opacity-50 flex justify-center items-center gap-2">
                {loading ? <Loader2 className="animate-spin" size={20} /> : 'Salvar Nova Senha'}
              </button>
            </form>
          </>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

===== C:\anime-site\app\top10\page.tsx =====
'use client';

import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { createClient } from '@/lib/supabase/client';
import { Search, Loader2, GripVertical, X, Save, Star, Share2 } from 'lucide-react';
import { GeradorCard } from '@/components/top10/GeradorCard';
import Image from 'next/image';
import { EspacoAnuncio } from '@/components/layout/EspacoAnuncio';

import {
  DndContext,
  closestCenter,
  KeyboardSensor,
  PointerSensor,
  useSensor,
  useSensors,
} from '@dnd-kit/core';
import {
  arrayMove,
  SortableContext,
  sortableKeyboardCoordinates,
  verticalListSortingStrategy,
  useSortable,
} from '@dnd-kit/sortable';
import { CSS } from '@dnd-kit/utilities';

// --- Componente de Anime Individual (Arrastável) ---
function SortableAnime({ anime, index, onRemove }: { anime: any, index: number, onRemove: (id: number) => void }) {
  const { attributes, listeners, setNodeRef, transform, transition } = useSortable({ id: anime.id });
  const style = { transform: CSS.Transform.toString(transform), transition };

  return (
    <div ref={setNodeRef} style={style} className="flex items-center gap-4 bg-surface border border-border p-3 rounded-xl mb-2 shadow-sm">
      <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded-full bg-accent text-white font-bold flex items-center justify-center flex-shrink-0">
        {index + 1}
      </div>
      <div className="relative w-12 h-16 rounded-md overflow-hidden flex-shrink-0">
        <Image src={anime.coverUrl} alt={anime.title} fill className="object-cover" />
      </div>
      <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
        <h3 className="text-text font-bold truncate">{anime.title}</h3>
        <p className="text-muted text-xs">Nota: {anime.score}</p>
      </div>
      <button onClick={() => onRemove(anime.id)} className="text-muted hover:text-red-500 cursor-pointer">
        <X size={20} />
      </button>
      <button {...attributes} {...listeners} className="cursor-grab active:cursor-grabbing text-muted hover:text-text p-1">
        <GripVertical size={20} />
      </button>
    </div>
  );
}

// --- Página Principal ---
export default function Top10Page() {
  const supabase = createClient();
  const router = useRouter();

  const [animes, setAnimes] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState('');
  const [searchResults, setSearchResults] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [isSearching, setIsSearching] = useState(false);
  const [isSaving, setIsSaving] = useState(false);
  
  const [userId, setUserId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [username, setUsername] = useState<string>('usuario'); 
  const [feedLiberado, setFeedLiberado] = useState(true);

  const sensors = useSensors(
    useSensor(PointerSensor, { activationConstraint: { distance: 5 } }),
    useSensor(KeyboardSensor, { coordinateGetter: sortableKeyboardCoordinates })
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    const init = async () => {
      const { data: config } = await supabase.from('site_config').select('feed_enabled').single();
      if (config?.feed_enabled === false) setFeedLiberado(false);

      const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
      if (!user) { router.push('/login'); return; }
      setUserId(user.id);

      const { data: profile } = await supabase.from('profiles').select('username').eq('id', user.id).single();
      if (profile?.username) setUsername(profile.username);

      const { data } = await supabase
        .from('user_top10')
        .select('position, anime_id')
        .eq('user_id', user.id)
        .order('position', { ascending: true })
        .limit(10);

      if (data && data.length > 0) {
        const animePromises = data.map(async (item) => {
          const res = await fetch(`/api/anime/${item.anime_id}`);
          if (!res.ok) return null;
          return await res.json();
        });
        const resolvedAnimes = (await Promise.all(animePromises)).filter(Boolean);
        setAnimes(resolvedAnimes);
      }
    };
    init();
  }, [supabase, router]);

  const handleSearch = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!searchQuery.trim()) return;
    setIsSearching(true);
    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/search-anime', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ search: searchQuery })
      });
      if (!res.ok) throw new Error('Falha na busca');
      setSearchResults(await res.json());
    } catch (error) {
      console.error("Erro na busca:", error);
    } finally {
      setIsSearching(false);
    }
  };

  const addAnime = (anime: any) => {
    if (animes.length >= 10) return alert("Você já tem 10 animes no Top!");
    if (animes.find(a => a.id === anime.id)) return alert("Esse anime já está na sua lista!");
    setAnimes([...animes, anime]);
    setSearchQuery('');
    setSearchResults([]);
  };

  const removeAnime = (id: number) => setAnimes(animes.filter(a => a.id !== id));

  const handleDragEnd = (event: any) => {
    const { active, over } = event;
    if (active.id !== over.id) {
      setAnimes((items) => {
        const oldIndex = items.findIndex((item) => item.id === active.id);
        const newIndex = items.findIndex((item) => item.id === over.id);
        return arrayMove(items, oldIndex, newIndex);
      });
    }
  };

  const saveTop10 = async () => {
    if (!userId) return;
    setIsSaving(true);
    await supabase.from('user_top10').delete().eq('user_id', userId);
    const insertData = animes.map((anime, index) => ({
      user_id: userId,
      anime_id: anime.id,
      position: index + 1
    }));
    const { error } = await supabase.from('user_top10').insert(insertData);
    if (error) alert("Erro ao salvar o Top 10."); else alert("Top 10 salvo com sucesso!");
    setIsSaving(false);
  };

  const handleCompartilhar = async () => {
    const url = `${window.location.origin}/perfil/${username}`;
    const shareData = {
      title: `Top 10 de ${username} no AniVerse`,
      text: `Olha o meu Top 10 de animes no AniVerse!`,
      url: url,
    };

    try {
      if (navigator.share) {
        await navigator.share(shareData);
      } else {
        await navigator.clipboard.writeText(url);
        alert('Link do seu perfil copiado para a área de transferência!');
      }
    } catch (error) {
      console.log('Compartilhamento cancelado', error);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="w-full max-w-7xl mx-auto py-8 px-4">
      
      <div className="mb-8">
        <EspacoAnuncio formato="banner" />
      </div>

      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-12 gap-6 items-start">
        
        <div className="hidden lg:block lg:col-span-2 w-full sticky top-24">
          <EspacoAnuncio formato="vertical" />
        </div>

        <div className="lg:col-span-8 w-full max-w-3xl mx-auto">
          <header className="mb-8">
            <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold text-text mb-2">Meu Top 10 Animes</h1>
            <p className="text-muted">Monte seu ranking definitivo. Arraste para ordenar do seu favorito (1º) ao menos favorito (10º).</p>
          </header>

          <div className="mb-8 relative">
            <form onSubmit={handleSearch} className="relative">
              <input
                type="text"
                value={searchQuery}
                onChange={(e) => setSearchQuery(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="Busque por um anime para adicionar..."
                className="w-full bg-surface border border-border text-text rounded-xl py-4 pl-12 pr-4 focus:outline-none focus:border-accent transition-colors"
              />
              <Search className="absolute left-4 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-muted" size={20} />
              <button type="submit" className="absolute right-2 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 bg-accent text-white p-2 rounded-lg cursor-pointer">
                {isSearching ? <Loader2 className="animate-spin" size={20} /> : 'Buscar'}
              </button>
            </form>

            {searchResults.length > 0 && (
              <div className="mt-2 bg-surface border border-border rounded-xl overflow-hidden shadow-xl absolute w-full z-10">
                {searchResults.map((anime) => (
                  <button
                    key={anime.id}
                    onClick={() => addAnime(anime)}
                    className="w-full flex items-center gap-4 p-3 hover:bg-bg transition-colors border-b border-border last:border-0 text-left cursor-pointer"
                  >
                    <div className="relative w-10 h-14 rounded-md overflow-hidden flex-shrink-0">
                      <Image src={anime.coverUrl} alt={anime.title} fill className="object-cover" />
                    </div>
                    <div className="flex-1">
                      <p className="font-bold text-text text-sm">{anime.title}</p>
                      <p className="text-xs text-muted">Nota: {anime.score}</p>
                    </div>
                  </button>
                ))}
              </div>
            )}
          </div>

          {animes.length > 0 ? (
            <div className="mb-8">
              <DndContext sensors={sensors} collisionDetection={closestCenter} onDragEnd={handleDragEnd}>
                <SortableContext items={animes.map(a => a.id)} strategy={verticalListSortingStrategy}>
                  {animes.map((anime, index) => (
                    <SortableAnime key={anime.id} anime={anime} index={index} onRemove={removeAnime} />
                  ))}
                </SortableContext>
              </DndContext>
              
              <button
                onClick={saveTop10}
                disabled={isSaving || animes.length < 1}
                className="w-full mt-6 bg-accent text-white font-bold py-4 rounded-xl hover:bg-accent/90 transition-all flex items-center justify-center gap-2 disabled:opacity-50 cursor-pointer"
              >
                {isSaving ? <Loader2 className="animate-spin" size={20} /> : <Save size={20} />}
                Salvar Meu Top 10
              </button>

              {/* BOTÃO DE COMPARTILHAR O TOP 10 */}
              {animes.length > 0 && (
                <button
                  onClick={handleCompartilhar}
                  className="w-full mt-3 bg-surface border border-border text-text font-bold py-4 rounded-xl hover:border-accent hover:text-accent transition-all flex items-center justify-center gap-2 cursor-pointer"
                >
                  <Share2 size={20} />
                  Compartilhar meu Top 10
                </button>
              )}

              {animes.length === 10 && (
                <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-4 mt-6 w-full max-w-md mx-auto">
                  <GeradorCard animes={animes} username={username} feedLiberado={feedLiberado} />
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="text-center py-20 border-2 border-dashed border-border rounded-2xl">
              <Star className="mx-auto text-muted mb-4" size={48} />
              <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-text mb-2">Seu Top 10 está vazio</h2>
              <p className="text-muted text-sm">Use a barra de busca acima para adicionar seus animes favoritos.</p>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        <div className="hidden lg:block lg:col-span-2 w-full sticky top-24">
          <EspacoAnuncio formato="vertical" />
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="mt-10">
        <EspacoAnuncio formato="banner" />
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

===== C:\anime-site\components\admin\BotaoNotificar.tsx =====
'use client';

import { useState } from 'react';
import { Bell, X, Loader2 } from 'lucide-react';

export function BotaoNotificar({ userId, username }: { userId: string, username: string }) {
  const [isOpen, setIsOpen] = useState(false);
  const [title, setTitle] = useState('');
  const [message, setMessage] = useState('');
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);

  const handleEnviar = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setLoading(true);

    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/admin/notificar', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ userId, title, message })
      });

      if (!res.ok) throw new Error('Erro ao enviar');

      alert(`Notificação enviada para ${username}!`);
      setIsOpen(false);
      setTitle('');
      setMessage('');
    } catch (error) {
      alert("Ocorreu um erro ao enviar a notificação.");
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <>
      <button
        onClick={() => setIsOpen(true)}
        className="p-2 bg-accent/10 text-accent rounded-lg hover:bg-accent hover:text-white transition-colors cursor-pointer"
        title="Enviar notificação"
      >
        <Bell size={16} />
      </button>

      {isOpen && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-[100] flex items-center justify-center bg-black/60 backdrop-blur-sm p-4">
          <div className="w-full max-w-md bg-surface border border-border rounded-2xl p-6 shadow-2xl relative">
            
            <button onClick={() => setIsOpen(false)} className="absolute top-4 right-4 text-muted hover:text-red-400">
              <X size={20} />
            </button>

            <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-text mb-1 flex items-center gap-2">
              <Bell className="text-accent" /> Notificar {username}
            </h2>
            <p className="text-xs text-muted mb-6">O aviso aparecerá no sininho do usuário.</p>

            <form onSubmit={handleEnviar} className="flex flex-col gap-4">
              <div>
                <label className="text-sm font-semibold text-text mb-1 block">Título</label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  value={title}
                  onChange={(e) => setTitle(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="Ex: Novo episódio disponível!"
                  required
                  className="w-full bg-bg border border-border text-text rounded-lg py-2 px-3 focus:outline-none focus:border-accent"
                />
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="text-sm font-semibold text-text mb-1 block">Mensagem</label>
                <textarea
                  value={message}
                  onChange={(e) => setMessage(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="Ex: O episódio 5 de One Piece acabou de sair!"
                  required
                  rows={3}
                  className="w-full bg-bg border border-border text-text rounded-lg py-2 px-3 focus:outline-none focus:border-accent resize-none"
                />
              </div>

              <button
                type="submit"
                disabled={loading}
                className="w-full bg-accent text-white font-bold py-3 rounded-lg hover:bg-accent/90 transition-all flex justify-center items-center gap-2 disabled:opacity-50 cursor-pointer mt-2"
              >
                {loading ? <Loader2 className="animate-spin" size={20} /> : 'Enviar Notificação'}
              </button>
            </form>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </>
  );
}

===== C:\anime-site\components\admin\CardFeedback.tsx =====
'use client';

import { useState } from 'react';
import { CheckCircle2, Circle, Trash2, Loader2 } from 'lucide-react';
import { deletarFeedback, toggleResolvido } from '@/app/actions/feedbacks';

interface Feedback {
  id: string;
  type: string;
  message: string;
  is_read: boolean;
  created_at: string;
  profiles: { username: string } | null;
}

export function CardFeedback({ feedback }: { feedback: Feedback }) {
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);

  const handleDeletar = async () => {
    if (!confirm('Tem certeza que deseja deletar este feedback?')) return;
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      await deletarFeedback(feedback.id);
    } catch (error: any) {
      alert(error.message);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const handleToggleResolvido = async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      await toggleResolvido(feedback.id, feedback.is_read);
    } catch (error: any) {
      alert(error.message);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className={`p-4 border-b border-border last:border-0 flex flex-col gap-2 ${feedback.is_read ? 'opacity-60 bg-bg/30' : ''}`}>
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <span className="font-bold text-sm text-text">{feedback.profiles?.username || 'Anônimo'}</span>
          <span className={`text-[10px] px-2 py-0.5 rounded font-bold uppercase ${
            feedback.type === 'bug' ? 'bg-red-500/20 text-red-500' : 
            feedback.type === 'denuncia' ? 'bg-orange-500/20 text-orange-500' : 
            'bg-accent/20 text-accent'
          }`}>
            {feedback.type}
          </span>
          {feedback.is_read && (
            <span className="text-[10px] text-green-500 font-bold flex items-center gap-1">
              <CheckCircle2 size={12} /> Resolvido
            </span>
          )}
        </div>
        
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <button 
            onClick={handleToggleResolvido} 
            disabled={loading}
            title={feedback.is_read ? "Marcar como não resolvido" : "Marcar como resolvido"}
            className="text-muted hover:text-green-500 transition-colors p-1"
          >
            {feedback.is_read ? <Circle size={18} /> : <CheckCircle2 size={18} />}
          </button>
          <button 
            onClick={handleDeletar} 
            disabled={loading}
            title="Deletar feedback"
            className="text-muted hover:text-red-500 transition-colors p-1"
          >
            {loading ? <Loader2 className="animate-spin" size={18} /> : <Trash2 size={18} />}
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
      <p className="text-sm text-muted">{feedback.message}</p>
      <span className="text-xs text-muted/50">
        {new Date(feedback.created_at).toLocaleDateString('pt-BR')}
      </span>
    </div>
  );
}

===== C:\anime-site\components\admin\GerenciarStreaming.tsx =====
'use client';

import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { createClient } from '@/lib/supabase/client';
import { Loader2, EyeOff, Eye } from 'lucide-react';
import Image from 'next/image';

interface AnimeData {
  id: number;
  title: string;
  coverUrl: string;
}

export function GerenciarStreaming() {
  const [animes, setAnimes] = useState<AnimeData[]>([]);
  const [inativos, setInativos] = useState<Record<number, string[]>>({});
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const supabase = createClient();

  const plataformas = [
    { id: 'crunchyroll', nome: 'Crunchyroll' },
    { id: 'prime', nome: 'Prime Video' },
    { id: 'disney', nome: 'Disney+' },
    { id: 'netflix', nome: 'Netflix' },
    { id: 'max', nome: 'HBO Max' },
  ];

  // Dias da semana para buscar os animes
  const dias = ['dom', 'seg', 'ter', 'qua', 'qui', 'sex', 'sab'];

  useEffect(() => {
    const fetchAnimes = async () => {
      try {
        let todosAnimes: AnimeData[] = [];

        // Busca os animes de cada dia da semana
        for (const dia of dias) {
          const res = await fetch(`/api/test-anilist?dia=${dia}`);
          if (res.ok) {
            const data = await res.json();
            if (data.animesDeHoje) {
              todosAnimes = [...todosAnimes, ...data.animesDeHoje];
            }
          }
        }

        // Remove duplicatas (animes que aparecem em mais de um dia)
        const animesUnicos = Array.from(
          new Map(todosAnimes.map((a) => [a.id, a])).values()
        );

        setAnimes(animesUnicos);
      } catch (error) {
        console.error('Erro ao buscar animes:', error);
      }
    };

    const fetchInativos = async () => {
      const { data } = await supabase.from('anime_streaming_inativos').select('*');
      if (data) {
        const map: Record<number, string[]> = {};
        data.forEach((item) => {
          if (!map[item.anime_id]) map[item.anime_id] = [];
          map[item.anime_id].push(item.plataforma);
        });
        setInativos(map);
      }
      setLoading(false);
    };

    fetchAnimes();
    fetchInativos();
  }, [supabase]);

  const toggleInativo = async (animeId: number, plataformaId: string) => {
    const isInativo = inativos[animeId]?.includes(plataformaId) || false;

    if (isInativo) {
      // Remove da lista de inativos (reativa)
      await supabase
        .from('anime_streaming_inativos')
        .delete()
        .eq('anime_id', animeId)
        .eq('plataforma', plataformaId);
      setInativos({
        ...inativos,
        [animeId]: inativos[animeId].filter((id) => id !== plataformaId),
      });
    } else {
      // Adiciona na lista de inativos
      await supabase
        .from('anime_streaming_inativos')
        .insert({ anime_id: animeId, plataforma: plataformaId });
      setInativos({
        ...inativos,
        [animeId]: [...(inativos[animeId] || []), plataformaId],
      });
    }
  };

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <div className="flex justify-center py-4">
        <Loader2 className="animate-spin text-accent" />
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="space-y-4 max-h-[500px] overflow-y-auto pr-2">
      {animes.length === 0 ? (
        <p className="text-muted text-sm">Nenhum anime encontrado.</p>
      ) : (
        animes.map((anime) => (
          <div
            key={anime.id}
            className="flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-center justify-between p-3 border border-border rounded-lg bg-bg/50 gap-4"
          >
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
              <div className="relative w-10 h-14 rounded-md overflow-hidden flex-shrink-0">
                <Image src={anime.coverUrl} alt={anime.title} fill className="object-cover" />
              </div>
              <div>
                <p className="text-sm font-bold text-text line-clamp-1">{anime.title}</p>
                <p className="text-xs text-muted">ID: {anime.id}</p>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
              {plataformas.map((p) => {
                const isAtivo = !inativos[anime.id]?.includes(p.id);
                return (
                  <button
                    key={p.id}
                    onClick={() => toggleInativo(anime.id, p.id)}
                    className={`flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-1 rounded text-xs font-bold transition-colors ${
                      isAtivo
                        ? 'bg-green-500/10 text-green-500 border border-green-500/30'
                        : 'bg-red-500/10 text-red-500 border border-red-500/30 opacity-50'
                    }`}
                  >
                    {p.nome}
                    {isAtivo ? <Eye size={12} /> : <EyeOff size={12} />}
                  </button>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          </div>
        ))
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

===== C:\anime-site\components\admin\ToggleChat.tsx =====
'use client';
import { useState } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';

export function ToggleChat({ inicial }: { inicial: boolean }) {
  const [enabled, setEnabled] = useState(inicial);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const router = useRouter();

  const toggle = async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    await fetch('/api/admin/toggle-chat', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ enabled: !enabled })
    });
    setEnabled(!enabled);
    setLoading(false);
    router.refresh();
  };

  return (
    <button 
      onClick={toggle} 
      disabled={loading} 
      className={`px-6 py-3 rounded-lg font-bold text-white transition-all cursor-pointer ${
        enabled ? 'bg-red-500 hover:bg-red-600' : 'bg-green-500 hover:bg-green-600'
      }`}
    >
      {loading ? 'Aguarde...' : enabled ? 'Desligar Chat' : 'Ligar Chat'}
    </button>
  );
}

===== C:\anime-site\components\admin\ToggleFeed.tsx =====
'use client';

import { useState } from 'react';
import { Loader2 } from 'lucide-react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';

export function ToggleFeed({ inicial }: { inicial: boolean }) {
  const [enabled, setEnabled] = useState(inicial);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const router = useRouter();

  const toggle = async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    
    // Atualiza visualmente na hora para ficar rápido
    const newState = !enabled;
    setEnabled(newState);

    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/admin/toggle-feed', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        // Manda a chave "enabled" (é isso que a API espera)
        body: JSON.stringify({ enabled: newState }), 
      });

      if (!res.ok) {
        // Se der erro, volta pro estado anterior
        setEnabled(!newState); 
        alert('Erro ao salvar no banco.');
      } else {
        // Mágica: O Next.js busca os dados do servidor novamente (força a atualização)
        router.refresh(); 
      }
    } catch (error) {
      setEnabled(!newState);
      alert('Erro de conexão.');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <button
      onClick={toggle}
      disabled={loading}
      className={`px-6 py-2 rounded-lg font-bold text-white transition-colors disabled:opacity-50 ${
        enabled ? 'bg-red-500 hover:bg-red-600' : 'bg-green-500 hover:bg-green-600'
      }`}
    >
      {loading ? <Loader2 className="animate-spin" size={20} /> : enabled ? 'Bloquear Feed' : 'Liberar Feed'}
    </button>
  );
}

===== C:\anime-site\components\anime\AcoesAnime.tsx =====
'use client';

import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { PlayCircle, Bookmark, CheckCircle2, Star, Loader2 } from 'lucide-react';
import { createClient } from '@/lib/supabase/client';

export function AcoesAnime({ animeId }: { animeId: number }) {
  const supabase = createClient();
  const [status, setStatus] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [nota, setNota] = useState<number>(0);
  const [hoverNota, setHoverNota] = useState<number>(0);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [isSaving, setIsSaving] = useState(false);
  const [userId, setUserId] = useState<string | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    const fetchUserData = async () => {
      const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
      if (!user) { setLoading(false); return; }
      setUserId(user.id);

      const { data } = await supabase
        .from('user_anime')
        .select('status, rating')
        .eq('user_id', user.id)
        .eq('anime_id', animeId)
        .single();

      if (data) {
        setStatus(data.status);
        setNota(data.rating || 0);
      }
      setLoading(false);
    };
    fetchUserData();
  }, [animeId, supabase]);

  const salvarNoBanco = async (novoStatus: string | null, novaNota: number) => {
    if (!userId) return;
    setIsSaving(true);
    await supabase.from('user_anime').upsert({
      user_id: userId, anime_id: animeId, status: novoStatus, rating: novaNota, updated_at: new Date().toISOString()
    }, { onConflict: 'user_id,anime_id' });
    setIsSaving(false);
  };

  const lidarComStatus = async (novoStatus: string) => {
    const statusFinal = status === novoStatus ? null : novoStatus;
    setStatus(statusFinal);
    await salvarNoBanco(statusFinal, nota);
  };

  const lidarComNota = async (novaNota: number) => {
    const notaFinal = nota === novaNota ? 0 : novaNota;
    setNota(notaFinal);
    await salvarNoBanco(status, notaFinal);
  };

  if (loading) return <div className="h-[100px] flex items-center text-muted"><Loader2 className="animate-spin mr-2" /> Carregando...</div>;

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col w-full relative">
      {!userId && (
        <div className="absolute inset-0 z-10 bg-bg/50 backdrop-blur-sm rounded-lg flex items-center justify-center border border-border">
          <span className="text-sm font-medium text-text">Faça login para salvar.</span>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* REDUZIDO O GAP DE 4 PARA 2 */}
      <div className={`transition-opacity duration-300 ${!userId ? 'opacity-30 pointer-events-none' : ''} flex flex-col gap-2 w-full`}>
        
        {/* 1. BLOCO DE NOTA */}
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <span className="text-sm font-medium text-muted">Sua nota:</span>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1">
            {[2, 4, 6, 8, 10].map((valorEstrela) => (
              <button
                key={valorEstrela}
                onMouseEnter={() => setHoverNota(valorEstrela)}
                onMouseLeave={() => setHoverNota(0)}
                onClick={() => lidarComNota(valorEstrela)}
                className="transition-transform hover:scale-110 cursor-pointer"
              >
                <Star 
                  size={24} 
                  className={`${
                    valorEstrela <= (hoverNota || nota) 
                      ? 'text-yellow-400 fill-yellow-400' 
                      : 'text-border fill-transparent'
                  } transition-colors`} 
                />
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
          <span className="text-sm font-bold text-text ml-1">{nota > 0 ? nota : ''}</span>
          {isSaving && <Loader2 className="animate-spin text-accent" size={16} />}
        </div>

        {/* 2. GRUPO DE STATUS */}
        <div className="flex items-center gap-6 border-b border-border pb-2 w-fit">
          <button
            onClick={() => lidarComStatus('assistindo')}
            className={`flex items-center gap-2 pb-2 text-sm font-bold transition-all cursor-pointer border-b-2 ${
              status === 'assistindo' 
                ? 'text-accent border-accent' 
                : 'text-muted border-transparent hover:text-text'
            }`}
          >
            <PlayCircle size={16} /> Assistindo
          </button>
          <button
            onClick={() => lidarComStatus('quero')}
            className={`flex items-center gap-2 pb-2 text-sm font-bold transition-all cursor-pointer border-b-2 ${
              status === 'quero' 
                ? 'text-yellow-500 border-yellow-500' 
                : 'text-muted border-transparent hover:text-text'
            }`}
          >
            <Bookmark size={16} /> Quero Assistir
          </button>
          <button
            onClick={() => lidarComStatus('concluido')}
            className={`flex items-center gap-2 pb-2 text-sm font-bold transition-all cursor-pointer border-b-2 ${
              status === 'concluido' 
                ? 'text-green-500 border-green-500' 
                : 'text-muted border-transparent hover:text-text'
            }`}
          >
            <CheckCircle2 size={16} /> Concluído
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

===== C:\anime-site\components\anime\BotaoCompartilhar.tsx =====
'use client';

import { Share2 } from 'lucide-react';

export function BotaoCompartilhar({ titulo }: { titulo: string }) {
  const handleCompartilhar = async () => {
    const url = window.location.href;
    const shareData = {
      title: `AniVerse | ${titulo}`,
      text: `Olha esse anime no AniVerse: ${titulo}`,
      url: url,
    };

    try {
      if (navigator.share) {
        await navigator.share(shareData);
      } else {
        await navigator.clipboard.writeText(url);
        alert('Link copiado para a área de transferência!');
      }
    } catch (error) {
      console.log('Compartilhamento cancelado ou falhou', error);
    }
  };

  return (
    <button
      onClick={handleCompartilhar}
      className=" mt-0 mb-1 flex items-center justify-center gap-2 bg-surface border border-border text-text font-bold px-6 py-3 rounded-xl hover:border-accent hover:text-accent transition-all shadow-sm"
      title="Compartilhar este anime"
    >
      <Share2 size={20} />
      <span>Compartilhar</span>
    </button>
  );
}

            {/* O Player */}

              <iframe
                src={`https://www.youtube.com/embed/${trailerId}?autoplay=1&color=white`}
                title="Trailer do Anime"
                allow="accelerometer; autoplay; clipboard-write; encrypted-media; gyroscope; picture-in-picture"
                allowFullScreen
                className="w-full h-full"
              />
            </div>

          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </>
  );
}

===== C:\anime-site\components\anime\OndeAssistir.tsx =====
'use client';

import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { PlayCircle, ExternalLink, AlertCircle, Loader2 } from 'lucide-react';
import { createClient } from '@/lib/supabase/client';

export function OndeAssistir({ tituloAnime, animeId }: { tituloAnime: string; animeId: number }) {
  const [plataformasInativas, setPlataformasInativas] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const supabase = createClient();

  const criarUrl = (baseUrl: string) => `${baseUrl}${encodeURIComponent(tituloAnime)}`;

  // Cores mais escuras para garantir contraste
  const todasPlataformas = [
    { id: 'crunchyroll', nome: 'Crunchyroll', url: criarUrl('https://www.crunchyroll.com/pt-br/search?q='), cor: 'bg-[#E65100]' },
    { id: 'prime', nome: 'Prime Video', url: `https://www.primevideo.com/search?phrase=${encodeURIComponent(tituloAnime)}&tag=aniverseofici-20`, cor: 'bg-[#005A9E]' },
    { id: 'disney', nome: 'Disney+', url: 'https://grfpr.com/g/z1w3b3celh62a5c9a7d55c1634180b/', cor: 'bg-[#0D2A8A]' },
    { id: 'netflix', nome: 'Netflix', url: criarUrl('https://www.netflix.com/search?q='), cor: 'bg-[#B20710]' },
    { id: 'max', nome: 'HBO Max', url: criarUrl('https://www.max.com/br/pt/search?q='), cor: 'bg-[#3E1A6E]' },
  ];

  useEffect(() => {
    const fetchInativos = async () => {
      const { data } = await supabase
        .from('anime_streaming_inativos')
        .select('plataforma')
        .eq('anime_id', animeId);

      if (data) {
        setPlataformasInativas(data.map((d) => d.plataforma));
      }
      setLoading(false);
    };
    fetchInativos();
  }, [animeId, supabase]);

  if (loading) {
    return <div className="bg-accent/5 border border-accent/20 rounded-xl p-4 w-full max-w-md flex justify-center"><Loader2 className="animate-spin text-accent" /></div>;
  }

  const plataformasParaMostrar = todasPlataformas.filter(p => !plataformasInativas.includes(p.id));

  return (
    <div className="bg-accent/5 border border-accent/20 rounded-xl p-4 w-full max-w-md">
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-3">
        <PlayCircle className="text-accent" size={20} />
        <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-text">Onde assistir "{tituloAnime}"?</h3>
      </div>
      
      <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
        {plataformasParaMostrar.map((p) => (
          <a
            key={p.id}
            href={p.url}
            target="_blank"
            rel="noopener noreferrer"
            className={`${p.cor} text-white text-sm font-bold px-4 py-2 rounded-lg hover:opacity-90 transition-opacity flex items-center gap-2`}
          >
            {p.nome}
            <ExternalLink size={14} className="opacity-70" />
          </a>
        ))}
      </div>
      
      <p className="text-xs text-text/70 mt-3">
        A disponibilidade pode variar. Clique para buscar no serviço.
      </p>
    </div>
  );
}

===== C:\anime-site\components\anime\SecaoComentarios.tsx =====
'use client';

import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { createClient } from '@/lib/supabase/client';
import { Loader2, Trash2, Eye, EyeOff } from 'lucide-react';

interface Comment {
  id: string;
  content: string;
  has_spoiler: boolean;
  created_at: string;
  user_id: string;
  profiles: { username: string };
}

export function SecaoComentarios({ animeId }: { animeId: number }) {
  const supabase = createClient();
  const [comments, setComments] = useState<Comment[]>([]);
  const [newComment, setNewComment] = useState('');
  const [isSpoiler, setIsSpoiler] = useState(false);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [userId, setUserId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [isSubmitting, setIsSubmitting] = useState(false);

  // Estados para mostrar/ocultar spoiler individualmente
  const [spoilersAbertos, setSpoilersAbertos] = useState<Record<string, boolean>>({});

  useEffect(() => {
    const fetchUser = async () => {
      const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
      setUserId(user?.id || null);
    };
    fetchUser();

    // Buscar comentários
    const fetchComments = async () => {
      const res = await fetch(`/api/comments?animeId=${animeId}`);
      if (res.ok) {
        const data = await res.json();
        setComments(data);
      }
      setLoading(false);
    };
    fetchComments();
  }, [animeId, supabase]);

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!newComment.trim() || !userId) return;

    setIsSubmitting(true);
    const { error } = await supabase
      .from('comments')
      .insert({
        user_id: userId,
        anime_id: animeId,
        content: newComment.trim(),
        has_spoiler: isSpoiler
      });

    if (!error) {
      setNewComment('');
      setIsSpoiler(false);
      
      // Recarrega os comentários
      const res = await fetch(`/api/comments?animeId=${animeId}`);
      if (res.ok) {
        const data = await res.json();
        setComments(data);
      }
    } else {
      alert("Erro ao enviar comentário. Tente novamente.");
    }
    setIsSubmitting(false);
  };

  const handleDelete = async (id: string) => {
    if (!confirm("Tem certeza que deseja apagar este comentário?")) return;

    const res = await fetch(`/api/comments/${id}`, { method: 'DELETE' });
    if (res.ok) {
      setComments(comments.filter(c => c.id !== id));
    } else {
      alert("Erro ao apagar comentário.");
    }
  };

  const toggleSpoiler = (id: string) => {
    setSpoilersAbertos(prev => ({ ...prev, [id]: !prev[id] }));
  };

  return (
    <div className="mt-12 bg-surface/50 border border-border rounded-xl p-6 md:p-8">
      <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold text-text mb-6">Discussão</h2>

      {/* FORMULÁRIO DE NOVO COMENTÁRIO */}
      {userId ? (
        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="mb-8">
          <textarea
            value={newComment}
            onChange={(e) => setNewComment(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="O que você achou deste anime/episódio?"
            rows={3}
            className="w-full bg-bg border border-border text-text rounded-lg p-4 focus:outline-none focus:border-accent resize-none mb-3"
          />
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-4">
            <label className="flex items-center gap-2 cursor-pointer text-sm text-muted hover:text-text transition-colors">
              <input
                type="checkbox"
                checked={isSpoiler}
                onChange={(e) => setIsSpoiler(e.target.checked)}
                className="w-4 h-4 rounded border-border text-accent focus:ring-accent bg-bg cursor-pointer"
              />
              Este comentário contém spoiler
            </label>
            <button
              type="submit"
              disabled={isSubmitting || !newComment.trim()}
              className="flex items-center gap-2 bg-accent text-white px-6 py-2 rounded-lg font-bold hover:bg-accent/90 transition-colors disabled:opacity-50 cursor-pointer"
            >
              {isSubmitting && <Loader2 size={16} className="animate-spin" />}
              Comentar
            </button>
          </div>
        </form>
      ) : (
        <div className="bg-bg border border-border rounded-lg p-4 text-center mb-8">
          <p className="text-muted text-sm">Faça login para participar da discussão.</p>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* LISTA DE COMENTÁRIOS */}
      {loading ? (
        <div className="flex justify-center py-8"><Loader2 className="animate-spin text-accent" /></div>
      ) : comments.length === 0 ? (
        <p className="text-muted text-center py-8">Nenhum comentário ainda. Seja o primeiro!</p>
      ) : (
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
          {comments.map((comment) => {
            const isSpoilerAberto = spoilersAbertos[comment.id];

            return (
              <div key={comment.id} className="bg-bg border border-border rounded-lg p-4">
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-2">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                    <span className="font-bold text-accent text-sm">
                      {comment.profiles?.username || 'Usuário'}
                    </span>
                    <span className="text-muted text-xs">
                      {new Date(comment.created_at).toLocaleDateString('pt-BR')}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                  
                  {userId === comment.user_id && (
                    <button
                      onClick={() => handleDelete(comment.id)}
                      className="text-muted hover:text-red-400 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
                      title="Apagar comentário"
                    >
                      <Trash2 size={16} />
                    </button>
                  )}
                </div>

                {/* CONTEÚDO COM BLUR ANTI-SPOILER */}
                <div className="relative">
                  <p className={`text-text text-sm leading-relaxed ${comment.has_spoiler && !isSpoilerAberto ? 'blur-sm select-none' : ''}`}>
                    {comment.content}
                  </p>
                  
                  {comment.has_spoiler && !isSpoilerAberto && (
                    <button
                      onClick={() => toggleSpoiler(comment.id)}
                      className="absolute inset-0 w-full h-full flex items-center justify-center gap-2 text-accent font-bold text-sm bg-bg/50 rounded cursor-pointer"
                    >
                      <Eye size={16} /> Mostrar Spoiler
                    </button>
                  )}
                  {comment.has_spoiler && isSpoilerAberto && (
                    <button
                      onClick={() => toggleSpoiler(comment.id)}
                      className="mt-2 text-xs text-muted hover:text-accent flex items-center gap-1 cursor-pointer"
                    >
                      <EyeOff size={14} /> Ocultar Spoiler
                    </button>
                  )}
                </div>
              </div>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

===== C:\anime-site\components\feed\CarrosselImagens.tsx =====
'use client';

import { useState } from 'react';
import { ChevronLeft, ChevronRight } from 'lucide-react';
import Image from 'next/image';

export function CarrosselImagens({ imagens, isRanking = false }: { imagens: string[], isRanking?: boolean }) {
  const [index, setIndex] = useState(0);

  if (!imagens || imagens.length === 0) return null;

  const next = () => setIndex((prev) => (prev + 1) % imagens.length);
  const prev = () => setIndex((prev) => (prev - 1 + imagens.length) % imagens.length);

  return (
    <div className="relative w-full aspect-[2/3] bg-black overflow-hidden group">
      
      <Image
        src={imagens[index]}
        alt={`Imagem ${index + 1}`}
        fill
        sizes="(max-width: 768px) 100vw, (max-width: 1200px) 50vw, 33vw"
        className="object-cover"
        priority={index === 0}
      />

      {/* O NÚMERO DA POSIÇÃO (SÓ APARECE SE FOR RANKING) */}
      {isRanking && (
        <div className="absolute top-3 left-3 bg-black/70 backdrop-blur-md text-white font-extrabold text-lg w-10 h-10 flex items-center justify-center rounded-xl border border-white/20 z-10 shadow-lg">
          {index + 1}º
        </div>
      )}

      {imagens.length > 1 && (
        <>
          <button 
            onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); prev(); }} 
            className="absolute left-2 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 bg-black/50 text-white p-2 rounded-full hover:bg-black/80 transition-colors cursor-pointer opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 z-10"
          >
            <ChevronLeft size={20} />
          </button>
          
          <button 
            onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); next(); }} 
            className="absolute right-2 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 bg-black/50 text-white p-2 rounded-full hover:bg-black/80 transition-colors cursor-pointer opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 z-10"
          >
            <ChevronRight size={20} />
          </button>
          
          <div className="absolute bottom-3 left-0 right-0 flex justify-center gap-1.5 z-10">
            {imagens.map((_, i) => (
              <div key={i} className={`w-2 h-2 rounded-full transition-colors ${i === index ? 'bg-white' : 'bg-white/50'}`} />
            ))}
          </div>
        </>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

===== C:\anime-site\components\feed\CriadorPost.tsx =====
'use client';

import { useState, useRef } from 'react';
import { createClient } from '@/lib/supabase/client';
import { Image as ImageIcon, X, Loader2, Send, ListOrdered } from 'lucide-react';
import Image from 'next/image';

export function CriadorPost({ onClose }: { onClose: () => void }) {
  const [legenda, setLegenda] = useState('');
  const [files, setFiles] = useState<File[]>([]);
  const [previews, setPreviews] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [isRanking, setIsRanking] = useState(false); // Estado do checkbox
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [erro, setErro] = useState('');
  
  const inputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
  const supabase = createClient();

  const podePostar = (legenda.trim().length > 0 || files.length > 0) && !loading;

  const handleSelecionarImagens = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    const selectedFiles = Array.from(e.target.files || []);
    if (selectedFiles.length === 0) return;

    const validFiles = selectedFiles.filter(f => f.size <= 5 * 1024 * 1024);
    if (validFiles.length !== selectedFiles.length) {
      setErro('Algumas imagens ultrapassaram 5MB e foram ignoradas.');
    }

    const newPreviews = validFiles.map(file => URL.createObjectURL(file));
    
    setFiles(prev => [...prev, ...validFiles]);
    setPreviews(prev => [...prev, ...newPreviews]);
  };

  const removerImagem = (index: number) => {
    setFiles(prev => prev.filter((_, i) => i !== index));
    setPreviews(prev => prev.filter((_, i) => i !== index));
  };

  const handlePostar = async () => {
    if (!podePostar) return;

    setLoading(true);
    setErro('');

    try {
      const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
      if (!user) throw new Error('Você precisa estar logado.');

      const mediaUrls: string[] = [];

      for (const file of files) {
        const fileName = `post-${user.id}-${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(36).substring(7)}.png`;
        const { error: uploadError } = await supabase.storage.from('feed_media').upload(fileName, file);
        if (uploadError) throw new Error(`Falha no upload: ${uploadError.message}`);

        const { data: { publicUrl } } = supabase.storage.from('feed_media').getPublicUrl(fileName);
        mediaUrls.push(publicUrl);
      }

      const { error: postError } = await supabase.from('posts').insert({
        user_id: user.id,
        media_url: mediaUrls[0] || null,
        media_urls: mediaUrls.length > 0 ? mediaUrls : null,
        media_type: mediaUrls.length > 0 ? 'image' : 'text', 
        caption: legenda,
        is_ranking: isRanking && mediaUrls.length > 0, // Só é ranking se tiver imagem
      });

      if (postError) throw new Error(postError.message);

      onClose();
      window.location.reload();

    } catch (error: any) {
      setErro(error.message || 'Erro ao postar.');
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-[200] bg-black/80 flex items-center justify-center p-4 backdrop-blur-sm" onClick={onClose}>
      <div className="w-full max-w-lg bg-surface rounded-2xl overflow-hidden shadow-2xl" onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}>
        
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between p-4 border-b border-border">
          <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-text">Criar Novo Post</h2>
          <button onClick={onClose} className="text-muted hover:text-red-500"><X size={24} /></button>
        </div>

        <div className="p-4 space-y-4 max-h-[70vh] overflow-y-auto">
          {erro && <div className="bg-red-500/10 text-red-500 p-2 rounded text-sm">{erro}</div>}

          <textarea
            value={legenda}
            onChange={(e) => setLegenda(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="O que você está pensando sobre animes?"
            rows={3}
            className="w-full bg-bg border border-border text-text rounded-xl p-3 focus:outline-none focus:border-accent resize-none"
          />

          {previews.length > 0 && (
            <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2">
              {previews.map((preview, index) => (
                <div key={index} className="relative aspect-square rounded-lg overflow-hidden bg-black">
                  <Image src={preview} alt={`Preview ${index}`} fill className="object-cover" />
                  <button 
                    onClick={() => removerImagem(index)}
                    className="absolute top-1 right-1 bg-black/70 text-white p-1 rounded-full hover:bg-red-500"
                  >
                    <X size={12} />
                  </button>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          )}

          <button 
            onClick={() => inputRef.current?.click()}
            className="w-full h-24 border-2 border-dashed border-border rounded-xl flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-2 text-muted hover:border-accent hover:text-accent transition-colors"
          >
            <ImageIcon size={24} />
            <span className="text-sm font-medium">Adicionar Imagens (Opcional)</span>
          </button>

          <input 
            type="file" 
            accept="image/*" 
            multiple 
            ref={inputRef} 
            onChange={handleSelecionarImagens} 
            className="hidden" 
          />
        </div>

        {/* RODAPÉ COM O CHECKBOX E O BOTÃO POSTAR */}
        <div className="p-4 border-t border-border flex flex-col gap-3">
          
          {/* CHECKBOX MOVIDO PARA CÁ */}
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <input 
              type="checkbox" 
              id="ranking-check"
              checked={isRanking}
              onChange={(e) => setIsRanking(e.target.checked)}
              className="w-4 h-4 accent-accent cursor-pointer"
            />
            <label htmlFor="ranking-check" className="text-sm text-text cursor-pointer flex items-center gap-2 select-none">
              <ListOrdered size={16} className="text-accent" />
              Numerar as imagens (Ranking)
            </label>
          </div>

          <div className="flex justify-end">
            <button
              onClick={handlePostar}
              disabled={!podePostar}
              className={`font-bold px-6 py-2 rounded-lg transition-colors flex items-center gap-2 ${
                podePostar 
                  ? 'bg-accent text-white hover:bg-accent/90 cursor-pointer' 
                  : 'bg-accent/50 text-white/70 cursor-not-allowed'
              }`}
            >
              {loading ? <Loader2 className="animate-spin" size={18} /> : <Send size={18} />}
              {loading ? 'Postando...' : 'Postar'}
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

===== C:\anime-site\components\feed\ModalComentarios.tsx =====
'use client';

import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { createClient } from '@/lib/supabase/client';
import { X, Send, Loader2 } from 'lucide-react';
import Image from 'next/image';

interface Post { id: string; }

export function ModalComentarios({ post, onClose }: { post: Post, onClose: () => void }) {
  const supabase = createClient();
  const [comentarios, setComentarios] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [novoComentario, setNovoComentario] = useState('');
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [carregandoInicial, setCarregandoInicial] = useState(true);
  
  useEffect(() => {
    const fetchComentarios = async () => {
      const { data } = await supabase
        .from('post_comments')
        .select(`id, content, created_at, profiles (username, avatar_url)`)
        .eq('post_id', post.id)
        .order('created_at', { ascending: true });
      
      if (data) setComentarios(data);
      setCarregandoInicial(false);
    };
    fetchComentarios();
  }, [post.id]);

  const handleEnviarComentario = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!novoComentario.trim()) return;
    setLoading(true);

    const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
    if (!user) { alert('Faça login para comentar.'); setLoading(false); return; }

    const { data, error } = await supabase.from('post_comments').insert({
      post_id: post.id,
      user_id: user.id,
      content: novoComentario
    }).select(`id, content, created_at, profiles (username, avatar_url)`).single();

    if (!error && data) {
      setComentarios([...comentarios, data]);
      setNovoComentario('');
    } else {
      alert('Erro ao comentar.');
    }
    setLoading(false);
  };

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-[200] bg-black/80 flex items-center justify-center p-4 backdrop-blur-sm" onClick={onClose}>
      <div className="w-full max-w-lg bg-surface rounded-xl flex flex-col max-h-[85vh]" onClick={e => e.stopPropagation()}>
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between p-4 border-b border-border">
          <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-text">Comentários</h2>
          <button onClick={onClose} className="text-muted hover:text-text"><X size={24} /></button>
        </div>

        <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-4 space-y-4">
          {carregandoInicial ? (
            <div className="flex justify-center"><Loader2 className="animate-spin text-accent" /></div>
          ) : comentarios.length === 0 ? (
            <p className="text-center text-muted text-sm py-8">Nenhum comentário ainda. Seja o primeiro!</p>
          ) : (
            comentarios.map((c) => (
              <div key={c.id} className="flex gap-3">
                <div className="relative w-8 h-8 rounded-full overflow-hidden bg-border shrink-0">
                  {c.profiles?.avatar_url ? (
                    <Image src={c.profiles.avatar_url} alt="Avatar" fill className="object-cover" />
                  ) : (
                    <div className="w-full h-full flex items-center justify-center text-xs font-bold">{c.profiles?.username?.charAt(0).toUpperCase()}</div>
                  )}
                </div>
                <div>
                  <p className="text-sm font-bold text-text">{c.profiles?.username}</p>
                  <p className="text-sm text-muted">{c.content}</p>
                </div>
              </div>
            ))
          )}
        </div>

        <form onSubmit={handleEnviarComentario} className="p-3 border-t border-border flex gap-2">
          <input 
            type="text" 
            value={novoComentario}
            onChange={e => setNovoComentario(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="Adicione um comentário..."
            className="flex-1 bg-background border border-border rounded-lg px-4 py-2 text-sm text-text focus:outline-none focus:border-accent"
          />
          <button type="submit" disabled={loading || !novoComentario.trim()} className="bg-accent text-white p-2 rounded-lg disabled:opacity-50 hover:bg-accent/90 transition-colors">
            {loading ? <Loader2 className="animate-spin" size={20} /> : <Send size={20} />}
          </button>
        </form>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

===== C:\anime-site\components\feed\TopCriadores.tsx =====
'use client';

import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { createClient } from '@/lib/supabase/client';
import { Crown, Medal, Award, Loader2 } from 'lucide-react';
import Image from 'next/image';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { SeloAdmin } from '../layout/SeloAdmin';

interface Criador {
  user_id: string;
  username: string;
  avatar_url: string;
  is_admin: boolean;
  total_likes: number;
  total_comments: number;
  pontuacao_total: number;
  total_posts: number;
}

export function TopCriadores() {
  const [criadores, setCriadores] = useState<Criador[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const supabase = createClient();

  useEffect(() => {
    const fetchRanking = async () => {
      const { data } = await supabase.from('ranking_criadores').select('*');
      if (data) setCriadores(data);
      setLoading(false);
    };
    fetchRanking();
  }, [supabase]);

  if (loading) return <div className="flex justify-center py-4"><Loader2 className="animate-spin text-accent" size={24} /></div>;
  if (criadores.length === 0) return null;

  return (
    <div className="bg-surface border border-border rounded-2xl p-6 shadow-sm">
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-6">
        <Crown className="text-yellow-500" size={24} />
        <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-text">Top Criadores da Semana</h2>
      </div>

      <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
        {criadores.map((user, index) => {
          let medalhaIcon = null;
          let medalhaCor = '';
          
          if (index === 0) { medalhaIcon = <Crown size={18} />; medalhaCor = 'text-yellow-400'; }
          else if (index === 1) { medalhaIcon = <Medal size={18} />; medalhaCor = 'text-gray-300'; }
          else if (index === 2) { medalhaIcon = <Medal size={18} />; medalhaCor = 'text-amber-600'; }
          else { medalhaIcon = <Award size={18} />; medalhaCor = 'text-muted'; }

          return (
            <Link 
              key={user.user_id} 
              href={`/perfil/${user.username}`}
              className="flex items-center gap-3 p-2 rounded-xl hover:bg-bg transition-colors group"
            >
              <div className={`flex items-center justify-center w-8 h-8 rounded-full font-bold ${index < 3 ? 'bg-bg border border-border shadow-sm' : ''}`}>
                <span className={medalhaCor}>{index + 1}º</span>
              </div>
              
              <div className="relative w-10 h-10 rounded-full overflow-hidden bg-border shrink-0 group-hover:ring-2 group-hover:ring-accent transition-all">
                {user.avatar_url ? (
                  <Image src={user.avatar_url} alt={user.username} fill sizes="40px" className="object-cover" />
                ) : (
                  <div className="w-full h-full flex items-center justify-center text-sm font-bold">{user.username?.charAt(0).toUpperCase()}</div>
                )}
              </div>

              <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                  <span className="font-bold text-text truncate group-hover:text-accent transition-colors">{user.username}</span>
                  {user.is_admin && <SeloAdmin />}
                </div>
                <span className="text-xs text-muted block">{user.total_posts} posts</span>
              </div>

              {/* PONTUAÇÃO TOTAL */}
              <div className="flex items-center gap-1 text-sm font-bold text-accent" title="Pontuação: curtidas (1) + comentários (2)">
                <span>{user.pontuacao_total} pts</span>
              </div>
            </Link>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

===== C:\anime-site\components\lancamentos\CardAnime.tsx =====
'use client';

import Link from 'next/link';
import Image from 'next/image';
import { Star, Clock } from 'lucide-react';

export interface AnimeData {
  id: number;
  title: string;
  coverUrl: string;
  score: number;
  episodes: number;
  formattedTime: string;
  source: string;
}

export function CardAnime({ anime }: { anime: AnimeData }) {
  return (
    <Link 
      href={`/anime/${anime.id}`} 
      className="group flex flex-col bg-surface rounded-xl overflow-hidden border border-border hover:border-accent transition-all shadow-sm"
    >
      <div className="relative aspect-[2/3] w-full overflow-hidden">
        <Image 
          src={anime.coverUrl} 
          alt={anime.title} 
          fill 
          sizes="(max-width: 768px) 50vw, 20vw" 
          className="object-cover group-hover:scale-110 transition-transform duration-300" 
        />
        
        {anime.score > 0 && (
          <div className="absolute top-2 right-2 bg-black/70 text-yellow-400 text-xs font-bold px-2 py-1 rounded-md flex items-center gap-1">
            <Star size={12} fill="currentColor" /> {anime.score}
          </div>
        )}

        <div className="absolute bottom-2 left-2 bg-accent text-white text-xs font-bold px-2 py-1 rounded-md flex items-center gap-1">
          EP {anime.episodes}
        </div>
      </div>
      
      <div className="p-3 flex-1 flex flex-col">
        {/* TÍTULO DO ANIME (H3) - Organiza a hierarquia */}
        <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-text line-clamp-2 group-hover:text-accent transition-colors">
          {anime.title}
        </h3>
        
        <div className="flex items-center gap-1 text-text/70 text-xs mt-2">
          <Clock size={12} />
          {anime.formattedTime}
        </div>
      </div>
    </Link>
  );
}

===== C:\anime-site\components\lancamentos\GridAnimes.tsx =====
import { CardAnime, AnimeData } from './CardAnime';

interface GridAnimesProps {
  animes: AnimeData[];
  isLoading: boolean;
}

export function GridAnimes({ animes, isLoading }: GridAnimesProps) {
  if (isLoading) {
    return (
      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-3 md:grid-cols-4 lg:grid-cols-5 gap-4">
        {Array.from({ length: 10 }).map((_, i) => (
          <div key={i} className="flex flex-col bg-surface rounded-xl overflow-hidden border border-border animate-pulse">
            <div className="aspect-[2/3] w-full bg-border/30" />
            <div className="p-3">
              <div className="h-4 bg-border/30 rounded w-full mb-2" />
              <div className="h-4 bg-border/30 rounded w-2/3" />
            </div>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (animes.length === 0) {
    return (
      <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center py-20 text-center">
        <span className="text-4xl mb-4">🍃</span>
        <h3 className="text-lg font-bold text-text">Nenhum lançamento</h3>
        <p className="text-muted text-sm mt-1">Não encontramos animes para este dia.</p>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-3 md:grid-cols-4 lg:grid-cols-5 gap-4">
      {animes.map((anime) => (
        <CardAnime key={anime.id} anime={anime} />
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}

===== C:\anime-site\components\lancamentos\SeletorDias.tsx =====
'use client';

import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';

const diasDaSemana = [
  { id: 'seg', label: 'Seg' },
  { id: 'ter', label: 'Ter' },
  { id: 'qua', label: 'Qua' },
  { id: 'qui', label: 'Qui' },
  { id: 'sex', label: 'Sex' },
  { id: 'sab', label: 'Sáb' },
  { id: 'dom', label: 'Dom' },
];

interface SeletorDiasProps {
  diaSelecionado: string;
  onChangeDia: (dia: string) => void;
}

export function SeletorDias({ diaSelecionado, onChangeDia }: SeletorDiasProps) {
  const [hojeId, setHojeId] = useState<string>('');

  useEffect(() => {
    const dataHoje = new Date();
    const diaSemanaString = dataHoje.toLocaleDateString('pt-BR', { 
      timeZone: 'America/Sao_Paulo', 
      weekday: 'short' 
    });
    
    const mapaDias: Record<string, string> = {
      'dom.': 'dom', 'seg.': 'seg', 'ter.': 'ter', 
      'qua.': 'qua', 'qui.': 'qui', 'sex.': 'sex', 'sáb.': 'sab'
    };
    
    setHojeId(mapaDias[diaSemanaString] || '');
  }, []);

  return (
    <div className="w-full mb-8">
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 overflow-x-auto pb-2 scrollbar-hide">
        {diasDaSemana.map((dia) => {
          const isAtivo = diaSelecionado === dia.id;
          const isHoje = hojeId === dia.id;

          return (
            <button
              key={dia.id}
              onClick={() => onChangeDia(dia.id)}
              className={`
                relative flex-shrink-0 flex flex-col items-center justify-center 
                w-16 h-16 rounded-2xl transition-all duration-200 border
                ${isAtivo 
                  ? 'bg-accent text-white border-accent shadow-lg shadow-accent/20' 
                  : 'bg-surface text-muted border-border hover:border-accent/50 hover:text-text'
                }
              `}
            >
              <span className={`text-sm font-semibold ${isAtivo ? 'text-white' : ''}`}>
                {dia.label}
              </span>
              
              {isHoje && (
                <span className={`text-[9px] font-bold mt-1 uppercase tracking-wider ${isAtivo ? 'text-white/80' : 'text-accent'}`}>
                  HOJE
                </span>
              )}
            </button>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

===== C:\anime-site\components\layout\BuscaGlobal.tsx =====
'use client';

import { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { Search, Loader2, X, PlayCircle } from 'lucide-react';
import Image from 'next/image';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { createClient } from '@/lib/supabase/client';
import { SeloAdmin } from './SeloAdmin';

export function BuscaGlobal() {
  const [aberto, setAberto] = useState(false);
  const [query, setQuery] = useState('');
  const [animes, setAnimes] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [usuarios, setUsuarios] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [buscando, setBuscando] = useState(false);
  
  const inputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
  const supabase = createClient();

  useEffect(() => {
    const handleKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if ((e.metaKey || e.ctrlKey) && e.key === 'k') {
        e.preventDefault();
        setAberto(true);
      }
      if (e.key === 'Escape') setAberto(false);
    };
    document.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
    return () => document.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (aberto) {
      setTimeout(() => inputRef.current?.focus(), 100);
    } else {
      setQuery('');
      setAnimes([]);
      setUsuarios([]);
    }
  }, [aberto]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const delayDebounceFn = setTimeout(async () => {
      if (query.trim().length < 2) {
        setAnimes([]);
        setUsuarios([]);
        return;
      }

      setBuscando(true);

      // 1. Busca Usuários no Supabase (igual antes)
      const { data: usersData } = await supabase
        .from('profiles')
        .select('id, username, avatar_url, is_admin')
        .ilike('username', `%${query}%`)
        .limit(5);
      if (usersData) setUsuarios(usersData);

      // 2. Busca Animes APENAS nos Lançamentos
      try {
        // Aqui você troca para a sua rota de lançamentos.
        // Se a sua rota for /api/destaques ou outra, é só ajustar o fetch.
        const res = await fetch('/api/lancamentos-semana'); 
        if (res.ok) {
          const data = await res.json();
          // Filtra os animes que contém o termo digitado (ignorando maiúsculas/minúsculas)
          const filtrados = data.filter((anime: any) => 
            anime.title.toLowerCase().includes(query.toLowerCase())
          );
          setAnimes(filtrados.slice(0, 5)); // Pega no máximo 5
        }
      } catch (err) {
        console.error("Erro ao buscar lançamentos:", err);
      }

      setBuscando(false);
    }, 500);

    return () => clearTimeout(delayDebounceFn);
  }, [query, supabase]);

  return (
    <>
      <button
        onClick={() => setAberto(true)}
        className="p-2 rounded-full hover:bg-bg transition-colors text-muted hover:text-accent cursor-pointer flex items-center gap-2"
      >
        <Search size={20} />
        <span className="hidden lg:flex items-center gap-1 text-xs border border-border px-2 py-1 rounded-md bg-bg/50">
          <span className="font-sans">Ctrl</span> K
        </span>
      </button>

      {aberto && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-[9999] flex items-start justify-center pt-[10vh] px-4 bg-black/60 backdrop-blur-sm" onClick={() => setAberto(false)}>
          <div 
            className="w-full max-w-2xl bg-surface border border-border rounded-2xl shadow-2xl overflow-hidden flex flex-col"
            onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
          >
            <div className="flex items-center px-4 border-b border-border">
              <Search className="text-muted" size={20} />
              <input
                ref={inputRef}
                type="text"
                value={query}
                onChange={(e) => setQuery(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="Buscar por animes (Lançamentos) ou usuários..."
                className="flex-1 bg-transparent text-text text-lg px-4 py-4 focus:outline-none placeholder:text-muted"
              />
              {buscando && <Loader2 className="animate-spin text-accent" size={20} />}
              <button onClick={() => setAberto(false)} className="text-muted hover:text-red-500 ml-2">
                <X size={20} />
              </button>
            </div>

            <div className="max-h-[60vh] overflow-y-auto p-2">
              
              {query.length < 2 ? (
                <div className="text-center py-10 text-muted text-sm flex flex-col items-center gap-2">
                  <PlayCircle size={32} className="opacity-50" />
                  Digite pelo menos 2 letras para buscar.
                </div>
              ) : (animes.length === 0 && usuarios.length === 0 && !buscando) ? (
                <div className="text-center py-10 text-muted text-sm">
                  Nenhum resultado encontrado nos lançamentos ou usuários para "{query}".
                </div>
              ) : (
                <div className="space-y-4">
                  {usuarios.length > 0 && (
                    <div>
                      <h3 className="px-3 text-xs font-bold text-muted uppercase tracking-wider mb-2">Usuários</h3>
                      {usuarios.map((user) => (
                        <Link 
                          key={user.id} 
                          href={`/perfil/${user.username}`}
                          onClick={() => setAberto(false)}
                          className="flex items-center gap-3 p-3 rounded-xl hover:bg-bg transition-colors"
                        >
                          <div className="relative w-8 h-8 rounded-full overflow-hidden bg-border shrink-0">
                            {user.avatar_url ? (
                              <Image src={user.avatar_url} alt="Avatar" fill sizes="32px" className="object-cover" />
                            ) : (
                              <div className="w-full h-full flex items-center justify-center text-xs font-bold">{user.username?.charAt(0).toUpperCase()}</div>
                            )}
                          </div>
                          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 flex-1">
                            <span className="font-bold text-text">{user.username}</span>
                            {user.is_admin && <SeloAdmin />}
                          </div>
                          <span className="text-xs text-muted">Ver Perfil</span>
                        </Link>
                      ))}
                    </div>
                  )}

                  {animes.length > 0 && (
                    <div>
                      <h3 className="px-3 text-xs font-bold text-muted uppercase tracking-wider mb-2">Lançamentos</h3>
                      {animes.map((anime) => (
                        <Link 
                          key={anime.id} 
                          href={`/anime/${anime.id}`}
                          onClick={() => setAberto(false)}
                          className="flex items-center gap-3 p-3 rounded-xl hover:bg-bg transition-colors"
                        >
                          <div className="relative w-8 h-12 rounded-md overflow-hidden bg-border shrink-0">
                            <Image src={anime.coverUrl} alt={anime.title} fill sizes="32px" className="object-cover" />
                          </div>
                          <div className="flex-1">
                            <span className="font-bold text-text line-clamp-1">{anime.title}</span>
                            <span className="text-xs text-muted block">Nota: {anime.score || 'N/A'}</span>
                          </div>
                          <span className="text-xs text-muted">Ir para o Anime</span>
                        </Link>
                      ))}
                    </div>
                  )}
                </div>
              )}
            </div>

            <div className="p-3 bg-bg/50 border-t border-border flex justify-between items-center text-xs text-muted">
              <span>Use <kbd className="font-sans bg-surface px-1 py-0.5 rounded border border-border">Esc</kbd> para fechar</span>
              <span>Buscando apenas nos lançamentos</span>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </>
  );
}
