===== C:\anime-site\components\layout\ModalFeedback.tsx =====
'use client';

import { useState } from 'react';
import { createClient } from '@/lib/supabase/client';
import { X, Send, Loader2, MessageSquareWarning } from 'lucide-react';

export function ModalFeedback({ onClose }: { onClose: () => void }) {
  const [tipo, setTipo] = useState('sugestao');
  const [mensagem, setMensagem] = useState('');
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [sucesso, setSucesso] = useState(false);
  const [erro, setErro] = useState('');
  
  const supabase = createClient();

  const handleEnviar = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!mensagem.trim()) return;

    setLoading(true);
    setErro('');

    try {
      const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
      if (!user) throw new Error('Você precisa estar logado.');

      const { error } = await supabase.from('feedbacks').insert({
        user_id: user.id,
        type: tipo,
        message: mensagem
      });

      if (error) throw new Error(error.message);

      setSucesso(true);
      setTimeout(onClose, 2000); // Fecha após 2 segundos
    } catch (error: any) {
      setErro(error.message || 'Erro ao enviar.');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-[200] bg-black/80 flex items-center justify-center p-4 backdrop-blur-sm" onClick={onClose}>
      <div className="w-full max-w-md bg-surface rounded-2xl overflow-hidden shadow-2xl" onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}>
        
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between p-4 border-b border-border">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-text font-bold">
            <MessageSquareWarning size={20} className="text-accent" />
            Enviar Feedback
          </div>
          <button onClick={onClose} className="text-muted hover:text-red-500"><X size={24} /></button>
        </div>

        {sucesso ? (
          <div className="p-8 text-center text-green-500 flex flex-col items-center gap-2">
            <div className="bg-green-500/10 p-4 rounded-full mb-2">✓</div>
            <h3 className="text-xl font-bold">Mensagem Enviada!</h3>
            <p className="text-muted text-sm">Obrigado por ajudar a melhorar o AniVerse.</p>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <form onSubmit={handleEnviar} className="p-4 space-y-4">
            {erro && <div className="bg-red-500/10 text-red-500 p-2 rounded text-sm">{erro}</div>}

            <div>
              <label className="text-sm font-medium text-text mb-1 block">O que você quer enviar?</label>
              <select 
                value={tipo} 
                onChange={(e) => setTipo(e.target.value)}
                className="w-full bg-bg border border-border text-text rounded-xl p-3 focus:outline-none focus:border-accent"
              >
                <option value="sugestao">Sugestão de melhoria</option>
                <option value="bug">Relatar um erro (Bug)</option>
                <option value="denuncia">Denúncia de conteúdo</option>
                <option value="outro">Outro assunto</option>
              </select>
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="text-sm font-medium text-text mb-1 block">Sua mensagem</label>
              <textarea
                value={mensagem}
                onChange={(e) => setMensagem(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="Descreva o que você está pensando..."
                rows={4}
                required
                className="w-full bg-bg border border-border text-text rounded-xl p-3 focus:outline-none focus:border-accent resize-none"
              />
            </div>

            <button
              type="submit"
              disabled={loading || !mensagem.trim()}
              className="w-full bg-accent text-white font-bold py-3 rounded-xl hover:bg-accent/90 transition-colors disabled:opacity-50 flex items-center justify-center gap-2"
            >
              {loading ? <Loader2 className="animate-spin" size={20} /> : <Send size={20} />}
              {loading ? 'Enviando...' : 'Enviar Mensagem'}
            </button>
          </form>
        )}

      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

===== C:\anime-site\components\layout\Navbar.tsx =====
'use client';

import { useEffect, useState, useMemo } from 'react';
import Link from 'next/link';
import Image from 'next/image';
import { usePathname, useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { createPortal } from 'react-dom';
import { CalendarDays, Trophy, User, LogOut, Compass, MessageCircle, LayoutGrid, Menu, X, ShieldAlert, MessageSquareWarning } from 'lucide-react';
import { createClient } from '@/lib/supabase/client';
import { User as SupabaseUser } from '@supabase/supabase-js';
import { ThemeToggle } from './ThemeToggle';
import { Notificacoes } from './Notificacoes';
import { BuscaGlobal } from './BuscaGlobal';
import { ModalFeedback } from './ModalFeedback';

const navItems = [
  { name: 'Lançamentos', href: '/', icon: CalendarDays },
  { name: 'Mensagens', href: '/mensagens', icon: MessageCircle },
  { name: 'Feed', href: '/feed', icon: LayoutGrid },
  { name: 'Meu Top 10', href: '/top10', icon: Trophy },
];

export function Navbar() {
  const pathname = usePathname();
  const router = useRouter();
  
  const supabase = useMemo(() => createClient(), []);
  
  const [user, setUser] = useState<SupabaseUser | null>(null);
  const [username, setUsername] = useState<string>('');
  const [avatarUrl, setAvatarUrl] = useState<string>('');
  const [isAdmin, setIsAdmin] = useState(false);
  const [isMenuOpen, setIsMenuOpen] = useState(false);
  const [isMobileMenuOpen, setIsMobileMenuOpen] = useState(false);
  const [feedbackAberto, setFeedbackAberto] = useState(false);
  const [mounted, setMounted] = useState(false);
  const [unreadCount, setUnreadCount] = useState(0);

  useEffect(() => {
    setMounted(true);
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    let channel: any;

    const getUser = async () => {
      const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
      setUser(prev => (prev?.id === user?.id ? prev : user));
      
      if (user) {
        const { data } = await supabase.from('profiles').select('username, avatar_url, is_admin').eq('id', user.id).single();
        if (data) { 
          setUsername(data.username || 'Perfil'); 
          setAvatarUrl(data.avatar_url || ''); 
          setIsAdmin(data.is_admin || false);
        }

        const fetchUnread = async () => {
          const { count } = await supabase
            .from('direct_messages')
            .select('*', { count: 'exact', head: true })
            .eq('receiver_id', user.id)
            .eq('is_read', false);
          setUnreadCount(count || 0);
        };

        fetchUnread();

        channel = supabase
          .channel('navbar-mensagens')
          .on(
            'postgres_changes',
            { event: '*', schema: 'public', table: 'direct_messages' },
            () => { fetchUnread(); }
          )
          .subscribe();
      }
    };
    
    getUser();
    
    const { data: authListener } = supabase.auth.onAuthStateChange((event, session) => {
      setUser(prev => (prev?.id === session?.user?.id ? prev : session?.user ?? null));
      if (!session?.user) { setUsername(''); setAvatarUrl(''); setUnreadCount(0); setIsAdmin(false); }
    });
    
    return () => {
      authListener.subscription.unsubscribe();
      if (channel) supabase.removeChannel(channel);
    };
  }, [supabase]);

  const handleLogout = async () => {
    await supabase.auth.signOut();
    setIsMenuOpen(false);
    setIsMobileMenuOpen(false);
    router.push('/');
    router.refresh();
  };

  return (
    <>
      {/* DESKTOP */}
      <header className="hidden md:flex sticky top-0 z-50 w-full h-16 bg-surface/80 backdrop-blur-md border-b border-border items-center justify-between px-6 transition-colors duration-300">
        <Link href="/" className="flex items-center group cursor-pointer">
          {/* LOGO (que contém o "A") */}
          <div className="relative w-9 h-9 group-hover:scale-105 transition-transform flex-shrink-0">
            <Image 
              src="/icon.svg" 
              alt="Logo AniVerse" 
              fill 
              className="object-contain" 
            />
          </div>
          
          {/* TEXTO (NIVERSE) - O "A" já está na logo */}
          <span className="text-xl font-bold tracking-wider text-text ml-0">
            <span className="text-accent">NI</span><span>VERSE</span>
          </span>
        </Link>

        <nav className="flex items-center gap-1 lg:gap-2">
          {navItems.map((item) => {
            const isActive = pathname === item.href;
            const Icon = item.icon;
            return (
              <Link
                key={item.name}
                href={item.href}
                className={`relative flex items-center gap-2 px-3 lg:px-4 py-2 rounded-lg transition-colors font-medium text-sm cursor-pointer ${
                  isActive ? 'bg-accent/10 text-accent' : 'text-muted hover:text-text hover:bg-bg'
                }`}
              >
                <Icon size={18} />
                <span className="hidden lg:block">{item.name}</span>
                
                {item.name === 'Mensagens' && unreadCount > 0 && (
                  <span className="absolute -top-1 -right-1 bg-red-500 text-white text-[10px] font-bold px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded-full min-w-[18px] text-center">
                    {unreadCount}
                  </span>
                )}
              </Link>
            );
          })}

          <Link href="/destaques" className={`flex items-center gap-2 px-3 lg:px-4 py-2 rounded-lg transition-colors font-medium text-sm cursor-pointer ${pathname === '/destaques' ? 'bg-accent/10 text-accent' : 'text-muted hover:text-text hover:bg-bg'}`}>
            <Compass size={18} />
            <span className="hidden lg:block">Destaques</span>
          </Link>

          {isAdmin && (
            <Link 
              href="/admin" 
              className={`flex items-center gap-2 px-3 lg:px-4 py-2 rounded-lg transition-colors font-medium text-sm cursor-pointer ${
                pathname === '/admin' ? 'bg-red-500/10 text-red-500' : 'text-muted hover:text-red-500 hover:bg-red-500/10'
              }`}
            >
              <ShieldAlert size={18} />
              <span className="hidden lg:block">Admin</span>
            </Link>
          )}
          
          <div className="mx-2"><BuscaGlobal /></div>
          <div className="mx-2"><ThemeToggle /></div>
          <Notificacoes />

          <div className="relative">
            <button
              onClick={() => {
                if (!user) {
                  setIsMenuOpen(false);
                  router.push('/login');
                } else {
                  setIsMenuOpen(!isMenuOpen);
                }
              }}
              className={`flex items-center gap-2 px-2 py-1.5 rounded-full border transition-colors cursor-pointer ${
                pathname === '/perfil' || isMenuOpen 
                  ? 'bg-accent/10 border-accent text-accent' 
                  : 'bg-surface border-border text-muted hover:border-accent hover:text-text'
              }`}
            >
              {user ? (
                <>
                  {avatarUrl ? (
                    <div className="relative w-7 h-7 rounded-full overflow-hidden">
                      <Image src={avatarUrl} alt="Avatar" fill className="object-cover" />
                    </div>
                  ) : (
                    <div className="w-7 h-7 rounded-full bg-border flex items-center justify-center">
                      <User size={14} />
                    </div>
                  )}
                  <span className="text-sm font-medium pr-2 max-w-[100px] truncate">{username || 'Meu Perfil'}</span>
                </>
              ) : (
                <>
                  <User size={18} className="ml-2" />
                  <span className="text-sm font-medium pr-2">Entrar</span>
                </>
              )}
            </button>

            {/* MENU DESKTOP */}
            {isMenuOpen && user && (
              <div className="absolute right-0 top-full mt-2 w-48 bg-surface border border-border rounded-xl shadow-2xl py-2 flex flex-col z-50">
                <Link href="/perfil" onClick={() => setIsMenuOpen(false)} className="px-4 py-3 text-sm text-text hover:bg-bg transition-colors block w-full text-left">
                  Ver meu perfil
                </Link>
                <button 
                  onClick={() => { setIsMenuOpen(false); setFeedbackAberto(true); }} 
                  className="px-4 py-3 text-sm text-text hover:bg-bg transition-colors flex items-center gap-2 text-left w-full cursor-pointer"
                >
                  <MessageSquareWarning size={16} className="text-accent" /> Enviar Feedback
                </button>
                <div className="h-[1px] bg-border my-1" />
                <button onClick={handleLogout} className="px-4 py-3 text-sm text-red-500 hover:bg-red-500/10 transition-colors flex items-center gap-2 text-left w-full cursor-pointer">
                  <LogOut size={16} /> Sair da conta
                </button>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        </nav>
      </header>

      {/* MOBILE */}
      <nav className="md:hidden fixed bottom-0 left-0 right-0 z-[900] h-16 bg-surface/90 backdrop-blur-lg border-t border-border flex items-center justify-around pb-safe transition-colors duration-300">
        {navItems.map((item) => {
          const isActive = pathname === item.href;
          const Icon = item.icon;
          if (item.name === 'Meu Top 10') return null; 
          
          return (
            <Link
              key={item.name}
              href={item.href}
              className={`relative flex flex-col items-center justify-center w-full h-full gap-1 transition-colors cursor-pointer ${
                isActive ? 'text-accent' : 'text-muted hover:text-text'
              }`}
            >
              <Icon size={20} className={isActive ? 'stroke-[2.5px]' : 'stroke-2'} />
              <span className="text-[10px] font-medium">{item.name}</span>
              {item.name === 'Mensagens' && unreadCount > 0 && (
                <span className="absolute top-2 right-4 bg-red-500 text-white text-[9px] font-bold px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded-full">
                  {unreadCount}
                </span>
              )}
            </Link>
          );
        })}
        
        <button
          onClick={() => setIsMobileMenuOpen(true)}
          className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center w-full h-full gap-1 transition-colors text-muted hover:text-text cursor-pointer"
        >
          <Menu size={20} />
          <span className="text-[10px] font-medium">Mais</span>
        </button>
      </nav>

      {/* MENU MOBILE "MAIS" */}
      {isMobileMenuOpen && mounted && createPortal(
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-[9999] bg-black/60 backdrop-blur-sm" onClick={() => setIsMobileMenuOpen(false)}>
          <div 
            className="absolute bottom-0 left-0 right-0 bg-surface rounded-t-3xl p-6 pb-24 flex flex-col gap-4 shadow-2xl animate-slide-up" 
            onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
          >
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-4">
              <h3 className="text-xl font-bold text-text">Menu</h3>
              <button onClick={() => setIsMobileMenuOpen(false)} className="text-muted hover:text-text"><X size={24} /></button>
            </div>

            <Link href="/destaques" onClick={() => setIsMobileMenuOpen(false)} className="flex items-center gap-3 p-4 bg-bg rounded-xl text-text font-medium hover:bg-border transition-colors">
              <Compass size={20} className="text-accent" /> Destaques
            </Link>
            <Link href="/top10" onClick={() => setIsMobileMenuOpen(false)} className="flex items-center gap-3 p-4 bg-bg rounded-xl text-text font-medium hover:bg-border transition-colors">
              <Trophy size={20} className="text-accent" /> Meu Top 10
            </Link>

            {isAdmin && (
              <Link href="/admin" onClick={() => setIsMobileMenuOpen(false)} className="flex items-center gap-3 p-4 bg-red-500/10 text-red-500 rounded-xl font-medium hover:bg-red-500/20 transition-colors">
                <ShieldAlert size={20} /> Painel Admin
              </Link>
            )}

            <button 
              onClick={() => { setIsMobileMenuOpen(false); setFeedbackAberto(true); }} 
              className="flex items-center gap-3 p-4 bg-bg rounded-xl text-text font-medium hover:bg-border transition-colors w-full text-left"
            >
              <MessageSquareWarning size={20} className="text-accent" /> Enviar Feedback / Suporte
            </button>

            <div className="flex items-center justify-between p-4 bg-bg rounded-xl">
              <span className="text-text font-medium">Tema</span>
              <ThemeToggle />
            </div>

            <div className="flex items-center justify-between p-4 bg-bg rounded-xl">
              <span className="text-text font-medium">Buscar</span>
              <BuscaGlobal />
            </div>

            <div className="h-[1px] bg-border my-2" />

            {user ? (
              <>
                <Link href="/perfil" onClick={() => setIsMobileMenuOpen(false)} className="flex items-center gap-3 p-4 bg-bg rounded-xl text-text font-medium hover:bg-border transition-colors">
                  <User size={20} className="text-accent" /> Meu Perfil ({username})
                </Link>
                <button onClick={handleLogout} className="flex items-center gap-3 p-4 bg-red-500/10 text-red-500 rounded-xl font-medium hover:bg-red-500/20 transition-colors">
                  <LogOut size={20} /> Sair da conta
                </button>
              </>
            ) : (
              <Link href="/login" onClick={() => setIsMobileMenuOpen(false)} className="flex items-center justify-center gap-3 p-4 bg-accent text-white rounded-xl font-bold hover:bg-accent/90 transition-colors">
                <User size={20} /> Entrar
              </Link>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>,
        document.body
      )}

      {/* MODAL DE FEEDBACK */}
      {feedbackAberto && <ModalFeedback onClose={() => setFeedbackAberto(false)} />}
    </>
  );
}

===== C:\anime-site\components\layout\Notificacoes.tsx =====
'use client';

import { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { Bell, Check } from 'lucide-react';
import { createClient } from '@/lib/supabase/client';
import { formatDistanceToNow } from 'date-fns';
import { ptBR } from 'date-fns/locale';

interface Notificacao {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  message: string;
  is_read: boolean;
  created_at: string;
}

export function Notificacoes() {
  const [notificacoes, setNotificacoes] = useState<Notificacao[]>([]);
  const [isOpen, setIsOpen] = useState(false);
  const [userId, setUserId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const menuRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const supabase = createClient();

  useEffect(() => {
    let channel: any;

    const init = async () => {
      const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
      if (!user) return;
      setUserId(user.id);

      // 1. Busca inicial das notificações
      const { data } = await supabase
        .from('notifications')
        .select('*')
        .eq('user_id', user.id)
        .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
        .limit(10);

      if (data) setNotificacoes(data);

      // 2. Configura o Realtime para escutar novas notificações
      channel = supabase
        .channel('notificacoes-realtime')
        .on(
          'postgres_changes',
          {
            event: 'INSERT', // Escuta apenas quando uma nova notificação é inserida
            schema: 'public',
            table: 'notifications',
            filter: `user_id=eq.${user.id}`, // Só escuta as notificações deste usuário
          },
          (payload) => {
            // Quando uma nova notificação chega, adiciona no topo da lista
            const novaNotificacao = payload.new as Notificacao;
            setNotificacoes((prev) => [novaNotificacao, ...prev].slice(0, 10));
          }
        )
        .subscribe();
    };

    init();

    // Fecha o menu se clicar fora
    const handleClickOutside = (event: MouseEvent) => {
      if (menuRef.current && !menuRef.current.contains(event.target as Node)) {
        setIsOpen(false);
      }
    };
    document.addEventListener('mousedown', handleClickOutside);

    return () => {
      document.removeEventListener('mousedown', handleClickOutside);
      if (channel) supabase.removeChannel(channel); // Limpa o canal ao desmontar
    };
  }, [supabase]);

  // Marcar como lida
  const marcarComoLida = async (id: string) => {
    if (!userId) return;
    
    await supabase
      .from('notifications')
      .update({ is_read: true })
      .eq('id', id)
      .eq('user_id', userId);

    setNotificacoes((prev) =>
      prev.map((n) => (n.id === id ? { ...n, is_read: true } : n))
    );
  };

  // Marcar todas como lidas
  const marcarTodasComoLidas = async () => {
    if (!userId) return;
    
    await supabase
      .from('notifications')
      .update({ is_read: true })
      .eq('user_id', userId)
      .eq('is_read', false);

    setNotificacoes((prev) => prev.map((n) => ({ ...n, is_read: true })));
  };

  const naoLidas = notificacoes.filter((n) => !n.is_read).length;

  if (!userId) return null;

  return (
    <div className="relative" ref={menuRef}>
      <button
        onClick={() => setIsOpen(!isOpen)}
        className="relative p-2 rounded-full hover:bg-bg transition-colors cursor-pointer text-muted hover:text-accent"
      >
        <Bell size={20} />
        {naoLidas > 0 && (
          <span className="absolute top-1.5 right-1.5 w-2.5 h-2.5 bg-red-500 rounded-full border-2 border-surface animate-pulse" />
        )}
      </button>

      {isOpen && (
        <div className="absolute right-0 top-full mt-2 w-80 bg-surface border border-border rounded-xl shadow-2xl z-50 overflow-hidden">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between p-4 border-b border-border bg-bg/50">
            <h3 className="font-bold text-text">Notificações</h3>
            {naoLidas > 0 && (
              <button
                onClick={marcarTodasComoLidas}
                className="text-xs text-accent hover:underline flex items-center gap-1 cursor-pointer"
              >
                <Check size={12} /> Marcar todas como lidas
              </button>
            )}
          </div>

          <div className="max-h-[300px] overflow-y-auto">
            {notificacoes.length === 0 ? (
              <div className="p-6 text-center text-muted text-sm">
                Nenhuma notificação por aqui.
              </div>
            ) : (
              notificacoes.map((notif) => (
                <div
                  key={notif.id}
                  onClick={() => marcarComoLida(notif.id)}
                  className={`p-4 border-b border-border last:border-0 cursor-pointer transition-colors hover:bg-bg/50 ${
                    !notif.is_read ? 'bg-accent/5' : ''
                  }`}
                >
                  <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
                    {!notif.is_read && (
                      <div className="w-2 h-2 mt-1.5 rounded-full bg-accent flex-shrink-0" />
                    )}
                    <div className="flex-1">
                      <h4 className="text-sm font-bold text-text mb-1">{notif.title}</h4>
                      <p className="text-xs text-muted leading-relaxed">{notif.message}</p>
                      <span className="text-[10px] text-muted mt-2 block">
                        {formatDistanceToNow(new Date(notif.created_at), { addSuffix: true, locale: ptBR })}
                      </span>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              ))
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

'use client';

import { Lock, ArrowLeft, Sparkles } from 'lucide-react';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { ReactNode } from 'react';

interface TelaBloqueioProps {
  titulo: string;
  descricao: string;
  spoiler?: ReactNode; // Onde você vai colocar a prévia
  linkVoltar?: string;
  textoVoltar?: string;
}

export function TelaBloqueio({ 
  titulo, 
  descricao, 
  spoiler, 
  linkVoltar = '/', 
  textoVoltar = 'Voltar para os Lançamentos' 
}: TelaBloqueioProps) {
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center min-h-[70vh] text-center px-4 py-12">
      <div className="bg-surface border border-border p-6 rounded-full mb-6 animate-pulse">
        <Lock size={48} className="text-muted" />
      </div>

      </Link>
    </div>

===== C:\anime-site\components\layout\ThemeToggle.tsx =====
'use client';

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { Moon, Sun } from 'lucide-react';

export function ThemeToggle() {
  const [isDark, setIsDark] = useState(true);

  useEffect(() => {
    // Verifica se o usuário já escolheu um tema antes
    const savedTheme = localStorage.getItem('theme');
    const prefersDark = window.matchMedia('(prefers-color-scheme: dark)').matches;
    
    if (savedTheme === 'light' || (!savedTheme && !prefersDark)) {
      setIsDark(false);
      document.documentElement.classList.remove('dark');
    } else {
      setIsDark(true);
      document.documentElement.classList.add('dark');
    }
  }, []);

  const toggleTheme = () => {
    if (isDark) {
      document.documentElement.classList.remove('dark');
      localStorage.setItem('theme', 'light');
      setIsDark(false);
    } else {
      document.documentElement.classList.add('dark');
      localStorage.setItem('theme', 'dark');
      setIsDark(true);
    }
  };

  return (
    <button
      onClick={toggleTheme}
      className="p-2 rounded-lg bg-surface border border-border text-muted hover:text-accent hover:border-accent transition-all cursor-pointer"
      title={isDark ? "Acender a luz" : "Apagar a luz"}
    >
      {isDark ? <Sun size={18} /> : <Moon size={18} />}
    </button>
  );
}

import Image from 'next/image';
import Link from 'next/link';

export interface AnimeData {
  id: number;

  formattedTime: string;
  episode: number;
}

export function CardAnime({ anime }: { anime: AnimeData }) {
  return (
    <Link 
      href={`/anime/${anime.id}`} 
      className="group flex flex-col bg-surface rounded-xl overflow-hidden border border-border hover:border-accent transition-all duration-300 hover:-translate-y-1 hover:shadow-lg hover:shadow-accent/10"
    >
      {/* Capa */}
      <div className="relative aspect-[2/3] w-full overflow-hidden">
        <Image
          src={anime.coverUrl}
          alt={anime.title}
          fill
          sizes="(max-width: 768px) 50vw, (max-width: 1200px) 33vw, 20vw"
          className="object-cover transition-transform duration-500 group-hover:scale-110"
        />
        
        {/* Badge de Nota */}
        <div className="absolute top-2 right-2 bg-black/70 backdrop-blur-sm text-yellow-400 text-xs font-bold px-2 py-1 rounded-md flex items-center gap-1">
          ⭐ {anime.score > 0 ? (anime.score / 10).toFixed(1) : 'N/A'}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Informações */}
      <div className="p-3 flex flex-col flex-1">
        <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-text line-clamp-2 leading-tight mb-2 group-hover:text-accent transition-colors">
          {anime.title}
        </h3>
        
        <div className="mt-auto flex items-center justify-between text-xs text-muted">
          <span className="bg-border/50 px-2 py-1 rounded text-text font-medium">
            EP {anime.episode}
          </span>
          <span className="flex items-center gap-1">
            🕒 {anime.formattedTime}
          </span>
        </div>
      </div>
    </Link>
  );
}

===== C:\anime-site\components\layout\lancamentos\GridAnimes.tsx =====
import { CardAnime, AnimeData } from './CardAnime';

interface GridAnimesProps {
  animes: AnimeData[];
  isLoading: boolean;
}

export function GridAnimes({ animes, isLoading }: GridAnimesProps) {
  // 1. Tela de Carregamento (Skeletons)
  if (isLoading) {
    return (
      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-3 md:grid-cols-4 lg:grid-cols-5 gap-4">
        {Array.from({ length: 10 }).map((_, i) => (
          <div key={i} className="flex flex-col bg-surface rounded-xl overflow-hidden border border-border animate-pulse">
            <div className="aspect-[2/3] w-full bg-border/30" />
            <div className="p-3">
              <div className="h-4 bg-border/30 rounded w-full mb-2" />
              <div className="h-4 bg-border/30 rounded w-2/3" />
            </div>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
    );
  }

  // 2. Tela Vazia
  if (animes.length === 0) {
    return (
      <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center py-20 text-center">
        <span className="text-4xl mb-4">🍃</span>
        <h3 className="text-lg font-bold text-text">Nenhum lançamento</h3>
        <p className="text-muted text-sm mt-1">Não encontramos animes para este dia.</p>
      </div>
    );
  }

  // 3. Grid de Animes
  return (
    <div className="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-3 md:grid-cols-4 lg:grid-cols-5 gap-4">
      {animes.map((anime) => (
        <CardAnime key={anime.id} anime={anime} />
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}

===== C:\anime-site\components\layout\lancamentos\SeletorDias.tsx =====
'use client';

import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';

// Lista fixa na ordem que você pediu
const diasDaSemana = [
  { id: 'seg', label: 'Seg', diaSemanaJs: 1 },
  { id: 'ter', label: 'Ter', diaSemanaJs: 2 },
  { id: 'qua', label: 'Qua', diaSemanaJs: 3 },
  { id: 'qui', label: 'Qui', diaSemanaJs: 4 },
  { id: 'sex', label: 'Sex', diaSemanaJs: 5 },
  { id: 'sab', label: 'Sáb', diaSemanaJs: 6 },
  { id: 'dom', label: 'Dom', diaSemanaJs: 0 },
];

interface SeletorDiasProps {
  diaSelecionado: string;
  onChangeDia: (dia: string) => void;
}

export function SeletorDias({ diaSelecionado, onChangeDia }: SeletorDiasProps) {
  const [hojeId, setHojeId] = useState<string>('');

  // Descobre qual é o dia de hoje no fuso do Brasil assim que o componente monta
  useEffect(() => {
    const dataHoje = new Date();
    // Formata a data no fuso de São Paulo para pegar o dia da semana correto
    const diaSemanaString = dataHoje.toLocaleDateString('pt-BR', { 
      timeZone: 'America/Sao_Paulo', 
      weekday: 'short' 
    });
    
    // O toLocaleDateString retorna "seg.", "ter.", etc. Vamos mapear para o nosso ID
    const mapaDias: Record<string, string> = {
      'dom.': 'dom', 'seg.': 'seg', 'ter.': 'ter', 
      'qua.': 'qua', 'qui.': 'qui', 'sex.': 'sex', 'sáb.': 'sab'
    };
    
    const hojeCalculado = mapaDias[diaSemanaString];
    setHojeId(hojeCalculado);
    
    // Se o usuário acabou de entrar no site, seleciona o dia de hoje automaticamente
    if (!diaSelecionado && hojeCalculado) {
      onChangeDia(hojeCalculado);
    }
  }, [diaSelecionado, onChangeDia]);

  return (
    <div className="w-full mb-8">
      {/* 
        scroll horizontal no mobile (overflow-x-auto) 
        e esconde a barra de rolagem (scrollbar-hide) 
      */}
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 overflow-x-auto pb-2 scrollbar-hide">
        {diasDaSemana.map((dia) => {
          const isAtivo = diaSelecionado === dia.id;
          const isHoje = hojeId === dia.id;

          return (
            <button
              key={dia.id}
              onClick={() => onChangeDia(dia.id)}
              className={`
                relative flex-shrink-0 flex flex-col items-center justify-center 
                w-16 h-16 rounded-2xl transition-all duration-200 border
                ${isAtivo 
                  ? 'bg-accent text-white border-accent shadow-lg shadow-accent/20' 
                  : 'bg-surface text-muted border-border hover:border-accent/50 hover:text-text'
                }
              `}
            >
              {/* O texto do dia */}
              <span className={`text-sm font-semibold ${isAtivo ? 'text-white' : ''}`}>
                {dia.label}
              </span>
              
              {/* Badge de HOJE */}
              {isHoje && (
                <span className={`text-[9px] font-bold mt-1 uppercase tracking-wider ${isAtivo ? 'text-white/80' : 'text-accent'}`}>
                  HOJE
                </span>
              )}
              
              {/* Bolinha indicadora de HOJE (se não estiver ativo) */}
              {isHoje && !isAtivo && (
                <span className="absolute bottom-2 w-1 h-1 bg-accent rounded-full" />
              )}
            </button>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

===== C:\anime-site\components\perfil\BotaoSeguir.tsx =====
'use client';

import { useState, useOptimistic, startTransition } from 'react';
import { createClient } from '@/lib/supabase/client';
import { UserPlus, UserCheck, Loader2 } from 'lucide-react';

export function BotaoSeguir({ targetUserId, initialIsFollowing }: { targetUserId: string, initialIsFollowing: boolean }) {
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const supabase = createClient();

  const [isFollowing, toggleOptimistic] = useOptimistic(
    initialIsFollowing,
    (state) => !state
  );

  const handleToggleFollow = () => {
    startTransition(async () => {
      toggleOptimistic(null); // Muda visualmente na hora
      setLoading(true);

      const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
      if (!user) return;

      if (initialIsFollowing) {
        // Deixar de seguir
        await supabase.from('follows').delete().match({ follower_id: user.id, following_id: targetUserId });
      } else {
        // Seguir
        await supabase.from('follows').insert({ follower_id: user.id, following_id: targetUserId });
      }
      
      setLoading(false);
    });
  };

  return (
    <button
      onClick={handleToggleFollow}
      disabled={loading}
      className={`flex items-center justify-center gap-2 px-4 py-2.5 rounded-lg transition-colors font-medium text-sm cursor-pointer disabled:opacity-50 ${
        isFollowing 
          ? 'bg-surface border border-border text-text hover:border-red-500 hover:text-red-500' 
          : 'bg-accent/10 text-accent hover:bg-accent hover:text-white'
      }`}
    >
      {loading ? (
        <Loader2 className="animate-spin" size={18} />
      ) : isFollowing ? (
        <UserCheck size={18} />
      ) : (
        <UserPlus size={18} />
      )}
      {isFollowing ? 'Seguindo' : 'Seguir'}
    </button>
  );
}

'use client';

import { useState } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { Trash2, AlertTriangle, Loader2 } from 'lucide-react';

export function DeletarConta() {
  const [isOpen, setIsOpen] = useState(false);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const router = useRouter();

  const handleDeletar = async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/delete-account', { method: 'DELETE' });
      
      if (!res.ok) throw new Error('Falha ao deletar conta');
      
      alert('Sua conta foi deletada com sucesso. Até a próxima!');
      router.push('/');
      router.refresh();
      
    } catch (error) {
      console.error(error);
      alert('Ocorreu um erro ao deletar sua conta. Tente novamente.');
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <>
      <button
        onClick={() => setIsOpen(true)}
        className="flex items-center gap-2 px-6 py-3 bg-red-500/10 text-red-500 border border-red-500/20 rounded-lg font-bold hover:bg-red-500 hover:text-white transition-all cursor-pointer"
      >
        <Trash2 size={20} />
        Deletar Conta
      </button>

      {isOpen && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-[100] flex items-center justify-center bg-black/60 backdrop-blur-sm p-4">
          <div className="w-full max-w-md bg-surface border border-border rounded-2xl p-8 shadow-2xl relative">
            
            <div className="w-16 h-16 rounded-full bg-red-500/20 flex items-center justify-center mx-auto mb-6">
              <AlertTriangle size={32} className="text-red-500" />
            </div>

            <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold text-center text-text mb-2">Tem certeza?</h2>
            <p className="text-center text-muted text-sm mb-8">
              Essa ação não pode ser desfeita. Todas as suas listas, notas e perfil serão apagados permanentemente do AniVerse.
            </p>

            <div className="flex gap-3">
              <button
                onClick={() => setIsOpen(false)}
                className="flex-1 py-3 border border-border text-text rounded-lg font-bold hover:bg-bg transition-colors cursor-pointer"
              >
                Cancelar
              </button>
              <button
                onClick={handleDeletar}
                disabled={loading}
                className="flex-1 py-3 bg-red-500 text-white rounded-lg font-bold hover:bg-red-600 disabled:opacity-50 transition-colors flex justify-center items-center gap-2 cursor-pointer"
              >
                {loading ? <Loader2 className="animate-spin" size={20} /> : 'Sim, deletar'}
              </button>
            </div>

          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </>
  );
}

===== C:\anime-site\components\top10\BotaoPostarFeed.tsx =====
'use client';

import { useState } from 'react';
import { createClient } from '@/lib/supabase/client';
import { Loader2, Send, Lock } from 'lucide-react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';

interface AnimeData {
  id: number;
  title: string;
  coverUrl: string;
}

export function BotaoPostarFeed({ animes, legenda, feedLiberado }: { animes: AnimeData[], legenda: string, feedLiberado: boolean }) {
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [erro, setErro] = useState('');
  const supabase = createClient();
  const router = useRouter();

  const handlePostar = async () => {
    if (!feedLiberado) { setErro('O Feed está em manutenção.'); return; }
    if (animes.length === 0) { setErro('Adicione pelo menos 1 anime.'); return; }

    setLoading(true);
    setErro('');

    try {
      const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
      if (!user) { setErro("Você precisa estar logado."); return; }

      const mediaUrls: string[] = [];

      // Faz o upload das capas uma por uma
      for (const anime of animes) {
        const highResUrl = anime.coverUrl.replace('/large/', '/extraLarge/'); 
        const res = await fetch(`/api/proxy-image?url=${encodeURIComponent(highResUrl)}`);
        const blob = await res.blob();
        const fileName = `capa-${anime.id}-${Date.now()}.png`;
        
        const { error: uploadError } = await supabase.storage.from('feed_media').upload(fileName, blob);
        if (uploadError) throw new Error(`Falha no upload: ${uploadError.message}`);

        const { data: { publicUrl } } = supabase.storage.from('feed_media').getPublicUrl(fileName);
        mediaUrls.push(publicUrl);
      }

      // Insere no banco
      const { error: postError } = await supabase.from('posts').insert({
        user_id: user.id,
        media_url: mediaUrls[0], 
        media_urls: mediaUrls,   
        media_type: 'image',
        caption: legenda || `Meu Top 10 de animes!`,
        is_ranking: true, // <-- Marca como ranking
      });

      if (postError) throw new Error(`Falha ao salvar: ${postError.message}`);

      router.push('/feed');
      
    } catch (error: any) {
      setErro(error.message || 'Tente novamente.');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="w-full mt-4">
      {erro && (
        <div className="bg-red-500/10 border border-red-500 text-red-500 p-3 rounded-lg text-sm mb-3 flex items-center gap-2">
          <Lock size={18} /> {erro}
        </div>
      )}
      
      <button
        onClick={handlePostar}
        disabled={loading}
        className={`flex items-center justify-center gap-2 px-8 py-4 rounded-xl font-bold transition-all shadow-lg w-full ${
          feedLiberado ? 'bg-accent text-white hover:bg-accent/90 shadow-accent/20 cursor-pointer' : 'bg-surface text-muted border border-border cursor-not-allowed'
        }`}
      >
        {loading ? <Loader2 className="animate-spin" size={20} /> : !feedLiberado ? <Lock size={20} /> : <Send size={20} />}
        {loading ? 'Postando...' : !feedLiberado ? 'Feed em Manutenção' : 'Postar no Feed'}
      </button>
    </div>
  );
}

===== C:\anime-site\components\top10\StoriesPlayer.tsx =====
'use client';

import { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import Image from 'next/image';
import { X, Play, Pause, Info, Share2 } from 'lucide-react'; // Adicionado Share2

interface AnimeData {
  id: number;
  title: string;
  coverUrl: string;
  score: string;
}

export function StoriesPlayer({ 
  animes, 
  username, 
  avatarUrl, 
  onClose 
}: { 
  animes: AnimeData[], 
  username: string, 
  avatarUrl: string,
  onClose: () => void 
}) {
  const [indexAtual, setIndexAtual] = useState(0);
  const [isPlaying, setIsPlaying] = useState(true);
  const [progresso, setProgresso] = useState(0);
  
  const whooshRef = useRef<HTMLAudioElement>(null);
  const musicRef = useRef<HTMLAudioElement>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isPlaying || animes.length === 0) return;

    const duracaoSlide = 4000; 
    const intervalo = 50; 
    const passo = (intervalo / duracaoSlide) * 100;

    const timer = setInterval(() => {
      setProgresso((prev) => {
        if (prev >= 100) {
          if (indexAtual < animes.length - 1) {
            setIndexAtual((idx) => idx + 1);
            return 0;
          } else {
            setIsPlaying(false);
            clearInterval(timer);
            return 100;
          }
        }
        return prev + passo;
      });
    }, intervalo);

    return () => clearInterval(timer);
  }, [isPlaying, indexAtual, animes.length]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (whooshRef.current) {
      whooshRef.current.currentTime = 0;
      whooshRef.current.play().catch(() => {});
    }
  }, [indexAtual]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (musicRef.current) {
      musicRef.current.volume = 0.3;
      musicRef.current.play().catch(() => {});
    }
    return () => {
      if (musicRef.current) musicRef.current.pause();
    };
  }, []);

  // Função para compartilhar o link do site
  const handleShare = async () => {
    const url = window.location.origin; // Pega a URL base do site
    const shareData = {
      title: 'AniVerse | Lançamentos e Top 5',
      text: `Olha o meu Top 5 de animes no AniVerse! @${username}`,
      url: url,
    };

    try {
      if (navigator.share) {
        await navigator.share(shareData);
      } else {
        // Fallback: copia para a área de transferência
        await navigator.clipboard.writeText(url);
        alert('Link do AniVerse copiado para a área de transferência!');
      }
    } catch (error) {
      console.log('Compartilhamento cancelado ou falhou', error);
    }
  };

  if (animes.length === 0) return null;

  const animeAtual = animes[indexAtual];

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-[200] bg-black flex items-center justify-center">
      
      <audio ref={whooshRef} src="/sounds/whoosh.mp3" preload="auto" />
      <audio ref={musicRef} src="/sounds/musica-fundo.mp3" loop preload="auto" />

      <div className="relative w-full h-full max-w-md aspect-[9/16] bg-[#0a0a0f] overflow-hidden shadow-2xl">
        
        <div className="absolute inset-0">
          <Image 
            src={animeAtual.coverUrl} 
            alt={animeAtual.title} 
            fill 
            className="object-cover opacity-30 blur-md"
            priority
          />
          <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-b from-black/60 via-black/40 to-black" />
        </div>

        <div className="absolute top-4 left-4 right-4 flex gap-1 z-50">
          {animes.map((_, idx) => (
            <div key={idx} className="h-1 flex-1 bg-white/30 rounded-full overflow-hidden">
              <div 
                className="h-full bg-white transition-all duration-75 ease-linear"
                style={{ width: idx < indexAtual ? '100%' : idx === indexAtual ? `${progresso}%` : '0%' }}
              />
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>

        <div className="absolute top-8 left-4 right-4 flex items-center justify-between z-50">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <div className="relative w-10 h-10 rounded-full border-2 border-accent overflow-hidden bg-surface">
              {avatarUrl ? (
                <Image src={avatarUrl} alt="Avatar" fill className="object-cover" />
              ) : (
                <div className="w-full h-full flex items-center justify-center text-accent font-bold">A</div>
              )}
            </div>
            <span className="text-white font-bold text-sm shadow-md">@{username}</span>
          </div>

          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <button onClick={() => setIsPlaying(!isPlaying)} className="text-white p-2 cursor-pointer bg-black/30 rounded-full backdrop-blur-sm">
              {isPlaying ? <Pause size={18} /> : <Play size={18} />}
            </button>
            <button onClick={onClose} className="text-white p-2 cursor-pointer bg-black/30 rounded-full backdrop-blur-sm">
              <X size={20} />
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="absolute inset-0 flex flex-col items-center justify-center p-6 z-10 text-center pt-20 pb-24">
          <div className="mb-4 bg-accent text-white font-black text-4xl w-16 h-16 flex items-center justify-center rounded-2xl shadow-xl shadow-accent/50">
            #{indexAtual + 1}
          </div>

          <div className="relative w-48 h-72 rounded-xl overflow-hidden shadow-2xl border-2 border-white/10 mb-6">
            <Image src={animeAtual.coverUrl} alt={animeAtual.title} fill className="object-cover" />
          </div>

          <h2 className="text-white font-black text-2xl line-clamp-2 leading-tight drop-shadow-lg mb-3 px-4">
            {animeAtual.title}
          </h2>

          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 bg-black/60 backdrop-blur-sm px-4 py-1.5 rounded-full text-yellow-400 font-bold text-sm">
            ⭐ {animeAtual.score}
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* RODAPÉ: Logo, Dica e Botão de Compartilhar */}
        <div className="absolute bottom-6 left-0 right-0 flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-3 z-50">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded-full border-2 border-accent flex items-center justify-center bg-black/50">
              <span className="text-accent font-black text-lg">A</span>
            </div>
            <span className="text-white font-black tracking-wider text-xl">ANIVERSE</span>
          </div>
          
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-white/60 text-[10px] bg-black/40 px-3 py-1 rounded-full">
            <Info size={12} />
            Grave a tela do seu celular para postar!
          </div>

          {/* BOTÃO DE COMPARTILHAR */}
          <button
            onClick={handleShare}
            className="flex items-center gap-2 bg-white/10 hover:bg-white/20 text-white px-4 py-2 rounded-full text-sm font-medium backdrop-blur-sm transition-colors cursor-pointer mt-2"
          >
            <Share2 size={16} />
            Compartilhar o AniVerse
          </button>
        </div>

      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

===== C:\anime-site\lib\date-utils.ts =====
// lib/date-utils.ts
import { AiringSchedule, AnimeCardData } from "./aniliist/types";
import { mapAnimeToCard } from "./aniliist/mappers";

// Converte o timestamp da AniList para o dia da semana no Brasil
export function getBrazilianWeekday(timestampInSeconds: number): string {
  const date = new Date(timestampInSeconds * 1000);
  
  const formatter = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('pt-BR', {
    timeZone: 'America/Sao_Paulo',
    weekday: 'long',
  });

  const weekday = formatter.format(date);
  // Capitaliza a primeira letra (ex: "segunda-feira" -> "Segunda-feira")
  return weekday.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + weekday.slice(1);
}

// Formata a hora de exibição no Brasil (ex: "21:30")
export function formatBrazilianTime(timestampInSeconds: number): string {
  const date = new Date(timestampInSeconds * 1000);
  
  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat('pt-BR', {
    timeZone: 'America/Sao_Paulo',
    hour: '2-digit',
    minute: '2-digit',
  }).format(date);
}

// Agrupa uma lista de animes por dia da semana no Brasil
export function groupAnimesByWeekday(animes: AiringSchedule[]) {
  const grouped: Record<string, AiringSchedule[]> = {};

  animes.forEach((anime) => {
    const weekday = getBrazilianWeekday(anime.airingAt);

    if (!grouped[weekday]) {
      grouped[weekday] = [];
    }
    
    grouped[weekday].push(anime);
  });

  return grouped;
}

// Interface que junta os dados do card com o horário de exibição
export interface AiringAnimeWithTime extends AnimeCardData {
  airingAt: number;
  formattedTime: string;
  episode: number;
}

export function getAnimesByWeekday(
  scheduleList: AiringSchedule[], 
  targetWeekday: string
): AiringAnimeWithTime[] {
  
  // 1. Filtra apenas os animes que caem no dia da semana escolhido
  const filteredAnimes = scheduleList.filter((anime) => {
    const weekday = getBrazilianWeekday(anime.airingAt);
    return weekday.toLowerCase() === targetWeekday.toLowerCase();
  });

  // 2. Mapeia (normaliza) os dados e adiciona o horário formatado
  const normalizedList = filteredAnimes.map((anime) => {
    const cardData = mapAnimeToCard(anime.media);
    
    return {
      ...cardData,
      airingAt: anime.airingAt,
      formattedTime: formatBrazilianTime(anime.airingAt),
      episode: anime.episode,
    };
  });

  // 3. Ordena do mais cedo para o mais tarde no dia
  return normalizedList.sort((a, b) => a.airingAt - b.airingAt);
}

===== C:\anime-site\lib\disponibilidade.ts =====
// lib/disponibilidade.ts
//['crunchyroll','prime','disney','netflix','max']
export const disponibilidade: Record<number, string[]> = {
  // Exemplo: ID 207191 (Você tem certeza que está nesses 4?)
  // A linha precisa ficar assim:

===== C:\anime-site\lib\aniliist\animeService.ts =====
import { getAnimeById } from './getAnimeById';

// Função para buscar na Jikan (Plano B)
async function getAnimeFromJikan(idMal: number) {
  try {
    const res = await fetch(`https://api.jikan.moe/v4/anime/${idMal}`, {
      next: { revalidate: 86400 }
    });
    if (!res.ok) return null;
    
    const json = await res.json();
    const data = json.data;

    // A Jikan tem um formato diferente, então a gente converte para o formato do AniList
    return {
      id: idMal,
      title: { english: data.title_english, romaji: data.title, native: data.title_japanese },
      description: data.synopsis,
      coverImage: { extraLarge: data.images.jpg.large_image_url },
      bannerImage: null, // A Jikan não tem banner
      averageScore: data.score ? data.score * 10 : null,
      format: data.type,
      status: data.status,
      episodes: data.episodes,
      seasonYear: data.year,
      genres: data.genres.map((g: any) => g.name),
      studios: { nodes: data.studios.map((s: any) => ({ name: s.name })) }
    };
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

// Função principal que o site vai chamar
export async function getAnimeSafe(id: string) {
  // 1. Tenta buscar no AniList
  const animeAnilist = await getAnimeById(id);
  
  // 2. Se o AniList funcionou, retorna ele
  if (animeAnilist) {
    return animeAnilist;
  }

  // 3. Se o AniList falhou (API off ou vazio), tenta o Plano B (Jikan)
  console.log("AniList falhou, tentando Jikan (Plano B) para o ID:", id);
  
  // Precisamos do idMal. Como o AniList caiu, não temos o idMal direto.
  // Para simplificar no MVP: vamos pegar o ID do AniList e subtrair 100000 (regra de ouro aproximada)
  // Se der certo, ótimo. Se não, o site mostra a tela de "indisponível".
  const idMalAproximado = parseInt(id) - 100000;
  
  if (idMalAproximado > 0) {
    const animeJikan = await getAnimeFromJikan(idMalAproximado);
    if (animeJikan) return animeJikan;
  }

  return null;
}

===== C:\anime-site\lib\aniliist\client.ts =====
// lib/aniliist/client.ts
import { connection } from 'next/server';
import { SEARCH_ANIME_QUERY, AIRING_SCHEDULE_QUERY, ANIME_DETAILS_QUERY } from './queries';
import { 
  AniListResponse, 
  PageResponse, 
  AiringScheduleResponse, 
  AnimeDetailsResponse, 
  AnimeDetails 
} from './types';

// 1. Função genérica de fetch
export async function anilistFetch<T>(
  query: string, 
  variables?: Record<string, any>,
  revalidateTime: number = 3600 
): Promise<T | null> {
  try {
    const response = await fetch('https://graphql.anilist.co', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: {
        'Content-Type': 'application/json',
        'Accept': 'application/json',
      },
      body: JSON.stringify({ query, variables }),
      next: { revalidate: revalidateTime }
    });

    if (!response.ok) {
      throw new Error(`Erro na API AniList: ${response.status}`);
    }

    const data = await response.json();
    return data.data as T;
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Falha no fetch da AniList:", error);
    return null;
  }
}

// 2. Busca por título: Sem cache (0)
export async function searchAnime(search: string, page: number = 1): Promise<PageResponse | null> {
  const data = await anilistFetch<AniListResponse>(SEARCH_ANIME_QUERY, {
    search: search,
    page: page,
    perPage: 10
  }, 0);

  return data ? data.Page : null;
}

// 3. Lançamentos do dia: Cache de 1 hora + Uso do connection() para evitar erro de data
export async function getAiringSchedule(): Promise<AiringScheduleResponse['Page'] | null> {
  await connection(); // <--- Avisa o Next.js que esta função precisa ser dinâmica

  const now = new Date();
  const startOfDay = Math.floor(now.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0) / 1000);
  const endOfDay = Math.floor(now.setHours(23, 59, 59, 999) / 1000);
  
  const data = await anilistFetch<AiringScheduleResponse>(AIRING_SCHEDULE_QUERY, {
    page: 1,
    perPage: 20,
    airingAtGreater: startOfDay,
    airingAtLesser: endOfDay,
  }, 3600);

  return data ? data.Page : null;
}

// 4. Detalhes do Anime: Cache de 24 horas
export async function getAnimeDetails(id: number): Promise<AnimeDetails | null> {
  const data = await anilistFetch<AnimeDetailsResponse>(ANIME_DETAILS_QUERY, { id }, 86400);
  return data ? data.Media : null;
}

===== C:\anime-site\lib\aniliist\getAnimeById.ts =====
export async function getAnimeById(id: string) {
  const query = `
    query ($id: Int) {
      Media(id: $id, type: ANIME) {
        id
        title {
          romaji
          english
          native
        }
        description
        coverImage {
          extraLarge
        }
        bannerImage
        averageScore
        format
        status
        episodes
        seasonYear
        genres
        studios(isMain: true) {
          nodes {
            name
          }
        }
      }
    }
  `;

  const response = await fetch('https://graphql.anilist.co', {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'Accept': 'application/json' },
    body: JSON.stringify({ query, variables: { id: parseInt(id) } }),
    next: { revalidate: 86400 }
  });

  const json = await response.json();

  // Se o AniList bloquear por excesso de requisições ou der erro:
if (json.errors) {
  console.error("ERRO DO ANILIST:", json.errors[0].message);
  throw new Error("AniList indisponível"); // <-- ISSO É O QUE FAZ O PAGE.TSX CAIR NO CATCH
}

  return json.data?.Media;
}

===== C:\anime-site\lib\aniliist\mappers.ts =====
// lib/aniliist/mappers.ts
import { AniListAnime, AnimeCardData } from './types';

// Transforma um anime vindo da AniList no formato interno do nosso site
export function mapAnimeToCard(anime: AniListAnime): AnimeCardData {
  // Lógica inteligente para escolher o melhor título (Inglês, senão Romaji)
  const bestTitle = anime.title.english || anime.title.romaji;

  return {
    id: anime.id,
    title: bestTitle,
    coverUrl: anime.coverImage.large,
    score: anime.averageScore ? anime.averageScore / 10 : 0, // A AniList usa nota de 0-100, vamos converter para 0-10
  };
}

// Bônus: Função para transformar uma lista inteira de uma vez
export function mapAnimeListToCards(animes: AniListAnime[]): AnimeCardData[] {
  return animes.map(mapAnimeToCard);
}

===== C:\anime-site\lib\aniliist\queries.ts =====
export const SEARCH_ANIME_QUERY = `
  query SearchAnime($search: String!, $page: Int, $perPage: Int) {
    Page(page: $page, perPage: $perPage) {
      pageInfo {
        total
        currentPage
        lastPage
        hasNextPage
      }
      media(search: $search, type: ANIME, sort: POPULARITY_DESC) {
        id
        title {
          romaji
          english
          native
        }
        coverImage {
          large
        }
        format
        status
        episodes
        averageScore
      }
    }
  }
`;
export const AIRING_SCHEDULE_QUERY = `
  query AiringSchedule($page: Int, $perPage: Int, $airingAtGreater: Int, $airingAtLesser: Int) {
    Page(page: $page, perPage: $perPage) {
      pageInfo {
        total
        currentPage
        hasNextPage
      }
      airingSchedules(airingAt_greater: $airingAtGreater, airingAt_lesser: $airingAtLesser, sort: TIME) {
        id
        airingAt
        episode
        media {
          id
          title {
            romaji
            english
          }
          coverImage {
            large
          }
        }
      }
    }
  }
`;
export const ANIME_DETAILS_QUERY = `
  query AnimeDetails($id: Int) {
    Media(id: $id, type: ANIME) {
      id
      title {
        romaji
        english
        native
      }
      description
      coverImage {
        extraLarge
        large
      }
      bannerImage
      format
      status
      episodes
      duration
      season
      seasonYear
      averageScore
      genres
      studios(isMain: true) {
        nodes {
          name
        }
      }
      trailer {
        id
        site
      }
    }
  }
`;

===== C:\anime-site\lib\aniliist\types.ts =====
// lib/aniliist/types.ts

export interface AniListAnime {
  id: number;
  title: {
    romaji: string;
    english: string | null;
    native: string;
  };
  coverImage: {
    large: string;
  };
  format: string;
  status: string;
  episodes: number | null;
  averageScore: number | null;
}

export interface PageInfo {
  total: number;
  currentPage: number;
  lastPage: number;
  hasNextPage: boolean;
}

export interface PageResponse {
  pageInfo: PageInfo;
  media: AniListAnime[];
}

export interface AniListResponse {
  Page: PageResponse;
}

// Tipos para os Lançamentos da Semana
export interface AiringSchedule {
  id: number;
  airingAt: number;
  episode: number;
  media: AniListAnime; 
}

export interface AiringScheduleResponse {
  Page: {
    pageInfo: PageInfo;
    airingSchedules: AiringSchedule[];
  };
}

// Tipos para os Detalhes do Anime
export interface AnimeDetails {
  id: number;
  title: {
    romaji: string;
    english: string | null;
    native: string;
  };
  description: string;
  coverImage: {
    extraLarge: string;
    large: string;
  };
  bannerImage: string | null;
  format: string;
  status: string;
  episodes: number | null;
  duration: number | null;
  season: string | null;
  seasonYear: number | null;
  averageScore: number | null;
  genres: string[];
  studios: {
    nodes: { name: string }[];
  };
  trailer: {
    id: string;
    site: string;
  } | null;
}

export interface AnimeDetailsResponse {
  Media: AnimeDetails;
}

// O formato PADRÃO que o SEU SITE vai usar (independente da API)
export interface AnimeCardData {
  id: number;
  title: string;
  coverUrl: string;
  score: number;
}

===== C:\anime-site\lib\supabase\server.ts =====
import { createServerClient } from '@supabase/ssr';
import { cookies } from 'next/headers';

export async function createClient() {
  const cookieStore = await cookies();

  return createServerClient(
    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL!,
    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY!,
    {
      cookies: {
        getAll() {
          return cookieStore.getAll();
        },
        setAll(cookiesToSet) {
          try {
            cookiesToSet.forEach(({ name, value, options }) =>
              cookieStore.set(name, value, options)
            );
          } catch {
            // O setAll pode falhar se for chamado de um Server Component
          }
        },
      },
    }
  );
}

===== C:\anime-site\lib\traduzirTitulo\tradutor.ts =====
async function traduzirTitulo(texto: string) {
  const API_KEY = process.env.GROQ_API_KEY; 
  if (!API_KEY) return texto; // Se não tiver chave, retorna o original

  const prompt = `
    Avalie o seguinte título de anime: "${texto}".
    Regras:
    1. Se o título já for universalmente conhecido em inglês ou for um nome próprio (ex: "Black Clover", "Dragon Ball", "Naruto"), retorne o título EXATAMENTE como está.
    2. Se o título for em japonês (romaji) ou tiver uma tradução clara e comum em português (ex: "Shingeki no Kyojin" -> "Ataque dos Titãs"), retorne APENAS a tradução em português.
    3. Não adicione explicações, apenas o título final.
  `;

  try {
    const res = await fetch('https://api.groq.com/openai/v1/chat/completions', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'Authorization': `Bearer ${API_KEY}` },
      body: JSON.stringify({ 
        model: "openai/gpt-oss-20b", 
        messages: [
          { role: "system", content: "Você é um especialista em animes e tradução de títulos." },
          { role: "user", content: prompt }
        ], 
        temperature: 0.1 // Baixa temperatura para ser mais direto
      })
    });
    
    if (!res.ok) throw new Error('Falha na API');
    const data = await res.json();
    return data.choices?.[0]?.message?.content?.trim() || texto;
  } catch (error) {
    return texto; // Se der erro, retorna o original
  }
}
